===== src/components/Sidebar.vue =====
<script setup>
import { LogOut } from 'lucide-vue-next'
import { useRouter } from 'vue-router'
import { useOperatorStore } from '../stores/operatorStore.js'

const props = defineProps({ groups: Array, activePage: String })
const emit = defineEmits(['select-page', 'close'])

const router = useRouter()
const operatorStore = useOperatorStore()

const handleItemClick = (item) => {
  emit('select-page', item)
  if (item.to) {
    router.push(item.to)
  }
}

const handleLogout = () => {
  if (confirm('Akhiri shift & keluar dari akun operator ini?')) {
    operatorStore.logout()
    router.push('/login')
  }
}
</script>

<template>
<div class="h-full bg-zinc-900 text-white flex flex-col overflow-hidden">
  <!-- Header App & Operator Active -->
  <div class="p-6 md:p-7 pb-4 shrink-0 space-y-3 md:space-y-4">
    <div class="flex justify-between items-center">
      <span class="font-black text-lg md:text-xl tracking-tight">SINAR PAGI</span>
    </div>

    <!-- Info Operator Aktif & Tombol Switch Shift -->
    <div class="bg-zinc-800/80 p-3 md:p-4 rounded-2xl border border-zinc-700/50 flex items-center justify-between">
      <div class="min-w-0 flex-1 pr-2">
        <div class="text-[9px] md:text-[10px] font-black text-zinc-400 uppercase tracking-wider">Shift Aktif</div>
        <div class="font-bold text-xs md:text-sm text-white truncate">{{ operatorStore.operatorName }}</div>
      </div>
      <button 
        @click="handleLogout" 
        title="Ganti Operator / Keluar Shift"
        class="px-2.5 py-1.5 md:px-3 md:py-2 bg-red-500/20 hover:bg-red-500/30 text-red-400 rounded-xl font-bold text-[10px] md:text-xs flex items-center gap-1 md:gap-1.5 active:scale-95 transition shrink-0"
      >
        <LogOut class="w-3 h-3 md:w-4 md:h-4" /> Shift
      </button>
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- Area Menu Scroll -->
  <div class="flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-6 md:px-7 py-2 space-y-6 md:space-y-7">
    <div v-for="group in groups" :key="group.title">
      <div class="text-[10px] md:text-xs font-black tracking-widest text-zinc-500 uppercase mb-3">
        {{ group.title }}
      </div>
      <ul class="flex flex-col gap-1 md:gap-1.5">
        <li 
          v-for="m in group.items" 
          :key="m.name" 
          @click="handleItemClick(m)"
          :class="[
            'px-4 py-3 md:px-5 md:py-3.5 rounded-2xl text-sm md:text-base font-bold flex items-center gap-3 md:gap-3.5 cursor-pointer transition active:scale-95', 
            activePage === m.name ? 'bg-white text-zinc-900 shadow-md' : 'text-zinc-400 hover:bg-zinc-800 hover:text-white'
          ]"
        >
          <component :is="m.icon" class="w-4 h-4 md:w-5 md:h-5 shrink-0"/> 
          <span>{{ m.name }}</span>
        </li>
      </ul>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="shrink-0 p-6 md:p-7 pt-4 border-t border-zinc-800 text-[10px] md:text-xs text-zinc-500 font-bold">
    Sinar Pagi v1.2 FIXED
  </div>
</div>
</template>

===== src/components/ReceiptModal.vue =====
<!-- src/components/ReceiptModal.vue -->
<template>
  <div class="fixed inset-0 z-[100] bg-black/50 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center p-0 sm:p-4">
    <div class="w-full max-w-sm bg-white rounded-t-[2rem] sm:rounded-[2rem] p-6 shadow-2xl flex flex-col gap-4 animate-in slide-in-from-bottom duration-200">
      
      <!-- Header Dialog -->
      <div class="flex justify-between items-center border-b border-zinc-100 pb-3">
        <div>
          <h2 class="font-black text-base text-zinc-900">Transaksi Selesai</h2>
          <p class="text-xs text-zinc-400">ID: #{{ transaction?.id?.slice(0, 8) }}</p>
        </div>
        <button @click="emit('close')" class="p-2 bg-zinc-100 rounded-xl">
          <X class="w-4 h-4" />
        </button>
      </div>

      <!-- Ringkasan Struk Nota (Thermal Look) -->
      <div class="bg-zinc-50 border border-zinc-200/60 p-4 rounded-2xl space-y-2 text-xs font-mono">
        <div class="text-center font-bold text-sm text-zinc-800">SINAR PAGI</div>
        
        <!-- Info Kasir & Member -->
        <div class="flex justify-between text-[10px] text-zinc-500 border-b border-zinc-200/60 pb-1.5">
          <span>Kasir: {{ transaction?.operatorName || 'Admin' }}</span>
          <span>Member: {{ transaction?.memberName || 'Umum' }}</span>
        </div>

        <div class="border-b border-dashed border-zinc-300 pb-2 text-[10px] text-zinc-500 text-center">
          {{ formatDate(transaction?.date) }}
        </div>

        <!-- Daftar Item Struk Thermal -->
        <div class="max-h-48 overflow-y-auto space-y-2 my-2 pr-1 border-b border-dashed border-zinc-300 pb-2">
          <div v-for="item in transaction?.items" :key="item.id || item.productId" class="text-[11px] space-y-0.5">
            <div class="font-bold text-zinc-800 break-words leading-tight">
              {{ item.productName || item.name }}
            </div>
            <div class="flex justify-between text-zinc-500 text-[10px]">
              <span>{{ item.qty }}x {{ formatRibuan(item.price) }}</span>
              <span class="font-bold text-zinc-800">Rp {{ formatRibuan(item.subtotal || (item.price * item.qty)) }}</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <!-- Ringkasan Rincian Pembayaran -->
        <div class="pt-1 space-y-1">
          <div class="flex justify-between font-black text-sm text-zinc-900">
            <span>TOTAL</span>
            <span>Rp {{ formatRibuan(transaction?.total) }}</span>
          </div>
          <div class="flex justify-between text-[11px] text-zinc-600">
            <span>METODE BAYAR</span>
            <span class="font-bold uppercase">{{ transaction?.paymentMethod }}</span>
          </div>
          <div v-if="transaction?.paymentMethod === 'CASH'" class="flex justify-between text-[11px] text-zinc-600">
            <span>BAYAR</span>
            <span>Rp {{ formatRibuan(transaction?.amountPaid || transaction?.total) }}</span>
          </div>
          <div v-if="transaction?.paymentMethod === 'CASH'" class="flex justify-between text-[11px] text-emerald-600 font-bold">
            <span>KEMBALI</span>
            <span>Rp {{ formatRibuan(Math.max(0, (transaction?.amountPaid || transaction?.total) - transaction?.total)) }}</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <!-- Tombol Aksi Cetak & Unduh -->
      <div class="space-y-2">
        <button 
          @click="handlePrintBluetooth" 
          :disabled="printing"
          class="w-full py-3.5 bg-zinc-900 hover:bg-zinc-800 disabled:bg-zinc-400 text-white rounded-2xl font-bold text-xs flex items-center justify-center gap-2 transition active:scale-95 shadow-md"
        >
          <Printer class="w-4 h-4" />
          <span>{{ printing ? 'Menghubungkan Printer...' : 'Cetak Thermal (Bluetooth)' }}</span>
        </button>

        <button 
          @click="handleDownloadPdf" 
          class="w-full py-3.5 bg-blue-50 hover:bg-blue-100 text-blue-700 border border-blue-200 rounded-2xl font-bold text-xs flex items-center justify-center gap-2 transition active:scale-95"
        >
          <FileText class="w-4 h-4" />
          <span>Unduh Nota (PDF)</span>
        </button>

        <button 
          @click="emit('close')" 
          class="w-full py-3 bg-zinc-100 text-zinc-700 rounded-2xl font-bold text-xs hover:bg-zinc-200 transition"
        >
          Transaksi Baru
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
</template>

<script setup>
import { ref } from 'vue'
import { X, Printer, FileText } from 'lucide-vue-next'
import { receiptService } from '../services/receiptService.js'
import { formatRibuan } from '../utils/formatters/currency.js'
import { toast } from 'vue-sonner'
import dayjs from 'dayjs'

const props = defineProps({
  transaction: {
    type: Object,
    required: true
  }
})

const emit = defineEmits(['close'])

const printing = ref(false)

const formatDate = (dateStr) => dateStr ? dayjs(dateStr).format('DD/MM/YYYY • HH:mm') : '-'

const handlePrintBluetooth = async () => {
  printing.value = true
  try {
    await receiptService.printThermal(props.transaction)
    toast.success('Struk berhasil dicetak!')
    emit('close')
  } catch (err) {
    toast.error(`Gagal mencetak: ${err.message}`)
  } finally {
    printing.value = false
  }
}

const handleDownloadPdf = async () => {
  try {
    await receiptService.downloadPdf(props.transaction)
    toast.success('Nota PDF berhasil diunduh!')
    emit('close')
  } catch (err) {
    toast.error(`Gagal mengunduh PDF: ${err.message}`)
  }
}
</script>

      >
        UMUM

        v-for="cat in categories" 
        :key="cat.id"

        @click="category = cat.name.toUpperCase(); showCategoryModal = false"
        :class="['w-full p-3 rounded-xl font-bold text-xs text-left uppercase transition', category === cat.name.toUpperCase() ? 'bg-zinc-900 text-white' : 'bg-zinc-50 hover:bg-zinc-100 text-zinc-800']"
      >
        {{ cat.name }}
      </button>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>

      <h3 class="font-black text-base text-zinc-900">Pilih Satuan Utama</h3>

    </div>

    <div class="space-y-1.5 overflow-auto flex-1 pr-1">
      <button 
        v-for="u in units" 
        :key="u.id" 
        type="button"
        @click="unit = u.name.toUpperCase(); showUnitModal = false"
        :class="['w-full p-3 rounded-xl font-bold text-xs text-left uppercase transition', unit === u.name.toUpperCase() ? 'bg-zinc-900 text-white' : 'bg-zinc-50 hover:bg-zinc-100 text-zinc-800']"
      >
        {{ u.name }}
      </button>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>

<!-- MODAL PILIH SATUAN GROSIR -->
<div v-if="showPackUnitModal" class="fixed inset-0 z-[90] bg-black/40 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center p-4">
  <div class="w-full max-w-sm bg-white rounded-[2rem] p-5 shadow-xl animate-in zoom-in-95 fade-in duration-200 max-h-[80vh] flex flex-col">
    <div class="flex justify-between items-center mb-3">
      <h3 class="font-black text-base text-zinc-900">Pilih Satuan Grosir</h3>

    </div>

      <button 
        type="button"

      >
        -- KOSONGKAN --
      </button>

      <button 
        v-for="u in units" 
        :key="u.id" 
        type="button"
        @click="purchasePackName = u.name.toUpperCase(); showPackUnitModal = false"
        :class="['w-full p-3 rounded-xl font-bold text-xs text-left uppercase transition', purchasePackName === u.name.toUpperCase() ? 'bg-zinc-900 text-white' : 'bg-zinc-50 hover:bg-zinc-100 text-zinc-800']"
      >
        {{ u.name }}
      </button>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>
</template>

<script setup>
import { ref, computed, watch, onMounted, onActivated } from 'vue'
import { ScanLine, ChevronDown, X, Globe, EyeOff } from 'lucide-vue-next'
import { useRouter } from 'vue-router'
import { useProductForm } from '../../composables/useProductForm.js'
import { useProductScanner } from '../../composables/useProductScanner.js'
import { calcModalDariPack, calcMargin } from '../../utils/calculator/product.js'
import { formatRp, toNumber } from '../../utils/formatters/currency.js'
import { useProductsStore } from '../../stores/productStore.js'
import { productRepo } from '../../db/repositories/productRepository.js'
import { toast } from 'vue-sonner'

const props = defineProps({
  initialData: {
    type: Object,
    default: null
  }
})

const emit = defineEmits(['success'])

const showCategoryModal = ref(false)
const showUnitModal = ref(false)
const showPackUnitModal = ref(false)

const { 
  name, code, category, unit, purchasePackName, packPrice, packQty, 
  price_modal, price_sell, qty, minStock, categories, units, loadCategories, loadUnits,
  handleSubmit, errors, isSubmitting 
} = useProductForm()

const { start } = useProductScanner()
const router = useRouter()
const productStore = useProductsStore()

// ===== GAMBAR - BARU =====
const imageFile = ref(null)
const imagePreview = ref(null)
const isPublished = ref(false)

function onImageChange(e) {
  const file = e.target.files[0]
  if (!file) return
  if (file.size > 5 * 1024 * 1024) {
    toast.error('Gambar max 5MB')
    return
  }
  compressImage(file).then(compressed => {
    imageFile.value = compressed
    imagePreview.value = URL.createObjectURL(compressed)
  })
}
function removeImage() {
  imageFile.value = null
  imagePreview.value = null
  isPublished.value = false
}
function compressImage(file) {
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    const reader = new FileReader()
    reader.onload = (ev) => {
      const img = new Image()
      img.onload = () => {
        const canvas = document.createElement('canvas')
        const maxW = 800
        let w = img.width, h = img.height
        if (w > maxW) { h = h * maxW / w; w = maxW }
        canvas.width = w; canvas.height = h
        const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d')
        ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, w, h)
        canvas.toBlob((blob) => resolve(blob), 'image/jpeg', 0.7)
      }
      img.src = ev.target.result
    }
    reader.readAsDataURL(file)
  })
}


const autoModal = computed(() => calcModalDariPack(packPrice.value, packQty.value))
const marginPersen = computed(() => calcMargin(price_sell.value, price_modal.value))

// --- LOGIKA BLOKIR SCANNER DI LUAR INPUT BARCODE ---
let lastKeyTime = 0
let fastKeyBurstCount = 0

const handleFormKeydown = (e) => {
  const isBarcodeInput = e.target && e.target.id === 'product-barcode-input'
  if (isBarcodeInput) return

  const now = Date.now()
  const timeDiff = now - lastKeyTime
  lastKeyTime = now

  if (timeDiff < 40) {
    fastKeyBurstCount++
  } else {
    fastKeyBurstCount = 0
  }

  if (fastKeyBurstCount >= 1) {
    e.preventDefault()
    e.stopPropagation()

    if (fastKeyBurstCount === 1 && e.target && 'value' in e.target) {
      const val = e.target.value
      if (val.length > 0) {
        e.target.value = val.slice(0, -1)
        e.target.dispatchEvent(new Event('input'))
      }
    }
  }
}

const resetFormFields = () => {
  name.value = ''
  code.value = ''
  category.value = 'UMUM'
  unit.value = ''
  purchasePackName.value = ''
  packPrice.value = 0
  packQty.value = 0
  price_modal.value = 0
  price_sell.value = 0
  qty.value = 0
  minStock.value = 2
  imageFile.value = null
  imagePreview.value = null
  isPublished.value = false
}

const checkExistingBarcode = async (barcodeVal) => {
  if (!barcodeVal) return false
  const cleanCode = String(barcodeVal).toUpperCase().trim()
  if (!cleanCode) return false

  const allProducts = await productRepo.getAll()
  const existingProduct = allProducts.find(
    p => String(p.code || '').toUpperCase().trim() === cleanCode
  )

  if (existingProduct && existingProduct.id !== props.initialData?.id) {
    const productName = existingProduct.name || 'Tanpa Nama'
    toast.error(`Barcode "${cleanCode}" sudah digunakan oleh: ${productName}`)
    code.value = ''
    return true
  }
  return false
}

const handleBarcodeEnter = async () => {
  await checkExistingBarcode(code.value)
}

const handleBarcodeBlur = async () => {
  await checkExistingBarcode(code.value)
}

const handleCameraScan = () => {
  start(async (scannedCode) => {
    code.value = scannedCode
    await checkExistingBarcode(scannedCode)
  })
}

const initFormData = async () => {
  await loadCategories()
  await loadUnits()

  if (props.initialData) {
    name.value = (props.initialData.name || '').toUpperCase()
    code.value = (props.initialData.code || '').toUpperCase()
    category.value = (props.initialData.category || 'UMUM').toUpperCase()
    unit.value = (props.initialData.unit || '').toUpperCase()
    purchasePackName.value = (props.initialData.purchasePackName || '').toUpperCase()
    packPrice.value = props.initialData.packPrice || 0
    packQty.value = props.initialData.packQty || 0
    price_modal.value = props.initialData.price_modal || 0
    price_sell.value = props.initialData.price_sell || 0
    qty.value = props.initialData.qty || 0
    minStock.value = props.initialData.minStock || 2
    isPublished.value = props.initialData.isPublished || false
    if (props.initialData.imageUrl) imagePreview.value = props.initialData.imageUrl
    else if (props.initialData.imageLocal) imagePreview.value = URL.createObjectURL(props.initialData.imageLocal)
  } else {
    resetFormFields()
  }
}

onMounted(initFormData)

onActivated(() => {
  if (!props.initialData) {
    resetFormFields()
  }
})

  packPrice.value = raw
  e.target.value = formatRp(raw)
}

  price_modal.value = raw
  e.target.value = formatRp(raw)
}
const onJualInput = (e) => {

  price_sell.value = raw
  e.target.value = formatRp(raw)
}

watch(autoModal, (val) => { if (val > 0) price_modal.value = val })

const onSubmit = handleSubmit(async (vals) => {
  const payload = {
    name: (vals.name || '').toUpperCase().trim(),
    code: (vals.code || '').toUpperCase().trim(),
    category: (category.value || 'UMUM').toUpperCase().trim(),
    unit: (unit.value || '').toUpperCase().trim(),
    purchasePackName: (purchasePackName.value || '').toUpperCase().trim(),
    packPrice: toNumber(packPrice.value),
    packQty: toNumber(packQty.value),
    price_modal: toNumber(price_modal.value),
    price_sell: toNumber(price_sell.value),
    qty: toNumber(qty.value),
    minStock: toNumber(minStock.value) || 2,
    isPublished: isPublished.value
  }

  const isDuplicate = await checkExistingBarcode(payload.code)
  if (isDuplicate) return

  if (props.initialData?.id) {
    await productStore.update(props.initialData.id, payload, imageFile.value)
    toast.success('Barang berhasil diperbarui!')
    emit('success')
  } else {
    await productStore.add(payload, imageFile.value)
    toast.success('Barang berhasil ditambahkan!')
    resetFormFields()
    router.push('/products')
  }
})
</script>

===== src/components/Numpad.vue =====
<script setup>
const props = defineProps({
  modelValue: { type: String, default: '' },
  maxLength: { type: Number, default: 6 }
})

const emit = defineEmits(['update:modelValue', 'submit', 'close'])

function onPress(num) {
  if (props.modelValue.length < props.maxLength) {
    emit('update:modelValue', props.modelValue + num)
  }
}
function onDelete() {
  emit('update:modelValue', props.modelValue.slice(0, -1))
}
function onClear() {
  emit('update:modelValue', '')
}
</script>

<template>
  <div class="mt-4 animate-in slide-in-from-bottom-2 duration-200">
    <div class="grid grid-cols-3 gap-3">
      <button v-for="n in 9" :key="n" type="button" @click="onPress(String(n))" class="h-[56px] bg-zinc-800 hover:bg-zinc-700 text-white rounded-2xl text-xl font-black active:scale-95 transition">
        {{ n }}
      </button>
      <button type="button" @click="onClear" class="h-[56px] bg-zinc-800 text-zinc-400 rounded-2xl text-[11px] font-black tracking-widest active:scale-95">CLEAR</button>
      <button type="button" @click="onPress('0')" class="h-[56px] bg-zinc-800 text-white rounded-2xl text-xl font-black active:scale-95">0</button>
      <button type="button" @click="onDelete" class="h-[56px] bg-zinc-800 text-amber-400 rounded-2xl font-bold active:scale-95">⌫</button>
    </div>
    <button type="button" @click="emit('submit')" class="w-full mt-3 h-12 bg-amber-500 hover:bg-amber-400 text-black rounded-2xl font-black text-xs tracking-widest active:scale-95 transition">MASUK SHIFT</button>
    <button type="button" @click="emit('close')" class="w-full mt-2 text-[10px] font-bold text-zinc-500">Tutup Numpad</button>
  </div>
</template>
===== src/stores/operatorStore.js =====
import { defineStore } from 'pinia'
import { operatorRepo } from '../db/repositories/operatorRepository.js'

export const useOperatorStore = defineStore('operator', {
  state: () => ({
    activeOperator: null,
    operators: []
  }),

  getters: {
    isLoggedIn: (state) => !!state.activeOperator,
    operatorName: (state) => state.activeOperator?.name || 'Kasir',
    operatorRole: (state) => state.activeOperator?.role || 'Kasir'
  },

  actions: {
    async loadOperators() {
      this.operators = await operatorRepo.getAll()
      return this.operators
    },

    async login(operatorId, pinInput) {
      if (this.operators.length === 0) {
        await this.loadOperators()
      }
      
      const target = this.operators.find(o => o.id === operatorId)

      if (!target) {
        throw new Error('Operator tidak ditemukan.')
      }

      if (target.pin && target.pin !== pinInput) {
        throw new Error('PIN yang Anda masukkan salah.')
      }

      this.activeOperator = {
        id: target.id,
        name: target.name,
        role: target.role
      }

      return this.activeOperator
    },

    logout() {
      this.activeOperator = null
    }
  },

  persist: true
})

===== src/stores/productStore.js =====
// src/stores/productStore.js - FINAL v3
import { defineStore } from 'pinia'
import { productRepo } from '../db/repositories/productRepository.js'

export const useProductsStore = defineStore('products', {
  state: () => ({ 
    list: [], 
    searchQuery: '',
    loading: false 
  }),
  getters: {
    filteredProducts: (state) => {
      const q = state.searchQuery.toLowerCase().trim()
      if (!q) return state.list
      return state.list
        .filter(p => 
          p.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) || 
          (p.code || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
          (p.category || '').toLowerCase().includes(q)
        )
        .sort((a, b) => {
          const nameA = a.name.toLowerCase()
          const nameB = b.name.toLowerCase()
          const idxA = nameA.indexOf(q)
          const idxB = nameB.indexOf(q)
          if (idxA !== -1 && idxB !== -1) {
            if (idxA !== idxB) return idxA - idxB
            return nameA.localeCompare(nameB)
          }
          if (idxA !== -1) return -1
          if (idxB !== -1) return 1
          return 0
        })
    }
  },
  actions: {
    async fetch() { 
      this.loading = true
      this.list = await productRepo.getAll()
      this.loading = false 
    },
    async add(payload, imageFile = null) { 
      await productRepo.create(payload, imageFile)
      await this.fetch() 
    },
    async update(id, payload, imageFile = null) {
      await productRepo.update(id, payload, imageFile)
      await this.fetch()
    },
    async remove(id) {
      await productRepo.delete(id) // soft delete, biar 498 ke-sync ke cloud
      await this.fetch()
    }
  },
  persist: true
})
===== src/stores/kasirStore.js =====
// src/stores/kasirStore.js
import { defineStore } from 'pinia'

export const useKasirStore = defineStore('kasir', {
  state: () => ({
    cart: [],
    selectedMember: null,
    paymentMethod: 'CASH',
    bayarNominal: 0,
    pendingCarts: [],
    editingTransactionId: null // ID transaksi yang sedang diedit
  }),
  actions: {
    // Muat transaksi lama ke keranjang kasir
    loadTransactionForEdit(transaction) {
      this.clearCart()
      this.editingTransactionId = transaction.id
      this.paymentMethod = transaction.paymentMethod || 'CASH'
      
      // Isi keranjang dengan item dari nota lama
      this.cart = transaction.items.map(item => ({
        id: item.productId || item.id,
        cartItemId: item.id || crypto.randomUUID(),
        name: item.productName || item.name,
        price_sell: item.price,
        cartQty: item.qty,
        isServed: !!item.serviceId
      }))
    },

    addToCart(product, options = {}) {
      const { packageInfo = null, isServed = false, serviceFee = 0, customName = '' } = options

      const itemId = packageInfo 
        ? `${product.id}_pkg_${packageInfo.id}`
        : isServed 
          ? `${product.id}_served` 
          : product.id

      const finalName = customName || (isServed ? `Seduh ${product.name}` : product.name)
      const basePrice = packageInfo ? packageInfo.price_sell : product.price_sell
      const finalPrice = basePrice + (isServed ? serviceFee : 0)

      const idx = this.cart.findIndex(c => c.cartItemId === itemId)

      if (idx > -1) {
        this.cart[idx].cartQty++
        const item = this.cart.splice(idx, 1)[0]
        this.cart.unshift(item)
      } else {
        this.cart.unshift({
          ...product,
          cartItemId: itemId,
          name: finalName,
          originalName: product.name,
          price_sell: finalPrice,
          cartQty: 1,
          packageInfo,
          isServed,
          serviceFee
        })
      }
    },
    inc(index) {
      if (this.cart[index]) this.cart[index].cartQty++
    },
    dec(index) {
      if (this.cart[index]) {
        this.cart[index].cartQty--
        if (this.cart[index].cartQty <= 0) {
          this.cart.splice(index, 1)
        }
      }
    },
    clearCart() {
      this.cart = []
      this.selectedMember = null
      this.bayarNominal = 0
      this.paymentMethod = 'CASH'
      this.editingTransactionId = null
    },
    holdCurrentCart(label = '') {
      if (this.cart.length === 0) return
      this.pendingCarts.push({
        id: crypto.randomUUID(),
        createdAt: new Date().toLocaleTimeString('id-ID', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' }),
        label: label || this.selectedMember?.name || `Transaksi #${this.pendingCarts.length + 1}`,
        cart: [...this.cart],
        selectedMember: this.selectedMember,
        paymentMethod: this.paymentMethod
      })
      this.clearCart()
    },
    resumeCart(pendingId) {
      const idx = this.pendingCarts.findIndex(p => p.id === pendingId)
      if (idx > -1) {
        const item = this.pendingCarts.splice(idx, 1)[0]
        this.cart = item.cart
        this.selectedMember = item.selectedMember
        this.paymentMethod = item.paymentMethod || 'CASH'
      }
    },
    removePending(pendingId) {
      this.pendingCarts = this.pendingCarts.filter(p => p.id !== pendingId)
    }
  },
  persist: true
})

===== src/composables/useProductsDigital.js =====
// src/composables/useProductsDigital.js
import { ref, computed } from 'vue'
import { productsDigitalService } from '../services/productsDigitalService.js'
import { memberRepo } from '../db/repositories/memberRepository.js'
import { formatRibuan } from '../utils/formatters/currency.js'
import { toast } from 'vue-sonner'

export function useProductsDigital() {
  const form = ref({
    type: 'topup',
    provider: '',
    nominal: null,
    adminFee: 2000,
    adminPaymentMethod: 'cash'
  })
  const loading = ref(false)

  const members = ref([])
  const selectedMember = ref(null)
  const memberQ = ref('')
  const showMemberPicker = ref(false)

  const loadMembers = async () => {
    members.value = await memberRepo.getAll()
  }

  const filteredMembers = computed(() => {
    const qq = memberQ.value.toLowerCase().trim()
    if (!qq) return members.value.slice(0, 20)
    return members.value.filter(m => m.name.toLowerCase().includes(qq) || (m.phone || '').includes(qq)).slice(0, 20)
  })

  const selectMember = (m) => {
    selectedMember.value = m
    showMemberPicker.value = false
  }

  const estimasiKas = computed(() => {
    const n = Number(form.value.nominal) || 0
    const fee = Number(form.value.adminFee) || 0
    const feeCash = form.value.adminPaymentMethod === 'cash' ? fee : 0
    return form.value.type === 'topup' ? n + feeCash : n - feeCash
  })

  const saveTransaction = async () => {
    if (!form.value.provider?.trim()) {
      toast.error('Pilih atau isi Layanan / Provider terlebih dahulu!')
      return
    }
    if (!form.value.nominal || form.value.nominal <= 0) {
      toast.error('Nominal transaksi harus lebih besar dari 0!')
      return
    }

    loading.value = true
    try {
      await productsDigitalService.create({
        ...form.value,
        memberId: selectedMember.value?.id || null,
        memberName: selectedMember.value?.name || null
      })
      toast.success('Transaksi digital berhasil disimpan!')
      form.value = {
        type: 'topup',
        provider: '',
        nominal: null,
        adminFee: 2000,
        adminPaymentMethod: 'cash'
      }
      selectedMember.value = null
    } catch (e) {
      toast.error(`Gagal menyimpan transaksi: ${e.message}`)
    } finally {
      loading.value = false
    }
  }

  return { 
    form, 
    estimasiKas, 
    loading, 
    members,
    selectedMember,
    memberQ,
    showMemberPicker,
    filteredMembers,
    loadMembers,
    selectMember,
    saveTransaction 
  }
}

===== src/composables/useOperators.js =====
// src/composables/useOperators.js
import { ref, computed } from 'vue'
import { operatorRepo } from '../db/repositories/operatorRepository.js'

export function useOperators() {
  const operators = ref([])
  const searchQuery = ref('')
  const showModal = ref(false)
  const editingId = ref(null)
  const form = ref({ name: '', role: 'Kasir', pin: '' })

  const filteredOperators = computed(() => {
    const q = searchQuery.value.toLowerCase().trim()
    if (!q) return operators.value
    return operators.value.filter(o => 
      o.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) || 
      (o.role || '').toLowerCase().includes(q)
    )
  })

  const loadOperators = async () => {
    operators.value = await operatorRepo.getAll()
  }

  const openModal = (op = null) => {
    if (op) {
      editingId.value = op.id
      form.value = { name: op.name, role: op.role || 'Kasir', pin: op.pin || '' }
    } else {
      editingId.value = null
      form.value = { name: '', role: 'Kasir', pin: '' }
    }
    showModal.value = true
  }

  const saveOperator = async () => {
    if (!form.value.name.trim()) return

    if (editingId.value) {
      await operatorRepo.update(editingId.value, form.value)
    } else {
      await operatorRepo.create(form.value)
    }

    showModal.value = false
    await loadOperators()
    // syncRealtime.pushLocalToCloud() -> Dihapus, ditangani Dexie Hook
  }

  const deleteOperator = async (op) => {
    if (confirm(`Hapus operator / kasir "${op.name}"?`)) {
      await operatorRepo.delete(op.id)
      await loadOperators()
      // syncRealtime.pushLocalToCloud() -> Dihapus, ditangani Dexie Hook
    }
  }

  return {
    operators,
    searchQuery,
    filteredOperators,
    showModal,
    editingId,
    form,
    loadOperators,
    openModal,
    saveOperator,
    deleteOperator
  }
}

===== src/composables/useProductForm.js =====
// src/composables/useProductForm.js
import { ref } from 'vue'
import { useForm, useField } from 'vee-validate'
import { productSchema } from '../schemas/product.schema.js'
import { categoryRepo } from '../db/repositories/categoryRepository.js'
import { unitRepo } from '../db/repositories/unitRepository.js'

function toTypedSchema(zodSchema) {
  return {
    parse(values) {
      const r = zodSchema.safeParse(values)
      if (r.success) return {}
      const errs = {}
      r.error.issues.forEach(i => { errs[i.path.join('.')] = i.message })
      return errs
    }
  }
}

export function useProductForm() {
  const categories = ref([])
  const units = ref([])

  const { handleSubmit, errors, isSubmitting } = useForm({
    validationSchema: toTypedSchema(productSchema),
    initialValues: {
      name: '',
      code: '',
      category: 'Umum',
      unit: '',
      purchasePackName: '',
      packPrice: '',
      packQty: '',
      price_modal: '',
      price_sell: '',
      qty: '',
      minStock: 2
    }
  })

  const { value: name } = useField('name')
  const { value: code } = useField('code')
  const { value: category } = useField('category')
  const { value: unit } = useField('unit')
  const { value: purchasePackName } = useField('purchasePackName')
  const { value: packPrice } = useField('packPrice')
  const { value: packQty } = useField('packQty')
  const { value: price_modal } = useField('price_modal')
  const { value: price_sell } = useField('price_sell')
  const { value: qty } = useField('qty')
  const { value: minStock } = useField('minStock')

  const loadCategories = async () => {
    const data = await categoryRepo.getAll()
    categories.value = data.sort((a, b) => (a.name || '').localeCompare(b.name || ''))
  }

  const loadUnits = async () => {
    const data = await unitRepo.getAll()
    units.value = data.sort((a, b) => (a.name || '').localeCompare(b.name || ''))
  }

  return {
    name,
    code,
    category,
    unit,
    purchasePackName,
    packPrice,
    packQty,
    price_modal,
    price_sell,
    qty,
    minStock,
    categories,
    units,
    loadCategories,
    loadUnits,
    handleSubmit,
    errors,
    isSubmitting
  }
}

===== src/composables/useShopping.js =====
// src/composables/useShopping.js
import { ref, computed, watch } from 'vue'
import { shoppingService } from '../services/shoppingService.js'
import { productRepo } from '../db/repositories/productRepository.js'
import { categoryRepo } from '../db/repositories/categoryRepository.js'

export function useShopping() {
  const items = ref([])
  const products = ref([])
  const categories = ref([])
  const searchQuery = ref('')
  const activeFilter = ref('ALL')
  const loading = ref(false)

  // State Modal Tambah Belanjaan & Searchbar Master Produk
  const showAddModal = ref(false)
  const isCustomItem = ref(false)
  const selectedProduct = ref(null)
  const productSearch = ref('')

  const form = ref({
    productId: null,
    name: '',
    category: 'Umum',
    qty: 0,
    unit: 'pcs',
    estimatedPrice: 0,
    notes: ''
  })

  // Filter Master Produk berdasarkan query di modal
  const filteredMasterProducts = computed(() => {
    const q = productSearch.value.toLowerCase().trim()
    if (!q) return []
    return products.value
      .filter(p => p.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) || (p.code || '').toLowerCase().includes(q))
      .slice(0, 10)
  })

  // Opsi pilihan satuan (Eceran & Grosir/Pack) berdasarkan skema DB Dexie
  const availableUnits = computed(() => {
    if (!selectedProduct.value) return []
    const p = selectedProduct.value
    const units = []

    // 1. Satuan Eceran (pcs / unit utama)
    const ecerUnit = p.unit || 'pcs'
    const ecerModal = Number(p.price_modal) || 0
    units.push({
      label: `${ecerUnit} (Ecer)`,
      value: ecerUnit,
      priceModal: ecerModal,
      type: 'unit'
    })

    // 2. Satuan Grosir / Pack / Dus (Membaca purchasePackName, packQty, packPrice)
    const packUnit = p.purchasePackName || p.packUnit || p.pack_unit
    const packQty = Number(p.packQty || p.pack_qty) || 1

    // Ambil harga modal grosir (packPrice) atau hitung otomatis
    let packModalPrice = Number(p.packPrice || p.packPriceModal)

    if (!packModalPrice || isNaN(packModalPrice) || packModalPrice === 0) {
      packModalPrice = ecerModal * packQty
    }

    // Masukkan opsi grosir jika purchasePackName terisi
    if (packUnit) {
      units.push({
        label: `${packUnit} (${packQty} ${ecerUnit})`,
        value: packUnit,
        priceModal: packModalPrice,
        type: 'pack'
      })
    }

    return units
  })

  // Watcher: Hitung otomatis estimasi modal berdasarkan input qty & satuan yang dipilih
  watch(
    [() => form.value.qty, () => form.value.unit],
    ([newQty, newUnit]) => {
      if (!selectedProduct.value) return

      const qtyNum = Number(newQty) || 0
      const matchedUnit = availableUnits.value.find(u => u.value === newUnit)
      const unitPriceModal = matchedUnit ? matchedUnit.priceModal : (selectedProduct.value.price_modal || 0)

      form.value.estimatedPrice = Math.round(unitPriceModal * qtyNum)
    }
  )

  // State Modal Konfirmasi Bayar Kulakan
  const showPayModal = ref(false)
  const targetItemForPay = ref(null)
  const payForm = ref({
    actualPrice: 0,
    accountId: 'acc_cash'
  })

  const totalItems = computed(() => items.value.length)
  const boughtItemsCount = computed(() => items.value.filter(i => i.isBought).length)
  const pendingItemsCount = computed(() => items.value.filter(i => !i.isBought).length)
  
  const estimatedTotalCost = computed(() => {
    return items.value.reduce((sum, item) => sum + (Number(item.estimatedPrice) || 0), 0)
  })

  const boughtTotalCost = computed(() => {
    return items.value
      .filter(i => i.isBought)
      .reduce((sum, item) => sum + (Number(item.estimatedPrice) || 0), 0)
  })

  const filteredItems = computed(() => {
    const q = searchQuery.value.toLowerCase().trim()
    return items.value.filter(item => {
      const matchQuery = !q || 
        item.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) || 
        (item.category || '').toLowerCase().includes(q)
      
      const matchFilter = 
        activeFilter.value === 'ALL' ||
        (activeFilter.value === 'PENDING' && !item.isBought) ||
        (activeFilter.value === 'BOUGHT' && item.isBought)

      return matchQuery && matchFilter
    })
  })

  const loadData = async () => {
    loading.value = true
    try {
      const [shoppingData, productData, categoryData] = await Promise.all([
        shoppingService.getShoppingItems(),
        productRepo.getAll(),
        categoryRepo.getAll()
      ])
      items.value = shoppingData
      products.value = productData
      categories.value = categoryData
    } finally {
      loading.value = false
    }
  }

  const handleAutoRestock = async () => {
    loading.value = true
    try {
      const addedCount = await shoppingService.populateLowStockItems()
      await loadData()
      return addedCount
    } finally {
      loading.value = false
    }
  }

  const handleToggleClick = (item) => {
    if (!item.isBought) {
      targetItemForPay.value = item
      payForm.value = {
        actualPrice: item.estimatedPrice || 0,
        accountId: 'acc_cash'
      }
      showPayModal.value = true
    } else {
      if (confirm(`Batalkan status selesai beli "${item.name}"?\nStok barang dan kas/bank akan dikembalikan.`)) {
        submitToggleBought(item, { accountId: 'acc_cash', actualPrice: item.estimatedPrice })
      }
    }
  }

  const submitToggleBought = async (item, options) => {
    await shoppingService.toggleBoughtStatus(item, options)
    showPayModal.value = false
    targetItemForPay.value = null
    await loadData()
  }

  const openModal = () => {
    isCustomItem.value = false
    selectedProduct.value = null
    productSearch.value = ''
    form.value = {
      productId: null,
      name: '',
      category: categories.value[0]?.name || 'Umum',
      qty: 0,
      unit: 'pcs',
      estimatedPrice: 0,
      notes: ''
    }
    showAddModal.value = true
  }

  const selectProductForAdd = (product) => {
    selectedProduct.value = product
    form.value.productId = product.id
    form.value.name = product.name
    form.value.category = product.category || 'Umum'
    form.value.qty = 0
    form.value.estimatedPrice = 0

    // Pilih otomatis opsi grosir jika produk memiliki purchasePackName
    const packUnit = product.purchasePackName || product.packUnit || product.pack_unit
    if (packUnit) {
      form.value.unit = packUnit
    } else {
      form.value.unit = product.unit || 'pcs'
    }

    productSearch.value = ''
  }

  const saveItem = async () => {
    if (!form.value.name.trim()) return

    await shoppingService.addShoppingItem(form.value)
    productSearch.value = ''
    showAddModal.value = false
    await loadData()
  }

  const deleteItem = async (id) => {
    await shoppingService.removeItem(id)
    await loadData()
  }

  const clearCompletedItems = async () => {
    await shoppingService.clearCompleted()
    await loadData()
  }

  const exportShoppingListText = () => {
    const pending = items.value.filter(i => !i.isBought)
    if (pending.length === 0) return ''

    let text = `*DAFTAR BELANJA KULAKAN SINAR PAGI*\n`
    text += `Tanggal: ${new Date().toLocaleDateString('id-ID')}\n`
    text += `===============================\n\n`

    pending.forEach((item, idx) => {
      text += `${idx + 1}. *${item.name}*\n`
      text += `   • Jumlah: ${item.qty} ${item.unit}\n`
      if (item.estimatedPrice > 0) {
        text += `   • Est. Modal: Rp ${item.estimatedPrice.toLocaleString('id-ID')}\n`
      }
      if (item.notes) {
        text += `   • Catatan: ${item.notes}\n`
      }
      text += `\n`
    })

    text += `===============================\n`
    text += `*Estimasi Total Modal:* Rp ${estimatedTotalCost.value.toLocaleString('id-ID')}\n`

    return text
  }

  return {
    items,
    products,
    categories,
    searchQuery,
    activeFilter,
    loading,
    showAddModal,
    showPayModal,
    targetItemForPay,
    payForm,
    isCustomItem,
    selectedProduct,
    productSearch,
    filteredMasterProducts,
    availableUnits,
    form,
    totalItems,
    boughtItemsCount,
    pendingItemsCount,
    estimatedTotalCost,
    boughtTotalCost,
    filteredItems,
    loadData,
    handleAutoRestock,
    handleToggleClick,
    submitToggleBought,
    openModal,
    selectProductForAdd,
    saveItem,
    deleteItem,
    clearCompletedItems,
    exportShoppingListText
  }
}

===== src/composables/useKasir.js =====
// src/composables/useKasir.js
import { ref, computed, onMounted, onUnmounted, onActivated, onDeactivated } from 'vue'
import { kasirService } from '../services/kasirService.js'
import { salesHistoryService } from '../services/salesHistoryService.js'
import { packageRepository } from '../db/repositories/packageRepository.js'
import { serviceRepository } from '../db/repositories/serviceRepository.js'
import { formatRibuan } from '../utils/formatters/currency.js'
import { useKasirStore } from '../stores/kasirStore.js'
import { useOperatorStore } from '../stores/operatorStore.js'
import { toast } from 'vue-sonner'

export function useKasir() {
  const store = useKasirStore()
  const operatorStore = useOperatorStore()

  // State Data Master
  const products = ref([])
  const members = ref([])
  const packagesList = ref([])
  const servicesList = ref([])

  // State UI & Pencarian
  const q = ref('')
  const memberQ = ref('')
  const showSearch = ref(false)
  const selectedProductForOption = ref(null)

  // Computed Keuangan & Pembayaran
  const total = computed(() => store.cart.reduce((s, c) => s + c.price_sell * c.cartQty, 0))
  const kembalian = computed(() => Math.max(0, (store.bayarNominal || 0) - total.value))
  const kurangBayar = computed(() => Math.max(0, total.value - (store.bayarNominal || 0)))

  const bayarDisplay = computed({
    get: () => store.bayarNominal ? formatRibuan(store.bayarNominal) : '',
    set: (val) => {
      store.bayarNominal = parseInt(String(val).replace(/\D/g, '') || '0', 10)
    }
  })

  // Computed Filter Produk & Member
  const filteredProducts = computed(() => {
    const qq = q.value.toLowerCase().trim()
    if (!qq) return products.value.slice(0, 15)

    return products.value
      .filter(p => p.name.toLowerCase().includes(qq) || (p.code || '').toLowerCase().includes(qq))
      .sort((a, b) => {
        const nameA = a.name.toLowerCase()
        const nameB = b.name.toLowerCase()
        const idxA = nameA.indexOf(qq)
        const idxB = nameB.indexOf(qq)

        if (idxA !== -1 && idxB !== -1) {
          if (idxA !== idxB) return idxA - idxB
          return nameA.localeCompare(nameB)
        }
        if (idxA !== -1) return -1
        if (idxB !== -1) return 1
        return 0
      })
      .slice(0, 20)
  })

  const filteredMembers = computed(() => {
    const qq = memberQ.value.toLowerCase().trim()
    if (!qq) return members.value.slice(0, 20)
    return members.value.filter(m => m.name.toLowerCase().includes(qq) || (m.phone || '').includes(qq)).slice(0, 20)
  })

  const filteredServicesByCategory = computed(() => {
    if (!selectedProductForOption.value) return []
    const pCat = (selectedProductForOption.value.category || '').toLowerCase()
    return servicesList.value.filter(s => (s.category || '').toLowerCase() === pCat)
  })

  // Muat Data Awal
  const load = async () => {
    const [data, packages, services] = await Promise.all([
      kasirService.getInitialData(),
      packageRepository.getAll(),
      serviceRepository.getAll()
    ])
    products.value = data.products
    members.value = data.members
    packagesList.value = packages
    servicesList.value = services

    if (!store.editingTransactionId) {
      store.bayarNominal = 0
    }
  }

  const getProductPackages = (productId) => {
    return packagesList.value.filter(pkg => pkg.productId === productId)
  }

  const handleSelectProduct = (product) => {
    const pCat = (product.category || '').toLowerCase()
    const isPackageCategory = ['rokok', 'permen'].includes(pCat)
    const hasPackages = getProductPackages(product.id).length > 0
    const hasConfiguredServices = servicesList.value.some(s => (s.category || '').toLowerCase() === pCat)

    if ((isPackageCategory && hasPackages) || (!isPackageCategory && hasConfiguredServices)) {
      selectedProductForOption.value = product
    } else {
      store.addToCart(product)
      q.value = ''
      showSearch.value = false
    }
  }

  const confirmAddToCart = (product, options) => {
    const targetPrice = (options.price_sell !== undefined) ? options.price_sell : product.price_sell
    store.addToCart({ ...product, price_sell: targetPrice }, options)
    selectedProductForOption.value = null
    showSearch.value = false
    q.value = ''
  }

  // Proses Checkout & Edit Transaksi
  const checkout = async () => {
    if (store.paymentMethod === 'TEMPO' && !store.selectedMember) throw new Error('Pilih member untuk TEMPO')
    if (store.paymentMethod === 'CASH' && store.bayarNominal < total.value) throw new Error('Nominal kurang')

    if (store.editingTransactionId) {
      const originalTrx = {
        id: store.editingTransactionId,
        paymentMethod: store.paymentMethod,
        memberId: store.selectedMember?.id || null,
        total: total.value
      }

      const updatedItems = store.cart.map(item => ({
        productId: item.id,
        productName: item.name,
        price: item.price_sell,
        qty: item.cartQty,
        serviceId: item.isServed ? 'SERVICE_SEDUH' : null
      }))

      const newPaymentData = {
        paymentMethod: store.paymentMethod,
        memberId: store.selectedMember?.id || null,
        amountPaid: store.paymentMethod === 'TEMPO' ? 0 : store.paymentMethod === 'QRIS' ? total.value : store.bayarNominal
      }

      const res = await salesHistoryService.updateTransaction(originalTrx, updatedItems, newPaymentData)
      store.clearCart()
      return res
    }

    const res = await kasirService.checkout({
      cart: store.cart,
      member: store.selectedMember,
      paymentMethod: store.paymentMethod,
      operator: operatorStore.activeOperator,
      amountPaid: store.paymentMethod === 'TEMPO' ? 0 : store.paymentMethod === 'QRIS' ? total.value : store.bayarNominal
    })
    store.clearCart()
    return res
  }

  // --- LOGIKA SCANNER BARCODE FISIK (HID MODE) ---
  let barcodeBuffer = ''
  let lastKeyTime = Date.now()

  const processBarcodeScan = (codeToScan) => {
    if (!codeToScan) return false
    const cleanCode = String(codeToScan).trim().toLowerCase()

    const matchedProduct = products.value.find(
      p => String(p.code || '').trim().toLowerCase() === cleanCode
    ) || products.value.find(
      p => String(p.name || '').trim().toLowerCase() === cleanCode
    )

    if (matchedProduct) {
      handleSelectProduct(matchedProduct)
      q.value = ''
      showSearch.value = false
      return true
    } else {
      toast.error(`Barang tidak ditemukan (${codeToScan})`)
      return false
    }
  }

  const handleGlobalKeydown = (e) => {
    if (['Shift', 'Control', 'Alt', 'Meta', 'CapsLock', 'Tab'].includes(e.key)) return

    const activeEl = document.activeElement
    const isTypingInInput = activeEl && ['INPUT', 'TEXTAREA', 'SELECT'].includes(activeEl.tagName)
    const isKasirSearchInput = activeEl && activeEl.id === 'kasir-search-input'
    
    if (isTypingInInput && !isKasirSearchInput) return

    const currentTime = Date.now()
    if (currentTime - lastKeyTime > 50) barcodeBuffer = ''

    if (e.key === 'Enter') {
      if (isKasirSearchInput && q.value.trim().length > 0) {
        e.preventDefault()
        const codeToScan = q.value.trim()
        barcodeBuffer = ''
        processBarcodeScan(codeToScan)
      } else if (barcodeBuffer.trim().length > 0) {
        e.preventDefault()
        const scannedCode = barcodeBuffer.trim()
        barcodeBuffer = ''
        processBarcodeScan(scannedCode)
      }
    } else if (e.key.length === 1) {
      if (!isTypingInInput || (currentTime - lastKeyTime < 30)) {
        barcodeBuffer += e.key
      }
    }

    lastKeyTime = currentTime
  }

  const attachListener = () => {
    window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleGlobalKeydown)
    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleGlobalKeydown)
  }

  const detachListener = () => {
    window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleGlobalKeydown)
  }

  onMounted(attachListener)
  onUnmounted(detachListener)
  onActivated(attachListener)
  onDeactivated(detachListener)

  return {
    store,
    operatorStore,
    q, 
    memberQ, 
    products, 
    filteredProducts, 
    filteredMembers,
    total, 
    kembalian, 
    kurangBayar, 
    bayarDisplay, 
    showSearch,
    selectedProductForOption, 
    filteredServicesByCategory,
    load, 
    handleSelectProduct, 
    confirmAddToCart, 
    getProductPackages, 
    checkout,
    processBarcodeScan
  }
}

===== src/composables/useProductScanner.js =====
// src/composables/useProductScanner.js
import { ref, onUnmounted } from 'vue'
import { Html5Qrcode } from 'html5-qrcode'

export function useProductScanner(elementId = 'reader') {
  const isScanning = ref(false)
  let qr = null

  const start = async (onDetected) => {
    if (isScanning.value) {
      await stop()
      return
    }

    try {
      isScanning.value = true
      qr = new Html5Qrcode(elementId)
      await qr.start(
        { facingMode: "environment" },
        { fps: 10, qrbox: 250 },
        (decoded) => {
          onDetected(decoded)
          stop()
        },
        null
      )
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("Gagal membuka kamera:", err)
      isScanning.value = false
      qr = null
      throw new Error("Kamera tidak dapat diakses atau tidak ditemukan.")
    }
  }

  const stop = async () => {
    if (qr && isScanning.value) {
      try {
        await qr.stop()
        qr.clear()
      } catch (e) {
        console.warn("Gagal menghentikan scanner:", e)
      } finally {
        isScanning.value = false
        qr = null
      }
    } else {
      isScanning.value = false
    }
  }

  onUnmounted(() => {
    stop()
  })

  return { isScanning, start, stop }
}

===== src/composables/useSettingsMenu.js =====
// src/composables/useSettingsMenu.js
import { ref } from 'vue'
import { productRepo } from '../db/repositories/productRepository.js'
import { packageRepository } from '../db/repositories/packageRepository.js'
import { serviceRepository } from '../db/repositories/serviceRepository.js'
import { categoryRepo } from '../db/repositories/categoryRepository.js'

export function useSettingsMenu() {
  const rokokProducts = ref([])
  const packages = ref([])
  const services = ref([])
  const categoryOptions = ref([])
  const loading = ref(false)

  const loadData = async () => {
    loading.value = true
    try {
      const [allProducts, allPackages, allServices, allCategories] = await Promise.all([
        productRepo.getAll(),
        packageRepository.getAll(),
        serviceRepository.getAll(),
        categoryRepo.getAll()
      ])
      
      const packageCategories = ['rokok', 'permen']
      
      rokokProducts.value = allProducts
        .filter(p => packageCategories.includes((p.category || '').toLowerCase()))
        .sort((a, b) => {
          const catCompare = (a.category || '').localeCompare(b.category || '')
          if (catCompare !== 0) return catCompare
          return (a.name || '').localeCompare(b.name || '')
        })

      packages.value = allPackages
      services.value = allServices

      // URUTKAN KATEGORI DARI A SEAMPAI Z
      categoryOptions.value = allCategories.sort((a, b) => 
        (a.name || '').localeCompare(b.name || '')
      )
    } finally {
      loading.value = false
    }
  }

  const getPackagesForProduct = (productId) => {
    return packages.value.filter(pkg => pkg.productId === productId)
  }

  const savePackage = async (pkgData) => {
    await packageRepository.savePackage(pkgData)
    await loadData()
  }

  const deletePackage = async (id) => {
    await packageRepository.deletePackage(id)
    await loadData()
  }

  const saveService = async (serviceData) => {
    await serviceRepository.saveService(serviceData)
    await loadData()
  }

  const deleteService = async (id) => {
    await serviceRepository.deleteService(id)
    await loadData()
  }

  return {
    rokokProducts,
    packages,
    services,
    categoryOptions,
    loading,
    loadData,
    getPackagesForProduct,
    savePackage,
    deletePackage,
    saveService,
    deleteService
  }
}

===== src/composables/useSalesHistory.js =====
import { ref, computed } from 'vue'
import { salesHistoryService } from '../services/salesHistoryService.js'
import { toast } from 'vue-sonner'
import dayjs from 'dayjs'
import localeId from 'dayjs/locale/id'

dayjs.locale(localeId)

export function useSalesHistory() {
  const transactions = ref([])
  const totalPiutang = ref(0)
  const searchQuery = ref('')
  const selectedFilter = ref('TODAY') // Default langsung ke 'Hari Ini'
  const selectedTransaction = ref(null)
  const loading = ref(false)

  const filterOptions = [
    { label: 'Hari Ini', value: 'TODAY' },
    { label: 'Semua', value: 'ALL' },
    { label: 'Barang', value: 'BARANG' },
    { label: 'Digital', value: 'DIGITAL' },
    { label: 'Cash', value: 'CASH' },
    { label: 'QRIS', value: 'QRIS' },
    { label: 'Tempo', value: 'TEMPO' },
    { label: 'Void', value: 'VOID' }
  ]

  const loadHistory = async () => {
    loading.value = true
    try {
      const data = await salesHistoryService.getCombinedHistory()
      transactions.value = data.transactions || []
      totalPiutang.value = data.totalPiutang || 0
    } catch (e) {
      toast.error(`Gagal memuat riwayat: ${e.message}`)
    } finally {
      loading.value = false
    }
  }

  const handleVoid = async (transaction) => {
    if (!confirm(`Apakah Anda yakin ingin membatalkan (VOID) nota ${transaction.id.slice(0, 8)}?\nStok barang dan kas/bank akan dikembalikan.`)) {
      return
    }

    loading.value = true
    try {
      await salesHistoryService.voidTransaction(transaction)
      toast.success('Transaksi berhasil dibatalkan!')
      selectedTransaction.value = null
      await loadHistory()
    } catch (e) {
      toast.error(`Gagal membatalkan transaksi: ${e.message}`)
    } finally {
      loading.value = false
    }
  }

  const filteredTransactions = computed(() => {
    const q = searchQuery.value.toLowerCase().trim()

    return transactions.value.filter(t => {
      // 1. Filter Pencarian Teks (Cari ID, Member, Provider, atau Nama Produk)
      const matchQuery = !q || 
        (t.memberName || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) || 
        (t.provider || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) || 
        (t.id || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        t.items?.some(item => (item.productName || '').toLowerCase().includes(q))

      // 2. Filter Kategori / Tanggal Hari Ini
      let matchFilter = true
      if (selectedFilter.value === 'TODAY') {
        matchFilter = dayjs(t.date).isSame(dayjs(), 'day')
      } else if (selectedFilter.value === 'BARANG') {
        matchFilter = t.trxType === 'BARANG'
      } else if (selectedFilter.value === 'DIGITAL') {
        matchFilter = t.trxType === 'DIGITAL'
      } else if (selectedFilter.value === 'CASH') {
        matchFilter = (t.paymentMethod === 'CASH' || t.paymentMethod === 'TUNAI')
      } else if (selectedFilter.value === 'QRIS') {
        matchFilter = t.paymentMethod === 'QRIS'
      } else if (selectedFilter.value === 'TEMPO') {
        matchFilter = t.paymentMethod === 'TEMPO'
      } else if (selectedFilter.value === 'VOID') {
        matchFilter = (t.status === 'void' || t.status === 'CANCELLED')
      }

      return matchQuery && matchFilter
    })
  })

  const totalOmzet = computed(() => {
    return filteredTransactions.value
      .filter(t => t.status !== 'void' && t.status !== 'CANCELLED')
      .reduce((sum, t) => sum + (t.total || 0), 0)
  })

  const formatDate = (dateString) => {
    if (!dateString) return '-'
    return dayjs(dateString).format('DD MMM YYYY • HH:mm')
  }

  return {
    transactions,
    searchQuery,
    selectedFilter,
    selectedTransaction,
    loading,
    filterOptions,
    filteredTransactions,
    totalOmzet,
    totalPiutang,
    loadHistory,
    handleVoid,
    formatDate
  }
}

===== src/composables/useFinance.js =====
// src/composables/useFinance.js
import { ref, computed } from 'vue'
import { financeRepo } from '../db/repositories/financeRepository.js'
import { toast } from 'vue-sonner'

export function useFinance() {
  const accounts = ref([])
  const mutations = ref([])
  const totalPiutang = ref(0)
  const loading = ref(false)

  const totalSaldo = computed(() => accounts.value.reduce((s, a) => s + (a.balance || 0), 0))

  const loadData = async () => {
    loading.value = true
    try {
      accounts.value = await financeRepo.getAccounts()
      mutations.value = await financeRepo.getMutations()
      totalPiutang.value = await financeRepo.getTotalPiutang()
    } finally {
      loading.value = false
    }
  }

  const syncFromHistory = async () => {
    loading.value = true
    try {
      await financeRepo.recalculateFromHistory()
      await loadData()
      toast.success('Berhasil menghitung ulang saldo dan piutang!')
    } catch (err) {
      toast.error(`Gagal menghitung ulang: ${err.message}`)
    } finally {
      loading.value = false
    }
  }

  const setBalance = async (accountId, amount, note) => {
    const targetAmount = Number(amount)

    if (isNaN(targetAmount) || targetAmount < 0) {
      toast.error('Nominal saldo awal tidak boleh bernilai negatif!')
      return false
    }

    await financeRepo.setInitialBalance(accountId, targetAmount, note)
    toast.success('Saldo awal berhasil diperbarui!')
    await loadData()
    return true
  }

  const handleTransfer = async (fromAccId, toAccId, amount, note) => {
    const transferAmount = Number(amount)

    if (isNaN(transferAmount) || transferAmount <= 0) {
      toast.error('Nominal transfer harus lebih besar dari 0!')
      return
    }
    
    await financeRepo.addMutation({
      accountId: fromAccId, type: 'OUT', amount: transferAmount,
      category: 'Pindah Saldo', note: `Transfer ke ${toAccId}: ${note}`
    })

    await financeRepo.addMutation({
      accountId: toAccId, type: 'IN', amount: transferAmount,
      category: 'Pindah Saldo', note: `Transfer dari ${fromAccId}: ${note}`
    })

    toast.success('Transfer saldo berhasil!')
    await loadData()
  }

  return {
    accounts,
    mutations,
    totalSaldo,
    totalPiutang,
    loading,
    loadData,
    syncFromHistory,
    setBalance,
    handleTransfer
  }
}

import { formatRp, formatRibuan, toNumber } from '../utils/formatters/currency.js'

export function useRupiah(fieldRef, withPrefix = true) {

===== src/composables/useMembers.js =====
// src/composables/useMembers.js
import { ref, computed } from 'vue'
import { memberRepo } from '../db/repositories/memberRepository.js'
import { memberService } from '../services/memberService.js'
import { toast } from 'vue-sonner'

export function useMembers() {
  const members = ref([])
  const searchQuery = ref('')

  const showModal = ref(false)
  const editingId = ref(null)
  const form = ref({ name: '', phone: '', address: '' })

  const showPayModal = ref(false)
  const selectedMemberForPay = ref(null)
  const payAmount = ref(0)
  const paymentMethod = ref('CASH')

  // Mengurutkan daftar member secara alfabetis A-Z
  const filteredMembers = computed(() => {
    const q = searchQuery.value.toLowerCase().trim()
    let list = members.value

    if (q) {
      list = list.filter(m => 
        (m.name || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) || 
        (m.phone || '').includes(q)
      )
    }

    return [...list].sort((a, b) => (a.name || '').localeCompare(b.name || ''))
  })

  const loadMembers = async () => {
    members.value = await memberRepo.getAll()
  }

  const openModal = (m = null) => {
    if (m) {
      editingId.value = m.id
      form.value = { name: m.name, phone: m.phone || '', address: m.address || '' }
    } else {
      editingId.value = null
      form.value = { name: '', phone: '', address: '' }
    }
    showModal.value = true
  }

  const saveMember = async () => {
    if (!form.value.name.trim()) return

    if (editingId.value) {
      await memberRepo.update(editingId.value, form.value)
    } else {
      await memberRepo.create(form.value)
    }

    showModal.value = false
    await loadMembers()
  }

  const deleteMember = async (m) => {
    if (confirm(`Hapus member "${m.name}"?`)) {
      await memberRepo.delete(m.id)
      await loadMembers()
    }
  }

  const openPayModal = (m) => {
    selectedMemberForPay.value = m
    payAmount.value = m.debt || 0
    paymentMethod.value = 'CASH'
    showPayModal.value = true
  }

  const submitPayDebt = async () => {
    if (!selectedMemberForPay.value) return

    const amount = Number(payAmount.value)
    const currentDebt = Number(selectedMemberForPay.value.debt || 0)

    if (isNaN(amount) || amount <= 0) {
      toast.error('Nominal pembayaran harus berupa angka lebih dari 0!')
      return
    }

    if (amount > currentDebt) {
      toast.error(`Nominal pembayaran melebihi sisa utang! (Maks: Rp ${currentDebt.toLocaleString('id-ID')})`)
      return
    }

    try {
      await memberService.payDebt({
        memberId: selectedMemberForPay.value.id,
        amount,
        paymentMethod: paymentMethod.value
      })

      toast.success('Pembayaran utang berhasil dicatat!')
      showPayModal.value = false
      payAmount.value = 0
      selectedMemberForPay.value = null
      paymentMethod.value = 'CASH'

      await loadMembers()
    } catch (e) {
      toast.error(e.message || 'Gagal memproses pembayaran utang')
    }
  }

  return {
    members,
    searchQuery,
    filteredMembers,
    showModal,
    editingId,
    form,
    showPayModal,
    selectedMemberForPay,
    payAmount,
    paymentMethod,
    loadMembers,
    openModal,
    saveMember,
    deleteMember,
    openPayModal,
    submitPayDebt
  }
}

===== src/composables/useCategories.js =====
// src/composables/useCategories.js
import { ref, computed, nextTick } from 'vue'
import { categoryRepo } from '../db/repositories/categoryRepository.js'
import { unitRepo } from '../db/repositories/unitRepository.js'

export function useCategories() {
  const categories = ref([])
  const units = ref([])
  const searchQuery = ref('')
  const activeTab = ref('category') // Pindahkan state tab ke composable
  const showModal = ref(false)
  const formName = ref('')
  const editingId = ref(null)
  const inputRef = ref(null)

  const filteredCategories = computed(() => {
    const q = searchQuery.value.toLowerCase().trim()
    if (!q) return categories.value
    return categories.value.filter(c => c.name.toLowerCase().includes(q))
  })

  const filteredUnits = computed(() => {
    const q = searchQuery.value.toLowerCase().trim()
    if (!q) return units.value
    return units.value.filter(u => u.name.toLowerCase().includes(q))
  })

  const loadCategories = async () => {
    const data = await categoryRepo.getAll()
    categories.value = data.sort((a, b) => (a.name || '').localeCompare(b.name || ''))
  }

  const loadUnits = async () => {
    const data = await unitRepo.getAll()
    units.value = data.sort((a, b) => (a.name || '').localeCompare(b.name || ''))
  }

  // Unified Modal Handler (Support Category & Unit)
  const openModal = (item = null) => {
    if (item) {
      editingId.value = item.id
      formName.value = item.name
    } else {
      editingId.value = null
      formName.value = ''
    }
    showModal.value = true
    nextTick(() => inputRef.value?.focus())
  }

  const closeModal = () => {
    showModal.value = false
    formName.value = ''
    editingId.value = null
  }

  const saveCategory = async () => {
    const formattedName = formName.value.trim().toUpperCase()
    if (!formattedName) return

    if (editingId.value) {
      await categoryRepo.update(editingId.value, formattedName)
    } else {
      await categoryRepo.create(formattedName)
    }

    closeModal()
    await loadCategories()
  }

  const deleteCategory = async (cat) => {
    if (confirm(`Hapus kategori "${cat.name}"?`)) {
      await categoryRepo.delete(cat.id)
      await loadCategories()
    }
  }

  const saveUnit = async () => {
    const formattedName = formName.value.trim().toUpperCase()
    if (!formattedName) return

    if (editingId.value) {
      await unitRepo.update(editingId.value, formattedName)
    } else {
      await unitRepo.create(formattedName)
    }

    closeModal()
    await loadUnits()
  }

  const deleteUnit = async (u) => {
    if (confirm(`Hapus satuan "${u.name}"?`)) {
      await unitRepo.delete(u.id)
      await loadUnits()
    }
  }

  // Single Form Submit Handler
  const handleSubmit = async () => {
    if (!formName.value.trim()) return

    if (activeTab.value === 'category') {
      await saveCategory()
    } else {
      await saveUnit()
    }
  }

  return {
    categories,
    units,
    searchQuery,
    activeTab,
    filteredCategories,
    filteredUnits,
    showModal,
    formName,
    editingId,
    inputRef,
    loadCategories,
    loadUnits,
    openModal,
    closeModal,
    handleSubmit,
    deleteCategory,
    deleteUnit
  }
}

===== src/composables/useExpenses.js =====
// src/composables/useExpenses.js
import { ref, computed } from 'vue'
import { expenseService } from '../services/expenseService.js'
import { toast } from 'vue-sonner'
import dayjs from 'dayjs'

export function useExpenses() {
  const expenses = ref([])
  const searchQuery = ref('')
  const selectedCategory = ref('ALL')
  const loading = ref(false)

  const showModal = ref(false)
  const form = ref({
    category: 'Plastik & Bungkusan',
    amount: null,
    paymentMethod: 'CASH',
    note: '',
    date: dayjs().format('YYYY-MM-DDTHH:mm')
  })

  const categoryOptions = [
    'Plastik & Bungkusan',
    'Listrik & Air',
    'Sewa Tempat',
    'Gaji Karyawan',
    'Transportasi / Bensin',
    'Perbaikan & Alat',
    'Lain-lain'
  ]

  const loadExpenses = async () => {
    loading.value = true
    try {
      expenses.value = await expenseService.getAllExpenses()
    } finally {
      loading.value = false
    }
  }

  const filteredExpenses = computed(() => {
    const q = searchQuery.value.toLowerCase().trim()
    return expenses.value.filter(e => {
      const matchQuery = !q || 
        (e.note || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) || 
        (e.category || '').toLowerCase().includes(q)
      
      const matchCat = selectedCategory.value === 'ALL' || e.category === selectedCategory.value

      return matchQuery && matchCat
    })
  })

  const totalHariIni = computed(() => {
    const today = dayjs().startOf('day')
    return expenses.value
      .filter(e => dayjs(e.date).isAfter(today))
      .reduce((sum, e) => sum + (Number(e.amount) || 0), 0)
  })

  const totalBulanIni = computed(() => {
    const startOfMonth = dayjs().startOf('month')
    return expenses.value
      .filter(e => dayjs(e.date).isAfter(startOfMonth))
      .reduce((sum, e) => sum + (Number(e.amount) || 0), 0)
  })

  const resetForm = () => {
    form.value = {
      category: 'Plastik & Bungkusan',
      amount: null,
      paymentMethod: 'CASH',
      note: '',
      date: dayjs().format('YYYY-MM-DDTHH:mm')
    }
  }

  const openModal = () => {
    resetForm()
    showModal.value = true
  }

  const closeModal = () => {
    showModal.value = false
    resetForm()
  }

  const saveExpense = async () => {
    const amount = Number(form.value.amount)

    // GUARD: Angka positif & valid
    if (isNaN(amount) || amount <= 0) {
      toast.error('Nominal pengeluaran harus berupa angka lebih dari 0!')
      return
    }

    loading.value = true
    try {
      await expenseService.addExpense({
        ...form.value,
        amount
      })
      toast.success('Pengeluaran berhasil dicatat & kas dipotong!')
      closeModal()
      await loadExpenses()
    } catch (err) {
      toast.error(`Gagal menyimpan: ${err.message}`)
    } finally {
      loading.value = false
    }
  }

  const deleteExpense = async (expense) => {
    if (!confirm(`Hapus catatan pengeluaran "${expense.category}" (Rp ${expense.amount.toLocaleString('id-ID')})?\nSaldo kas/bank akan dikembalikan.`)) {
      return
    }

    loading.value = true
    try {
      await expenseService.deleteExpense(expense)
      toast.success('Pengeluaran dihapus & kas dikembalikan!')
      await loadExpenses()
    } catch (err) {
      toast.error(`Gagal menghapus: ${err.message}`)
    } finally {
      loading.value = false
    }
  }

  const formatDate = (d) => dayjs(d).format('DD MMM YYYY • HH:mm')

  return {
    expenses,
    searchQuery,
    selectedCategory,
    loading,
    showModal,
    form,
    categoryOptions,
    filteredExpenses,
    totalHariIni,
    totalBulanIni,
    loadExpenses,
    openModal,
    closeModal,
    saveExpense,
    deleteExpense,
    formatDate
  }
}
