===== src/composables/useReports.js =====
// src/composables/useReports.js
import { ref, computed } from 'vue'
import { reportService } from '../services/reportService.js'
import { toast } from 'vue-sonner'
import dayjs from 'dayjs'

export function useReports() {
  const loading = ref(false)
  const rawTransactions = ref([])
  const rawTransactionItems = ref([])
  const rawDigitalTransactions = ref([])
  const productsMap = ref(new Map())

  const selectedPeriod = ref('today')

  const periodOptions = [
    { label: 'Hari Ini', value: 'today' },
    { label: '7 Hari', value: 'week' },
    { label: 'Bulan Ini', value: 'month' },
    { label: 'Semua', value: 'all' }
  ]

  const loadData = async () => {
    loading.value = true
    try {
      const data = await reportService.getReportData()
      rawTransactions.value = data.rawTransactions
      rawTransactionItems.value = data.rawTransactionItems
      rawDigitalTransactions.value = data.rawDigitalTransactions
      productsMap.value = data.productsMap
    } catch (err) {
      toast.error(`Gagal memuat data laporan: ${err.message}`)
    } finally {
      loading.value = false
    }
  }

  const filteredTransactions = computed(() => {
    const now = dayjs()
    return rawTransactions.value.filter(t => {
      if (!t.date) return false
      const d = dayjs(t.date)
      if (selectedPeriod.value === 'today') return d.isSame(now, 'day')
      if (selectedPeriod.value === 'week') return d.isAfter(now.subtract(7, 'day'))
      if (selectedPeriod.value === 'month') return d.isSame(now, 'month')
      return true
    })
  })

  const filteredDigitalTransactions = computed(() => {
    const now = dayjs()
    return rawDigitalTransactions.value.filter(d => {
      if (!d.date) return false
      const date = dayjs(d.date)
      if (selectedPeriod.value === 'today') return date.isSame(now, 'day')
      if (selectedPeriod.value === 'week') return date.isAfter(now.subtract(7, 'day'))
      if (selectedPeriod.value === 'month') return date.isSame(now, 'month')
      return true
    })
  })

  const omzetBarang = computed(() => filteredTransactions.value.reduce((s, t) => s + (t.total || 0), 0))
  const countBarang = computed(() => filteredTransactions.value.length)

  const profitBarang = computed(() => {
    const validTrxIds = new Set(filteredTransactions.value.map(t => t.id))
    const items = rawTransactionItems.value.filter(i => validTrxIds.has(i.transactionId))
    
    return items.reduce((sum, item) => {
      const prod = productsMap.value.get(item.productId)
      const modal = prod?.price_modal || 0
      const profitPerPcs = (item.price || 0) - modal
      return sum + (Math.max(0, profitPerPcs) * (item.qty || 1))
    }, 0)
  })

  const profitDigital = computed(() => filteredDigitalTransactions.value.reduce((s, d) => s + (d.profit || d.adminFee || 0), 0))
  const countDigital = computed(() => filteredDigitalTransactions.value.length)

  const totalOmzet = computed(() => omzetBarang.value + profitDigital.value)
  const totalProfit = computed(() => profitBarang.value + profitDigital.value)
  const totalTrxCount = computed(() => countBarang.value + countDigital.value)

  const omzetCash = computed(() => filteredTransactions.value.filter(t => t.paymentMethod === 'CASH').reduce((s, t) => s + t.total, 0))
  const omzetQRIS = computed(() => filteredTransactions.value.filter(t => t.paymentMethod === 'QRIS').reduce((s, t) => s + t.total, 0))
  const omzetTempo = computed(() => filteredTransactions.value.filter(t => t.paymentMethod === 'TEMPO').reduce((s, t) => s + t.total, 0))

  const handleExportToExcel = () => {
    try {
      reportService.exportToExcel(
        filteredTransactions.value,
        filteredDigitalTransactions.value,
        selectedPeriod.value
      )
      toast.success('Laporan berhasil diexport ke Excel!')
    } catch (err) {
      toast.error(`Gagal export Excel: ${err.message}`)
    }
  }

  return {
    loading,
    selectedPeriod,
    periodOptions,
    filteredTransactions,
    filteredDigitalTransactions,
    totalOmzet,
    totalProfit,
    totalTrxCount,
    profitBarang,
    countBarang,
    profitDigital,
    countDigital,
    omzetCash,
    omzetQRIS,
    omzetTempo,
    loadData,
    handleExportToExcel
  }
}

===== src/composables/useSettings.js =====
// src/composables/useSettings.js
import { ref } from 'vue'
import { settingsRepo } from '../db/repositories/settingsRepository.js'
import { backupService } from '../services/backupService.js'
import { toast } from 'vue-sonner'

export function useSettings() {
  const loading = ref(false)
  const form = ref({
    storeName: '',
    storeAddress: '',
    storePhone: '',
    receiptFooter: '',
    printerPaperSize: '58mm',
    autoPrintReceipt: false
  })

  const loadSettings = async () => {
    loading.value = true
    try {
      const config = await settingsRepo.getSettings()
      form.value = { ...config }
    } finally {
      loading.value = false
    }
  }

  const saveSettings = async () => {
    loading.value = true
    try {
      await settingsRepo.saveSettings(form.value)
      toast.success('Pengaturan toko berhasil disimpan!')
    } catch (e) {
      toast.error(`Gagal menyimpan: ${e.message}`)
    } finally {
      loading.value = false
    }
  }

  const exportBackup = async () => {
    try {
      await backupService.exportBackup()
      toast.success('File backup berhasil dibuat!')
    } catch (e) {
      toast.error(`Gagal membuat backup: ${e.message}`)
    }
  }

  const importBackup = async (event) => {
    const file = event.target.files[0]
    if (!file) return

    if (!confirm('PERINGATAN: Impor backup akan memperbarui/menimpa data lokal saat ini. Lanjutkan?')) {
      event.target.value = ''
      return
    }

    try {
      await backupService.importBackup(file)
      toast.success('Data berhasil dipulihkan dari file backup!')
      setTimeout(() => window.location.reload(), 800)
    } catch (err) {
      toast.error(`File backup tidak valid: ${err.message}`)
    }
  }

  const backupToCloud = async () => {
    if (!navigator.onLine) {
      toast.error('Membutuhkan koneksi internet untuk cadangkan ke cloud.')
      return
    }

    loading.value = true
    try {
      await backupService.uploadBackupToCloud()
      toast.success('Berhasil mencadangkan data terbaru ke Cloud Firestore!')
    } catch (e) {
      toast.error(`Gagal cadangkan ke cloud: ${e.message}`)
    } finally {
      loading.value = false
    }
  }

  const restoreFromCloud = async () => {
    if (!confirm('PERINGATAN: Restor dari Cloud akan menimpa data lokal saat ini dengan cadangan terbaru dari Firebase Firestore. Lanjutkan?')) {
      return
    }

    loading.value = true
    try {
      await backupService.restoreFromCloud()
      toast.success('Data berhasil dipulihkan dari Firebase Cloud!')
      setTimeout(() => window.location.reload(), 800)
    } catch (err) {
      toast.error(`Gagal restor dari Cloud: ${err.message}`)
    } finally {
      loading.value = false
    }
  }

  const resetTransactionsData = async () => {
    const confirmation = confirm(
      'PERINGATAN DANGER!\n\nApakah Anda yakin ingin MENGHAPUS SEMUA RIWAYAT TRANSAKSI?\n\n' +
      'Data yang akan dihapus:\n' +
      '• Riwayat Penjualan Kasir & Item Nota\n' +
      '• Transaksi Digital (Topup/Tarik)\n' +
      '• Pengeluaran Operasional\n' +
      '• Mutasi Saldo Kas/Bank (Saldo direset ke Rp 0)\n' +
      '• Piutang & Poin Member (Direset ke Rp 0)\n' +
      '• Daftar Belanja Kulakan\n\n' +
      'Data Produk/Barang, Kategori, Satuan, dan Member TETAP AMAN.'
    )

    if (!confirmation) return

    loading.value = true
    try {
      await backupService.resetTransactions()
      toast.success('Semua riwayat transaksi berhasil dibersihkan!')
      setTimeout(() => window.location.reload(), 800)
    } catch (err) {
      toast.error(`Gagal mereset transaksi: ${err.message}`)
    } finally {
      loading.value = false
    }
  }

  const convertDataToUppercase = async () => {
    if (!confirm('Ubah seluruh data Barang, Kategori, Satuan, dan Paket Rokok lama menjadi KAPITAL secara permanen?')) {
      return
    }

    loading.value = true
    try {
      await backupService.convertAllToUppercase()
      toast.success('Semua data master berhasil diubah menjadi KAPITAL!')
      setTimeout(() => window.location.reload(), 800)
    } catch (err) {
      toast.error(`Gagal mengubah data ke kapital: ${err.message}`)
    } finally {
      loading.value = false
    }
  }

  return {
    form,
    loading,
    loadSettings,
    saveSettings,
    exportBackup,
    importBackup,
    backupToCloud,
    restoreFromCloud,
    resetTransactionsData,
    convertDataToUppercase
  }
}

===== src/composables/usePrinter.js =====
// src/composables/usePrinter.js - FIXED
import { ref } from 'vue'
import { toast } from 'vue-sonner'

// State global biar ke-share antar komponen
const isConnected = ref(false)
const isConnecting = ref(false)
const isPrinting = ref(false)
const printerDevice = ref(null)
const connectedDevice = ref(null)
const printerCharacteristic = ref(null)

export function usePrinter() {
  const checkBluetoothSupport = () => {
    if (!window.isSecureContext) {
      toast.error('Web Bluetooth butuh HTTPS.')
      return false
    }
    if (!navigator.bluetooth) {
      toast.error('Pakai Chrome Android/Desktop.')
      return false
    }
    return true
  }

  const connectPrinter = async () => {
    if (!checkBluetoothSupport()) return null
    try {
      isConnecting.value = true
      const device = await navigator.bluetooth.requestDevice({
        acceptAllDevices: true,
        optionalServices: ['000018f0-0000-1000-8000-00805f9b34fb', '49535343-fe7d-4ae5-8fa9-9fafd205e455']
      })

      const server = await device.gatt.connect()
      const services = await server.getPrimaryServices()
      
      let writeChar = null
      for (const service of services) {
        const chars = await service.getCharacteristics()
        for (const c of chars) {
          if (c.properties.write || c.properties.writeWithoutResponse) {
            writeChar = c
            break
          }
        }
        if (writeChar) break
      }

      if (!writeChar) throw new Error('Characteristic write tidak ditemukan')

      printerDevice.value = device
      connectedDevice.value = device
      printerCharacteristic.value = writeChar
      isConnected.value = true
      
      toast.success(`Terhubung: ${device.name || 'Printer Thermal'}`)
      return writeChar
    } catch (err) {
      if (err.name !== 'NotFoundError') {
        toast.error(`Gagal connect: ${err.message}`)
      } else {
        toast.info('Pencarian dibatalkan')
      }
      isConnected.value = false
      return null
    } finally {
      isConnecting.value = false
      isPrinting.value = false
    }
  }

  const toggleConnection = async () => {
    if (isConnected.value) {
      try {
        printerDevice.value?.gatt?.disconnect()
      } catch {}
      isConnected.value = false
      printerDevice.value = null
      connectedDevice.value = null
      printerCharacteristic.value = null
      toast.info('Printer terputus')
    } else {
      await connectPrinter()
    }
  }

  const printReceipt = async (encoderBytes) => {
    if (!checkBluetoothSupport()) return false
    try {
      isPrinting.value = true
      let char = printerCharacteristic.value
      let device = connectedDevice.value

      if (!isConnected.value || !device?.gatt?.connected || !char) {
        char = await connectPrinter()
        if (!char) return false
      }

      const chunkSize = 20
      for (let i = 0; i < encoderBytes.length; i += chunkSize) {
        const chunk = encoderBytes.slice(i, i + chunkSize)
        if (char.properties?.writeWithoutResponse) {
          await char.writeValueWithoutResponse(chunk)
        } else {
          await char.writeValue(chunk)
        }
        await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 30))
      }
      toast.success('Struk terkirim!')
      return true
    } catch (err) {
      toast.error(`Gagal cetak: ${err.message}`)
      return false
    } finally {
      isPrinting.value = false
    }
  }

  return {
    // yang dipakai MainLayout.vue
    isConnected,
    isConnecting,
    printerDevice,
    printerCharacteristic,
    toggleConnection,
    // yang dipakai ReceiptModal & file lama
    isPrinting,
    connectedDevice,
    checkBluetoothSupport,
    connectPrinter,
    printReceipt
  }
}
===== src/services/backupService.js =====

// src/services/backupService.js - VERSI FINAL HEMAT KUOTA
import { db } from '../db/index.js'
import dayjs from 'dayjs'
import { firestore, storage } from './firebase.js'
import { doc, getDoc } from 'firebase/firestore'
import { ref, uploadBytes, getDownloadURL } from 'firebase/storage'

export const backupService = {
  // 1. Export Manual ke File JSON Lokal - PERTAHANIN, ini yang paling aman
  async exportBackup() {
    try {
      const backupData = {}
      for (const table of db.tables) {
        backupData[table.name] = await table.toArray()
      }
      const jsonString = JSON.stringify(backupData, null, 2)
      const blob = new Blob([jsonString], { type: 'application/json' })
      const fileName = `${dayjs().format('DDMMYYYY-HH.mm.ss')}.json`
      const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob)
      const a = document.createElement('a')
      a.href = url
      a.download = fileName
      document.body.appendChild(a)
      a.click()
      document.body.removeChild(a)
      URL.revokeObjectURL(url)
      return true
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Gagal export backup:', error)
      throw error
    }
  },

  // 2. Auto Backup ke STORAGE (bukan Firestore doc) - HEMAT & GA JEBOL 1MB
  // Hanya backup master, jangan transaksi harian biar kecil
  async uploadBackupToCloud() {
    if (!navigator.onLine) return
    try {
      const masterTables = ['products','product_packages','categories','members','services','units','operators','cash_accounts','settings']
      const backupData = {}
      for (const t of masterTables) {
        if (db[t]) backupData[t] = await db[t].toArray()
      }
      const jsonString = JSON.stringify(backupData)
      const blob = new Blob([jsonString], { type: 'application/json' })
      
      // Simpan ke Storage, bukan Firestore doc - limit 5GB, bukan 1MB
      const fileName = `backups/master-${dayjs().format('YYYY-MM-DD-HHmm')}.json`
      const storageRef = ref(storage, fileName)
      await uploadBytes(storageRef, blob)
      console.log('[Backup] Success to Storage:', fileName)
      return true
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('[AutoBackup] Storage upload failed:', error)
      throw error
    }
  },

  // 3. Restor dari file lokal (yang paling aman pas develop di HP)
  async importBackup(file) {
    if (!file) return false
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      const reader = new FileReader()
      reader.onload = async (e) => {
        try {
          const rawParsed = JSON.parse(e.target.result)
          const importedData = rawParsed.database || rawParsed
          await this.processImportData(importedData)
          resolve(true)
        } catch (err) {
          console.error('Error saat impor backup:', err)
          reject(err)
        }
      }
      reader.onerror = () => reject(new Error('Gagal membaca file backup'))
      reader.readAsText(file)
    })
  },

  // Helper impor - jangan clear transaksi dulu kalo cuma master
  async processImportData(importedData) {
    const tableNames = db.tables.map(t => t.name)
    await db.transaction('rw', tableNames, async () => {
      for (const table of db.tables) {
        // Jangan clear semua kalo cuma restore master, tapi untuk full restore ok clear
        if (Object.keys(importedData).includes(table.name)) {
          await table.clear()
        }
      }
      for (const tableName of Object.keys(importedData)) {
        if (db[tableName]) {
          const rows = importedData[tableName]
          if (Array.isArray(rows) && rows.length > 0) {
            const preparedRows = rows.map(item => ({
              ...item,
              synced: item.synced ?? 1 // kalo dari backup cloud anggap sudah sync
            }))
            await db[tableName].bulkPut(preparedRows)
          }
        }
      }
    })
  },

  async resetTransactions() {
    const tableNames = db.tables.map(t => t.name)
    await db.transaction('rw', tableNames, async () => {
      await db.transactions.clear()
      await db.transaction_items.clear()
      await db.expenses.clear()
      await db.digital_transactions.clear()
      await db.debt_payments.clear()
      await db.cash_mutations.clear()
      await db.shopping_list.clear()
      const members = await db.members.toArray()
      for (const m of members) {
        await db.members.update(m.id, { debt: 0, total_spending: 0, points: 0, synced: 0 })
      }
      const accounts = await db.cash_accounts.toArray()
      for (const a of accounts) {
        await db.cash_accounts.update(a.id, { balance: 0, synced: 0 })
      }
    })
  },

  async convertAllToUppercase() {
    const tableNames = ['products', 'categories', 'units', 'product_packages', 'members']
    const now = dayjs().toISOString()
    await db.transaction('rw', tableNames, async () => {
      const products = await db.products.toArray()
      for (const p of products) {
        await db.products.update(p.id, { name: (p.name || '').toUpperCase().trim(), code: (p.code || '').toUpperCase().trim(), category: (p.category || 'UMUM').toUpperCase().trim(), unit: (p.unit || '').toUpperCase().trim(), purchasePackName: (p.purchasePackName || '').toUpperCase().trim(), updatedAt: now, synced: 0 })
      }
      const categories = await db.categories.toArray()
      for (const c of categories) { await db.categories.update(c.id, { name: (c.name || '').toUpperCase().trim(), updatedAt: now, synced: 0 }) }
      const units = await db.units.toArray()
      for (const u of units) { await db.units.update(u.id, { name: (u.name || '').toUpperCase().trim(), updatedAt: now, synced: 0 }) }
      const packages = await db.product_packages.toArray()
      for (const pkg of packages) { await db.product_packages.update(pkg.id, { name: (pkg.name || '').toUpperCase().trim(), updatedAt: now, synced: 0 }) }
      const members = await db.members.toArray()
      for (const m of members) { await db.members.update(m.id, { name: (m.name || '').toUpperCase().trim(), address: (m.address || '').toUpperCase().trim(), updatedAt: now, synced: 0 }) }
    })
  }
}

===== src/services/shoppingService.js =====
// src/services/shoppingService.js
import { shoppingRepository } from '../db/repositories/shoppingRepository.js'
import { productRepo } from '../db/repositories/productRepository.js'
import { financeRepo } from '../db/repositories/financeRepository.js'

export const shoppingService = {
  async getShoppingItems() {
    return await shoppingRepository.getAll()
  },

  async populateLowStockItems() {
    const allProducts = await productRepo.getAll()
    const currentShoppingList = await shoppingRepository.getAll()
    
    const lowStockProducts = allProducts.filter(p => (p.qty || 0) <= (p.minStock || 5))
    const existingProductIds = new Set(
      currentShoppingList.filter(i => !i.isBought).map(i => i.productId).filter(Boolean)
    )

    const itemsToAdd = []
    for (const p of lowStockProducts) {
      if (!existingProductIds.has(p.id)) {
        const targetQty = (p.packQty && p.packQty > 0) ? p.packQty : Math.max(10, (p.minStock || 5) * 2 - (p.qty || 0))
        
        itemsToAdd.push({
          productId: p.id,
          name: p.name,
          category: p.category || 'Umum',
          qty: targetQty,
          unit: p.unit || 'pcs',
          estimatedPrice: p.packPrice || (p.price_modal * targetQty) || 0,
          isBought: false,
          notes: `Restock otomatis (Stok sisa: ${p.qty})`
        })
      }
    }

    if (itemsToAdd.length > 0) {
      await shoppingRepository.addBulk(itemsToAdd)
    }

    return itemsToAdd.length
  },

  async toggleBoughtStatus(item, options = {}) {
    const { accountId = 'acc_cash', actualPrice = null } = options
    const newStatus = !item.isBought
    const finalPrice = actualPrice !== null ? Number(actualPrice) : (Number(item.estimatedPrice) || 0)

    // 1. Update status barang belanjaan & harga aktual
    await shoppingRepository.update(item.id, { 
      isBought: newStatus,
      estimatedPrice: finalPrice
    })

    if (newStatus) {
      // A. STATUS DIBELI (Selesai): Tambah stok & potong kas/bank
      if (item.productId) {
        const product = await productRepo.getById(item.productId)
        if (product) {
          const qtyToAdd = Number(item.qty) || 0
          const updatedQty = (product.qty || 0) + qtyToAdd

          const changes = { qty: updatedQty }
          if (finalPrice > 0 && item.qty > 0 && !product.packQty) {
            changes.price_modal = Math.round(finalPrice / item.qty)
          }

          await productRepo.update(item.productId, changes)
        }
      }

      // Catat Mutasi Pengeluaran Keuangan (OUT)
      if (finalPrice > 0) {
        const accName = accountId === 'acc_bank' ? 'Bank / QRIS' : 'Kas Laci'
        await financeRepo.addMutation({
          accountId,
          type: 'OUT',
          amount: finalPrice,
          category: 'Belanja Kulakan',
          note: `Kulakan ${item.name} (${item.qty} ${item.unit || 'pcs'}) via ${accName}`,
          refId: item.id
        })
      }
    } else {
      // B. BATAL DIBELI (Dicentang ulang jadi pending): Kurangi stok & kembalikan kas (IN)
      if (item.productId) {
        const product = await productRepo.getById(item.productId)
        if (product) {
          const qtyToRemove = Number(item.qty) || 0
          await productRepo.update(item.productId, {
            qty: Math.max(0, (product.qty || 0) - qtyToRemove)
          })
        }
      }

      // Catat Pengembalian Kas (IN)
      if (finalPrice > 0) {
        await financeRepo.addMutation({
          accountId,
          type: 'IN',
          amount: finalPrice,
          category: 'Pembatalan Belanja',
          note: `Batal Kulakan ${item.name}`,
          refId: item.id
        })
      }
    }

    return newStatus
  },

  async addShoppingItem(data) {
    const newItem = await shoppingRepository.add({
      productId: data.productId || null,
      name: data.name,
      category: data.category || 'Umum',
      qty: Number(data.qty) || 1,
      unit: data.unit || 'pcs',
      estimatedPrice: Number(data.estimatedPrice) || 0,
      isBought: false,
      notes: data.notes || ''
    })

    return newItem
  },

  async removeItem(id) {
    await shoppingRepository.delete(id)
  },

  async clearCompleted() {
    const count = await shoppingRepository.clearBought()
    return count
  }
}

===== src/services/dashboardService.js =====
// src/services/dashboardService.js
import { productRepo } from '../db/repositories/productRepository.js'
import { transactionRepo } from '../db/repositories/transactionRepository.js'
import { memberRepo } from '../db/repositories/memberRepository.js'
import { getBarangTipis } from '../utils/calculator/product.js'
import dayjs from 'dayjs'

export const dashboardService = {
  async getDashboardData() {
    try {
      const [products, transactions, members] = await Promise.all([
        productRepo.getAll().catch(() => []),
        transactionRepo.getAll().catch(() => []),
        memberRepo.getAll().catch(() => [])
      ])

      const tipis = getBarangTipis(products || [])
      const startToday = dayjs().startOf('day').toISOString()
      const trxToday = (transactions || []).filter(t => t && t.date >= startToday)

      const days = Array.from({ length: 7 }, (_, i) => dayjs().subtract(6 - i, 'day'))
      const chartRaw = days.map(d => {
        const start = d.startOf('day').toISOString()
        const end = d.endOf('day').toISOString()
        const total = (transactions || [])
          .filter(t => t && t.date >= start && t.date <= end)
          .reduce((s, t) => s + (Number(t.total) || 0), 0)
        return { label: d.format('DD MMM'), value: total, date: d }
      })

      // PERBAIKAN: Hitung total piutang langsung dari kolom `debt` milik member
      const totalPiutang = (members || []).reduce((sum, m) => sum + (Number(m.debt) || 0), 0)
      const memberBerutang = (members || []).filter(m => (Number(m.debt) || 0) > 0).length

      return {
        products: products || [],
        tipis: tipis || [],
        trxToday: trxToday || [],
        transactions: transactions || [],
        members: members || [],
        stats: {
          totalProducts: (products || []).length,
          lowStock: (tipis || []).length,
          trxHariIni: trxToday.length,
          omzetHariIni: trxToday.reduce((s, t) => s + (Number(t.total) || 0), 0),
          totalPiutang,
          memberBerutang
        },
        chartRaw
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Error dashboardService:', err)
      return {
        products: [], tipis: [], trxToday: [], transactions: [], members: [],
        stats: { totalProducts: 0, lowStock: 0, trxHariIni: 0, omzetHariIni: 0, totalPiutang: 0, memberBerutang: 0 },
        chartRaw: []
      }
    }
  }
}

===== src/services/receiptService.js =====
// src/services/receiptService.js
import { jsPDF } from 'jspdf'
import EscPosEncoder from 'esc-pos-encoder'
import { settingsRepo } from '../db/repositories/settingsRepository.js'
import { usePrinter } from '../composables/usePrinter.js'
import dayjs from 'dayjs'

export const receiptService = {
  // 1. Cetak Struk via Bluetooth Thermal Printer
  async printThermal(transaction) {
    if (!navigator.bluetooth) {
      throw new Error('Browser ini belum mendukung Web Bluetooth. Gunakan Chrome di Android.')
    }

    const { printerCharacteristic, isConnected, connectPrinter } = usePrinter()

    let targetChar = printerCharacteristic.value

    if (!isConnected.value || !targetChar) {
      targetChar = await connectPrinter()
    }

    if (!targetChar) {
      throw new Error('Printer Bluetooth tidak terhubung.')
    }

    const settings = await settingsRepo.getSettings()
    const is58mm = settings.printerPaperSize !== '80mm'
    const width = is58mm ? 32 : 48
    const line = '-'.repeat(width)

    // Alokasi lebar kolom (20 karakter detail + 12 karakter subtotal untuk 58mm)
    const detailWidth = is58mm ? 20 : 32
    const subtotalWidth = 12

    const EncoderClass = typeof EscPosEncoder === 'function' ? EscPosEncoder : (EscPosEncoder.default || EscPosEncoder)
    const encoder = new EncoderClass()

    const storeName = (settings.storeName || 'SINAR PAGI').toUpperCase()
    const address = settings.storeAddress || settings.address || ''
    const phone = settings.storePhone || settings.phone || ''

    // Formatter Tanggal & Waktu
    const dateObj = transaction.date ? new Date(transaction.date) : new Date()
    const validDate = isNaN(dateObj.getTime()) ? new Date() : dateObj
    const tglStr = dayjs(validDate).format('DD/MM/YYYY')
    const jamStr = dayjs(validDate).format('HH:mm')

    let encoded = encoder
      .initialize()
      .align('center')
      .line(storeName)

    if (address) {
      encoded = encoded.line(address)
    }
    if (phone) {
      encoded = encoded.line(phone)
    }

    encoded = encoded
      .line(line)
      .align('left')
      .line(`Nota  : #${String(transaction.id || '').slice(-5)}`)
      .line(`Tgl   : ${tglStr} ${jamStr}`)

    // Member ditaruh di bagian header metadata
    if (transaction.memberName) {
      encoded = encoded.line(`Member: ${transaction.memberName}`)
    }

    encoded = encoded.line(line)

    // Rincian Items
    const items = transaction.items || []
    items.forEach(item => {
      const name = String(item.name || item.productName || 'BARANG').toUpperCase()
      const qty = item.qty || item.cartQty || 1
      const price = (Number(item.price || item.price_sell || 0) + Number(item.extraCharge || 0))
      const subtotalVal = item.subtotal || (price * qty)

      const detail = `${qty}x${price.toLocaleString('id-ID')}`
      const subtotal = subtotalVal.toLocaleString('id-ID')

      encoded = encoded.line(name)
      encoded = encoded.line(detail.padEnd(detailWidth) + subtotal.padStart(subtotalWidth))
    })

    // Block Total & Pembayaran Rata Kanan
    encoded = encoded
      .line(line)
      .align('right')
      .line(`TOTAL:   ${(transaction.total || 0).toLocaleString('id-ID').padStart(subtotalWidth)}`)

    const paymentMethod = String(transaction.paymentMethod || '').toLowerCase()
    if (paymentMethod === 'cash' || paymentMethod === 'tunai') {
      const bayar = transaction.amountPaid || transaction.paidAmount || transaction.total || 0
      const kembali = transaction.change !== undefined ? transaction.change : Math.max(0, bayar - (transaction.total || 0))

      encoded = encoded
        .line(`BAYAR:   ${bayar.toLocaleString('id-ID').padStart(subtotalWidth)}`)
        .line(`KEMBALI: ${kembali.toLocaleString('id-ID').padStart(subtotalWidth)}`)
    } else {
      encoded = encoded.line(`METODE:  ${paymentMethod.toUpperCase().padStart(subtotalWidth)}`)
    }

    // Footer Nota Rata Tengah
    encoded = encoded
      .align('center')
      .newline()

    const footerText = settings.receiptFooter || settings.footerNote || 'Terima kasih telah berbelanja!'
    footerText.split('\n').forEach(fLine => {
      if (fLine.trim()) encoded = encoded.line(fLine.trim())
    })

    // Nama Kasir ditaruh paling bawah (di bawah ucapan terima kasih)
    const operator = transaction.operatorName || transaction.kasir || 'Admin'
    encoded = encoded.line(`Kasir: ${operator}`)

    const fullData = encoded
      .newline()
      .newline()
      .cut()
      .encode()

    // Kirim chunk data via Bluetooth
    const chunkSize = 20
    for (let i = 0; i < fullData.length; i += chunkSize) {
      const chunk = fullData.slice(i, i + chunkSize)
      if (targetChar.properties?.writeWithoutResponse) {
        await targetChar.writeValueWithoutResponse(chunk)
      } else if (targetChar.properties?.write) {
        await targetChar.writeValue(chunk)
      }
      await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 30))
    }
  },

  // 2. Unduh Nota PDF
  async downloadPdf(transaction) {
    const settings = await settingsRepo.getSettings()
    const is58mm = settings.printerPaperSize !== '80mm'
    const items = transaction.items || []
    
    const paperWidth = is58mm ? 58 : 80
    const estimatedHeight = 95 + (items.length * 12)

    const doc = new jsPDF({
      orientation: 'portrait',
      unit: 'mm',
      format: [paperWidth, estimatedHeight]
    })

    let y = 7
    doc.setFont('courier', 'bold')
    doc.setFontSize(11)
    doc.text((settings.storeName || 'SINAR PAGI').toUpperCase(), paperWidth / 2, y, { align: 'center' })

    doc.setFont('courier', 'normal')
    doc.setFontSize(8)
    if (settings.storeAddress) {
      y += 4
      doc.text(settings.storeAddress, paperWidth / 2, y, { align: 'center' })
    }
    if (settings.storePhone) {
      y += 3.5
      doc.text(`Telp: ${settings.storePhone}`, paperWidth / 2, y, { align: 'center' })
    }

    y += 4
    doc.text('-'.repeat(is58mm ? 32 : 45), paperWidth / 2, y, { align: 'center' })

    y += 4
    doc.text(`Nota  : #${String(transaction.id || '').slice(-5)}`, 4, y)
    y += 3.5
    doc.text(`Tgl   : ${dayjs(transaction.date || new Date()).format('DD/MM/YYYY HH:mm')}`, 4, y)

    // Member ditaruh di header
    if (transaction.memberName) {
      y += 3.5
      doc.text(`Member: ${transaction.memberName}`, 4, y)
    }

    y += 4
    doc.text('-'.repeat(is58mm ? 32 : 45), paperWidth / 2, y, { align: 'center' })

    items.forEach(item => {
      y += 4
      const name = String(item.name || item.productName || 'BARANG').toUpperCase()
      doc.text(name, 4, y)
      
      y += 3.5
      const qty = item.qty || item.cartQty || 1
      const price = Number(item.price || item.price_sell || 0) + Number(item.extraCharge || 0)
      const subtotalVal = item.subtotal || (price * qty)

      const qtyPrice = `${qty}x${price.toLocaleString('id-ID')}`
      const subtotal = subtotalVal.toLocaleString('id-ID')
      doc.text(qtyPrice, 4, y)
      doc.text(subtotal, paperWidth - 4, y, { align: 'right' })
    })

    y += 4
    doc.text('-'.repeat(is58mm ? 32 : 45), paperWidth / 2, y, { align: 'center' })

    y += 4
    doc.setFont('courier', 'bold')
    doc.text('TOTAL :', 4, y)
    doc.text(`Rp ${(transaction.total || 0).toLocaleString('id-ID')}`, paperWidth - 4, y, { align: 'right' })

    const isCash = String(transaction.paymentMethod || '').toLowerCase() === 'cash' || String(transaction.paymentMethod || '').toLowerCase() === 'tunai'
    if (isCash) {
      const bayar = transaction.amountPaid || transaction.paidAmount || transaction.total || 0
      const kembali = transaction.change !== undefined ? transaction.change : Math.max(0, bayar - (transaction.total || 0))
      
      y += 3.5
      doc.setFont('courier', 'normal')
      doc.text('BAYAR :', 4, y)
      doc.text(`Rp ${bayar.toLocaleString('id-ID')}`, paperWidth - 4, y, { align: 'right' })
      
      y += 3.5
      doc.text('KEMBALI :', 4, y)
      doc.text(`Rp ${kembali.toLocaleString('id-ID')}`, paperWidth - 4, y, { align: 'right' })
    } else {
      y += 3.5
      doc.setFont('courier', 'normal')
      doc.text('METODE :', 4, y)
      doc.text(String(transaction.paymentMethod || 'NON-TUNAI').toUpperCase(), paperWidth - 4, y, { align: 'right' })
    }

    y += 6
    doc.setFont('courier', 'normal')
    doc.setFontSize(7)
    doc.text(settings.receiptFooter || settings.footerNote || 'Terima kasih telah berbelanja!', paperWidth / 2, y, { align: 'center' })

    // Nama Kasir ditaruh di paling bawah (di bawah ucapan terima kasih)
    y += 3.5
    const operator = transaction.operatorName || transaction.kasir || 'Admin'
    doc.text(`Kasir: ${operator}`, paperWidth / 2, y, { align: 'center' })

    doc.save(`Nota_${String(transaction.id || '').slice(-5)}_${dayjs().format('YYYYMMDD_HHmm')}.pdf`)
  }
}

===== src/services/productsDigitalService.js =====
// src/services/productsDigitalService.js
import { db } from '../db/index.js'
import { financeRepo } from '../db/repositories/financeRepository.js'
import dayjs from 'dayjs'

export const productsDigitalService = {
  async create(data) {
    const now = dayjs().toISOString()
    const id = crypto.randomUUID()
    const nominal = Number(data.nominal) || 0
    const adminFee = Number(data.adminFee) || 0
    const isTopup = data.type === 'topup'
    const isCashAdmin = data.adminPaymentMethod === 'cash'
    const memberId = data.memberId || null
    const memberName = data.memberName || null

    const doc = {
      id,
      date: now,
      updatedAt: now,
      type: data.type,
      provider: data.provider?.toUpperCase().trim(),
      nominal,
      adminFee,
      adminPaymentMethod: data.adminPaymentMethod,
      totalReceived: isTopup && isCashAdmin ? nominal + adminFee : nominal,
      profit: adminFee,
      memberId,
      memberName,
      synced: 0
    }

    const result = await db.digital_transactions.add(doc)

    if (isTopup) {
      const cashIn = nominal + (isCashAdmin ? adminFee : 0)
      if (cashIn > 0) {
        await financeRepo.addMutation({
          accountId: 'acc_cash',
          type: 'IN',
          amount: cashIn,
          category: 'Transaksi Digital',
          note: `Top Up ${doc.provider} (Uang Diterima)`,
          refId: id
        })
      }

      const bankOut = isCashAdmin ? nominal : Math.max(0, nominal - adminFee)
      await financeRepo.addMutation({
        accountId: 'acc_bank',
        type: 'OUT',
        amount: bankOut,
        category: 'Transaksi Digital',
        note: `Modal Top Up ${doc.provider}`,
        refId: id
      })
    } else {
      const cashOut = nominal - (isCashAdmin ? adminFee : 0)
      if (cashOut > 0) {
        await financeRepo.addMutation({
          accountId: 'acc_cash',
          type: 'OUT',
          amount: cashOut,
          category: 'Transaksi Digital',
          note: `Tarik Tunai ${doc.provider} (Uang Diserahkan)`,
          refId: id
        })
      }
      
      const bankIn = isCashAdmin ? nominal : nominal + adminFee
      await financeRepo.addMutation({
        accountId: 'acc_bank',
        type: 'IN',
        amount: bankIn,
        category: 'Transaksi Digital',
        note: `Masuk Saldo Tarik Tunai ${doc.provider}`,
        refId: id
      })
    }

    // Jika ada member yang dipilih, update poin dan total belanja
    if (memberId) {
      const currentMember = await db.members.get(memberId)
      if (currentMember) {
        const totalOmzetDigital = nominal + adminFee
        const earnedPoints = Math.floor(adminFee * 0.01) // 1% dari profit admin fee
        const updatedPoints = (currentMember.points || 0) + earnedPoints
        const updatedSpending = (currentMember.total_spending || 0) + totalOmzetDigital

        await db.members.update(memberId, {
          points: updatedPoints,
          total_spending: updatedSpending,
          updatedAt: now,
          synced: 0
        })
      }
    }

    return result
  },

  async getUnsynced() {
    return await db.digital_transactions.where('synced').equals(0).toArray()
  },

  async markSynced(ids) {
    return await db.digital_transactions.bulkUpdate(
      ids.map(id => ({ key: id, changes: { synced: 1 } }))
    )
  },

  async getAll() {
    return await db.digital_transactions.orderBy('date').reverse().toArray()
  }
}

===== src/services/reportService.js =====
// src/services/reportService.js
import { db } from '../db/index.js'
import dayjs from 'dayjs'
import * as XLSX from 'xlsx'

export const reportService = {
  async getReportData() {
    const [trxs, items, digitals, prods] = await Promise.all([
      db.transactions.toArray(),
      db.transaction_items.toArray(),
      db.digital_transactions.toArray(),
      db.products.toArray()
    ])

    return {
      rawTransactions: trxs,
      rawTransactionItems: items,
      rawDigitalTransactions: digitals,
      productsMap: new Map(prods.map(p => [p.id, p]))
    }
  },

  exportToExcel(filteredTransactions, filteredDigitalTransactions, selectedPeriod) {
    const dataToExport = []

    // Data Transaksi Kasir
    filteredTransactions.forEach(t => {
      dataToExport.push({
        Tanggal: dayjs(t.date).format('YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm'),
        Tipe: 'Penjualan Barang',
        'ID Nota': t.id.slice(0, 8),
        'Metode Bayar': t.paymentMethod,
        'Total Omzet (Rp)': t.total,
        'Status Bayar': t.status
      })
    })

    // Data Transaksi Digital
    filteredDigitalTransactions.forEach(d => {
      dataToExport.push({
        Tanggal: dayjs(d.date).format('YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm'),
        Tipe: `Digital (${d.provider || 'Topup/Tarik'})`,
        'ID Nota': d.id.slice(0, 8),
        'Metode Bayar': d.adminPaymentMethod === 'cash' ? 'Tunai' : 'Potong Saldo',
        'Total Omzet (Rp)': d.nominal + (d.adminFee || 0),
        'Status Bayar': 'Lunas'
      })
    })

    const worksheet = XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(dataToExport)
    const workbook = XLSX.utils.book_new()
    XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(workbook, worksheet, 'Laporan Penjualan')

    const fileName = `Laporan_SinarPagi_${selectedPeriod}_${dayjs().format('YYYYMMDD')}.xlsx`
    XLSX.writeFile(workbook, fileName)
  }
}

===== src/services/expenseService.js =====
import { expenseRepository } from '../db/repositories/expenseRepository.js'
import { financeRepo } from '../db/repositories/financeRepository.js'

export const expenseService = {
  async getAllExpenses() {
    return await expenseRepository.getAll()
  },

  async addExpense(data) {
    // 1. Simpan data pengeluaran
    const newItem = await expenseRepository.create(data)

    // 2. Potong kas / bank di sistem Keuangan (OUT)
    const accountId = data.paymentMethod === 'QRIS' ? 'acc_bank' : 'acc_cash'
    const accLabel = accountId === 'acc_bank' ? 'Bank / QRIS' : 'Kas Laci'

    await financeRepo.addMutation({
      accountId,
      type: 'OUT',
      amount: newItem.amount,
      category: `Operasional: ${newItem.category}`,
      note: `${newItem.note || newItem.category} (via ${accLabel})`,
      refId: newItem.id
    })

    return newItem
  },

  async deleteExpense(expense) {
    // 1. Hapus data pengeluaran
    await expenseRepository.delete(expense.id)

    // 2. Pengembalian dana mutasi kas / bank (IN)
    const accountId = expense.paymentMethod === 'QRIS' ? 'acc_bank' : 'acc_cash'

    await financeRepo.addMutation({
      accountId,
      type: 'IN',
      amount: expense.amount,
      category: 'Pembatalan Pengeluaran',
      note: `Batal Pengeluaran ${expense.category}: ${expense.note || ''}`,
      refId: expense.id
    })
  }
}

===== src/services/memberService.js =====
import { memberRepo } from '../db/repositories/memberRepository.js'
import { financeRepo } from '../db/repositories/financeRepository.js'
import dayjs from 'dayjs'

export const memberService = {
  async payDebt({ memberId, amount, paymentMethod = 'CASH' }) {
    const now = dayjs().toISOString()
    const member = await memberRepo.getById(memberId)
    if (!member) throw new Error('Member tidak ditemukan')

    const payAmount = Number(amount) || 0
    if (payAmount <= 0) throw new Error('Nominal pembayaran harus lebih besar dari 0')

    const newDebt = Math.max(0, (member.debt || 0) - payAmount)

    // 1. Catat riwayat cicilan utang
    const paymentId = crypto.randomUUID()
    await memberRepo.addDebtPayment({
      id: paymentId,
      memberId,
      transactionId: null,
      date: now,
      amount: payAmount,
      paymentMethod
    })

    // 2. Update sisa utang member
    await memberRepo.update(memberId, { debt: newDebt })

    // 3. Tentukan akun kas tujuan: CASH ke acc_cash, QRIS/BANK ke acc_bank
    const targetAccount = (paymentMethod === 'QRIS' || paymentMethod === 'BANK') ? 'acc_bank' : 'acc_cash'
    const accountLabel = targetAccount === 'acc_bank' ? 'QRIS / Bank' : 'Tunai'

    await financeRepo.addMutation({
      accountId: targetAccount,
      type: 'IN',
      amount: payAmount,
      category: 'Pembayaran Utang Member',
      note: `Cicilan/Pelunasan utang a.n ${member.name} (${accountLabel})`,
      refId: paymentId
    })

    return newDebt
  }
}

===== src/services/kasirService.js =====
// src/services/kasirService.js
import { db } from '../db/index.js'
import dayjs from 'dayjs'
import { financeRepo } from '../db/repositories/financeRepository.js'

export const kasirService = {
  async checkout({ cart, member, paymentMethod, operator }) {
    const now = dayjs().toISOString()
    const id = crypto.randomUUID()
    const total = cart.reduce((s, c) => s + (c.price_sell * c.cartQty), 0)
    const isTempo = paymentMethod === 'TEMPO'

    // GUARD 1: Validasi metode TEMPO wajib memilih member
    if (isTempo && !member) throw new Error('Member wajib dipilih untuk metode pembayaran TEMPO!')
    
    // GUARD 2: Validasi keranjang tidak boleh kosong
    if (!cart || cart.length === 0) throw new Error('Keranjang belanja masih kosong!')

    let totalProfit = 0
    const stockWarnings = []

    // 1. Simpan header transaksi
    await db.transactions.add({
      id, 
      date: now, 
      total, 
      memberId: member?.id || null,
      operatorId: operator?.id || null,
      operatorName: operator?.name || 'Kasir',
      paymentMethod, 
      amountPaid: isTempo ? 0 : total, 
      paidAmount: isTempo ? 0 : total,
      remaining: isTempo ? total : 0, 
      status: isTempo ? 'unpaid' : 'paid',
      dueDate: isTempo ? dayjs().add(7, 'day').toISOString() : null,
      updatedAt: now, 
      synced: 0
    })

    // 2. Pencatatan mutasi kas/bank
    if (paymentMethod === 'CASH') {
      await financeRepo.addMutation({
        accountId: 'acc_cash',
        type: 'IN',
        amount: total,
        category: 'Penjualan Toko',
        note: `Transaksi Kasir (${operator?.name || 'Kasir'}) #${id.slice(0, 8)}`,
        refId: id
      })
    } else if (paymentMethod === 'QRIS') {
      await financeRepo.addMutation({
        accountId: 'acc_bank',
        type: 'IN',
        amount: total,
        category: 'Penjualan Toko',
        note: `Transaksi QRIS (${operator?.name || 'Kasir'}) #${id.slice(0, 8)}`,
        refId: id
      })
    }

    // 3. Olah item transaksi & pemotongan stok (OPSI B: Boleh Minus)
    for (const c of cart) {
      const modal = c.price_modal || 0
      const itemProfit = (c.price_sell - modal) * c.cartQty
      totalProfit += Math.max(0, itemProfit)

      // Simpan item transaksi
      await db.transaction_items.add({
        id: crypto.randomUUID(), 
        transactionId: id, 
        productId: c.id,
        serviceId: c.isServed ? 'SERVICE_SEDUH' : null, 
        name: c.name, 
        qty: c.cartQty, 
        price: c.price_sell,
        subtotal: c.price_sell * c.cartQty, 
        updatedAt: now, 
        synced: 0
      })

      // Cek ketersediaan produk di database
      const product = await db.products.get(c.id)
      if (product) {
        const qtyDipotong = c.packageInfo ? (c.packageInfo.qty_pcs * c.cartQty) : c.cartQty
        const currentQty = product.qty || 0
        const newQty = currentQty - qtyDipotong

        // GUARD OPSI B: Catat peringatan jika stok menjadi minus (< 0)
        if (newQty < 0) {
          stockWarnings.push(`${product.name} (Sisa Stok: ${newQty})`)
        }

        await db.products.update(c.id, { 
          qty: newQty, 
          updatedAt: now, 
          synced: 0 
        })
      }
    }

    // 4. Update data member (poin, spending, & piutang) jika ada
    if (member) {
      const currentMember = await db.members.get(member.id)
      if (currentMember) {
        const earnedPoints = Math.floor(totalProfit * 0.01)
        const updatedPoints = (currentMember.points || 0) + earnedPoints
        const updatedSpending = (currentMember.total_spending || 0) + total
        const updatedDebt = isTempo ? (currentMember.debt || 0) + total : (currentMember.debt || 0)

        await db.members.update(member.id, {
          points: updatedPoints,
          total_spending: updatedSpending,
          debt: updatedDebt,
          updatedAt: now,
          synced: 0
        })
      }
    }

    return { id, total, stockWarnings }
  },

  async getInitialData() {
    const [products, members] = await Promise.all([
      db.products.toArray(),
      db.members.toArray()
    ])
    return { products, members }
  }
}

import { productRepo } from '../db/repositories/productRepository.js'

export const productService = {
  async createProduct(formData, modalPerPcs) {
    const exist = formData.code? await productRepo.getByCode(formData.code) : null
    if(exist) throw new Error(`Kode sudah dipakai ${exist.name}`)

  }
}
===== src/services/syncService.js =====
// src/services/syncService.js
import { reactive } from 'vue'
import { db } from '../db'
import { firestore } from './firebase'
import { collection, doc, writeBatch, getDocs, query, where } from 'firebase/firestore'
import dayjs from 'dayjs'
import Big from 'big.js'
import { cloudinaryService } from './cloudinaryService.js'

const TABLES_TO_SYNC = [
  'products',
  'product_packages',
  'categories',
  'members',
  'services',
  'units',
  'operators',
  'cash_accounts',
  'settings'
]

const TRANSACTION_TABLES = [
  'transactions',
  'transaction_items',
  'expenses',
  'digital_transactions',
  'debt_payments',
  'shopping_list',
  'cash_mutations'
]

let isSyncing = false
let intervalId = null
let isPullingFromCloud = false

export function getIsPullingFromCloud() { return isPullingFromCloud }

export const syncStatus = reactive({
  isOnline: navigator.onLine,
  isSyncing: false,
  unsyncedCount: 0,
  lastSyncedAt: null,
  isAutoSyncEnabled: false
})

async function updateUnsyncedCount() {
  if (!db) return
  try {
    let total = 0
    for (const t of [...TABLES_TO_SYNC, ...TRANSACTION_TABLES]) {
      try {
        if (!db[t]) continue
        const c = await db[t].where('synced').equals(0).count().catch(()=>0)
        total += c
        await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r, 20))
      } catch(e) {
        if (e.name !== 'AbortError') console.warn('[Sync] count', t, e)
      }
    }
    syncStatus.unsyncedCount = total
  } catch (e) {
    if (e.name !== 'AbortError') console.warn('[Sync] Count check error:', e)
  }
}

export async function pullAllFromCloud() {
  if (!navigator.onLine || isPullingFromCloud || !db || !firestore) return
  isPullingFromCloud = true
  syncStatus.isSyncing = true
  try {
    console.log('[Sync] DB kosong terdeteksi, narik master dari cloud...')
    for (const tableName of TABLES_TO_SYNC) {
      if (!db[tableName]) continue
      let snap
      try {
        snap = await getDocs(collection(firestore, tableName))
      } catch (e) {
        if (e.name === 'AbortError' || e.message?.includes('aborted')) {
          console.warn(`[Sync] getDocs ${tableName} ke-abort, skip`)
          continue
        }
        throw e
      }
      if (snap.empty) continue
      const data = snap.docs.map(d => ({
        ...d.data(),
        id: d.id,
        synced: 1
      }))
      if (data.length) {
        try {
          await db[tableName].bulkPut(data)
          console.log(`[Pull] ${tableName}: ${data.length}`)
        } catch (e) {
          if (e.name === 'AbortError') {
            console.warn(`[Sync] bulkPut ${tableName} ke-abort`)
            continue
          }
          throw e
        }
      }
      await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 100))
    }
    localStorage.setItem('lastPullAt', new Date().toISOString())
    syncStatus.lastSyncedAt = dayjs().format('HH:mm')
    await updateUnsyncedCount()
  } finally {
    isPullingFromCloud = false
    syncStatus.isSyncing = false
  }
}

export async function pullDeltaFromCloud() {
  if (!navigator.onLine || isPullingFromCloud) return
  const lastPull = localStorage.getItem('lastPullAt')
  if (!lastPull) return pullAllFromCloud()
  isPullingFromCloud = true
  try {
    for (const tableName of TABLES_TO_SYNC) {
      try {
        const q = query(collection(firestore, tableName), where('updatedAt', '>', lastPull))
        let snap
        try {
          snap = await getDocs(q)
        } catch(e) {
          if (e.name === 'AbortError') continue
          throw e
        }
        if (snap.empty) continue
        const dirtyIds = await db[tableName].where('synced').equals(0).primaryKeys().catch(()=>[])
        const incoming = snap.docs.map(d=>({...d.data(), id: d.id, synced: 1})).filter(i=>!dirtyIds.includes(i.id))
        if (incoming.length) await db[tableName].bulkPut(incoming)
        await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r, 50))
      } catch(e) {
        if (e.name !== 'AbortError') console.warn('[Sync] delta', tableName, e)
      }
    }
    localStorage.setItem('lastPullAt', new Date().toISOString())
  } finally {
    isPullingFromCloud = false
  }
}

async function recalculateMemberDebt(memberId) {
  if (!memberId || !db?.transactions || !db?.members || !db?.debt_payments) return
  try {
    const unpaid = await db.transactions.where('memberId').equals(memberId).filter(t => (t.remaining || 0) > 0 && t.status !== 'void' && t.status !== 'CANCELLED' && !t.deleted).toArray()
    const totalTrxDebt = unpaid.reduce((acc, t) => Big(acc).plus(t.remaining || 0).toString(), '0')
    const payments = await db.debt_payments.where('memberId').equals(memberId).toArray()
    const validPayments = payments.filter(p => !p.deleted)
    const totalPaid = validPayments.reduce((acc, p) => Big(acc).plus(p.amount || 0).toString(), '0')
    const finalDebt = Math.max(0, Number(Big(totalTrxDebt).minus(totalPaid)))
    await db.members.update(memberId, { debt: finalDebt })
  } catch (e) {
    console.warn('[Sync] recalc debt fail', e)
  }
}

export const syncRealtime = {
  toggleAutoSync(enabled) {
    syncStatus.isAutoSyncEnabled = enabled
    localStorage.setItem('auto_sync_enabled', enabled ? 'true' : 'false')
    if (!enabled) this.stopRealtime()
  },
  async pushLocalToCloud() {
    syncStatus.isOnline = navigator.onLine
    if (!navigator.onLine || isSyncing || !db || !firestore) {
      await updateUnsyncedCount()
      return
    }
    isSyncing = true
    syncStatus.isSyncing = true
    try {
      // Upload gambar pending ke Cloudinary terlebih dahulu sebelum push ke Firestore[span_4](start_span)[span_4](end_span)[span_5](start_span)[span_5](end_span)
      try {
        await cloudinaryService.uploadPendingImages()
      } catch (imgErr) {
        console.warn('[Sync] Gagal upload pending images ke Cloudinary:', imgErr?.message)
      }

      for (const tableName of [...TABLES_TO_SYNC, ...TRANSACTION_TABLES]) {
        if (!db[tableName]) continue
        const unsynced = await db[tableName].where('synced').equals(0).toArray()
        if (!unsynced.length) continue
        for (let i = 0; i < unsynced.length; i += 400) {
          const batch = writeBatch(firestore)
          const chunk = unsynced.slice(i, i + 400)
          chunk.forEach(item => {
            const { synced, imageLocal, ...clean } = item
            batch.set(doc(firestore, tableName, item.id), clean, { merge: true })
          })
          await batch.commit()
          await db[tableName].bulkUpdate(chunk.map(c => ({ key: c.id, changes: { synced: 1 } })))
        }
      }
      syncStatus.lastSyncedAt = dayjs().format('HH:mm')
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn('[Sync] Push manual gagal:', e?.message)
    } finally {
      isSyncing = false
      syncStatus.isSyncing = false
      await updateUnsyncedCount()
    }
  },
  initRealtimePull() { this.stopRealtime() },
  stopRealtime() { if (intervalId) { clearInterval(intervalId); intervalId = null } },
  async init() {
    try {
      if (!firestore || !db) return
      const updateOnlineStatus = () => { syncStatus.isOnline = navigator.onLine }
      window.addEventListener('online', updateOnlineStatus)
      window.addEventListener('offline', updateOnlineStatus)
      this.stopRealtime()
      const prodCount = await db.products.where('deleted').notEqual(true).count().catch(()=>db.products.count().catch(()=>0))
      if (prodCount === 0 && navigator.onLine) {
        await pullAllFromCloud()
      } else {
        if (navigator.onLine) pullDeltaFromCloud()
        await updateUnsyncedCount()
      }
    } catch (e) {
      if (e.name !== 'AbortError') console.error('[Sync] Init error:', e?.message, e)
    }
  }
}

export const syncService = syncRealtime
export default syncRealtime

===== src/services/cloudinaryService.js =====

// src/services/cloudinaryService.js - FINAL OFFLINE-FIRST
import { db } from '../db'

const CLOUD_NAME = import.meta.env.VITE_CLOUDINARY_CLOUD_NAME || 'vezqidog'
const UPLOAD_PRESET = import.meta.env.VITE_CLOUDINARY_PRESET || 'sinarpagi-products'

export const cloudinaryService = {
  async uploadPendingImages() {
    if (!navigator.onLine) {
      console.log('[Cloudinary] Offline, skip upload')
      return { uploaded: 0 }
    }
    const pending = await db.products.where('imageSynced').equals(0).toArray()
    if (!pending.length) return { uploaded: 0 }
    
    let count = 0
    for (const p of pending) {
      if (!p.imageLocal) {
        // ga ada blob lokal, skip tapi tandain synced biar ga loop
        await db.products.update(p.id, { imageSynced: 1 })
        continue
      }
      try {
        console.log(`[Cloudinary] Uploading ${p.name}...`)
        const form = new FormData()
        form.append('file', p.imageLocal)
        form.append('upload_preset', UPLOAD_PRESET)
        if (p.imagePublicId) form.append('public_id', p.imagePublicId)
        else form.append('public_id', `sinar-pagi/products/${p.id}`)

        const res = await fetch(`https://api.cloudinary.com/v1_1/${CLOUD_NAME}/image/upload`, {
          method: 'POST',
          body: form
        })
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error(await res.text())
        const data = await res.json()
        
        // Simpen URL, hapus blob lokal biar irit IndexedDB
        await db.products.update(p.id, {
          imageUrl: data.secure_url,
          imagePublicId: data.public_id,
          imageSynced: 1,
          synced: 0, // biar URL nya ke-push ke Firestore
          updatedAt: new Date().toISOString()
        })
        // Hapus blob setelah sukses upload biar ga bengkak
        try {
          await db.products.update(p.id, { imageLocal: undefined })
        } catch {}
        count++
        console.log(`[Cloudinary] ✅ ${p.name} -> ${data.secure_url}`)
        await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r, 300))
      } catch (e) {
        console.warn('[Cloudinary] Upload fail', p.name, e.message)
      }
    }
    return { uploaded: count }
  },

  getThumbUrl(publicId, width = 300) {
    if (!publicId) return null
    return `https://res.cloudinary.com/${CLOUD_NAME}/image/upload/w_${width},f_auto,q_auto/${publicId}`
  },

  getFullUrl(publicId) {
    if (!publicId) return null
    return `https://res.cloudinary.com/${CLOUD_NAME}/image/upload/f_auto,q_auto/${publicId}`
  }
}

===== src/services/salesHistoryService.js =====
// src/services/salesHistoryService.js
import { db } from '../db/index.js'
import { transactionRepo } from '../db/repositories/transactionRepository.js'
import { memberRepo } from '../db/repositories/memberRepository.js'
import { productRepo } from '../db/repositories/productRepository.js'
import { financeRepo } from '../db/repositories/financeRepository.js'
import { productsDigitalService } from './productsDigitalService.js'
import dayjs from 'dayjs'

export const salesHistoryService = {
  async getCombinedHistory() {
    const [transactions, members, products, allItems, digitalTransactions] = await Promise.all([
      transactionRepo.getAll(),
      memberRepo.getAll(),
      productRepo.getAll(),
      transactionRepo.getAllItems(),
      productsDigitalService.getAll()
    ])

    const memberMap = new Map(members.map(m => [m.id, m.name]))
    const productMap = new Map(products.map(p => [p.id, p.name]))

    const totalPiutang = members.reduce((sum, m) => sum + (Number(m.debt) || 0), 0)

    const formattedBarang = transactions.map(t => {
      const items = allItems
        .filter(item => item.transactionId === t.id)
        .map(item => ({
          ...item,
          productName: item.name || productMap.get(item.productId) || 'Produk Dihapus'
        }))

      return {
        ...t,
        trxType: 'BARANG',
        memberName: t.memberId ? (memberMap.get(t.memberId) || 'Member Tidak Ditemukan') : 'Umum / Non-Member',
        items
      }
    })

    const formattedDigital = digitalTransactions.map(d => {
      const isTopup = d.type === 'topup'
      const isCashAdmin = d.adminPaymentMethod === 'cash'
      
      let kasImpact = 0
      if (isTopup) {
        kasImpact = d.nominal + (isCashAdmin ? d.adminFee : 0)
      } else {
        kasImpact = -(d.nominal - (isCashAdmin ? d.adminFee : 0))
      }

      return {
        id: d.id,
        date: d.date,
        trxType: 'DIGITAL',
        digitalType: d.type,
        provider: d.provider,
        nominal: d.nominal,
        adminFee: d.adminFee,
        adminPaymentMethod: d.adminPaymentMethod,
        total: kasImpact,
        profit: d.profit || d.adminFee,
        status: d.status || 'SUCCESS',
        paymentMethod: isCashAdmin ? 'CASH' : 'DIGITAL_SALDO',
        memberName: `Digital: ${d.provider}`,
        items: [
          {
            id: d.id,
            productName: `${isTopup ? 'TOP UP' : 'TARIK TUNAI'} ${d.provider}`,
            qty: 1,
            price: d.nominal,
            subtotal: d.nominal
          }
        ]
      }
    })

    const transactionsList = [...formattedBarang, ...formattedDigital].sort((a, b) => new Date(b.date) - new Date(a.date))

    return {
      transactions: transactionsList,
      totalPiutang
    }
  },

  // FUNGSI EDIT TRANSAKSI BARANG
  async updateTransaction(originalTrx, updatedItems, newPaymentData = {}) {
    const now = dayjs().toISOString()
    const oldItems = await transactionRepo.getItemsByTransactionId(originalTrx.id)

    // Ambil data header transaksi lama secara aman dari repository atau Dexie langsung
    const existingTrxHeader = (await transactionRepo.getById(originalTrx.id)) || (await db.transactions.get(originalTrx.id)) || originalTrx

    // Tentukan metode bayar & member baru dari payload
    const newPaymentMethod = newPaymentData.paymentMethod || existingTrxHeader.paymentMethod || 'CASH'
    const newMemberId = newPaymentData.memberId !== undefined ? newPaymentData.memberId : (existingTrxHeader.memberId || null)

    // 1. Peta stok lama vs stok baru untuk menghitung selisih kuantitas
    const oldQtyMap = new Map()
    oldItems.forEach(i => {
      if (i.productId) oldQtyMap.set(i.productId, (oldQtyMap.get(i.productId) || 0) + (i.qty || 0))
    })

    const newQtyMap = new Map()
    let newTotal = 0

    updatedItems.forEach(i => {
      const subtotal = (Number(i.price) || 0) * (Number(i.qty) || 0)
      newTotal += subtotal
      if (i.productId) {
        newQtyMap.set(i.productId, (newQtyMap.get(i.productId) || 0) + (Number(i.qty) || 0))
      }
    })

    // 2. Adjust stok produk berdasarkan selisih (Delta)
    const allProductIds = new Set([...oldQtyMap.keys(), ...newQtyMap.keys()])
    for (const prodId of allProductIds) {
      const oldQty = oldQtyMap.get(prodId) || 0
      const newQty = newQtyMap.get(prodId) || 0
      const delta = oldQty - newQty // Positif = stok kembali, Negatif = stok berkurang

      if (delta !== 0) {
        const prod = await productRepo.getById(prodId)
        if (prod) {
          await productRepo.update(prodId, {
            qty: (prod.qty || 0) + delta
          })
        }
      }
    }

    // 3. Re-write item transaksi di database
    const existingItemIds = oldItems.map(i => i.id)
    if (existingItemIds.length > 0) {
      await db.transaction_items.bulkDelete(existingItemIds)
    }

    const itemsToInsert = updatedItems.map(i => ({
      id: crypto.randomUUID(),
      transactionId: originalTrx.id,
      productId: i.productId || null,
      serviceId: i.serviceId || null,
      name: i.productName || i.name || 'Barang',
      qty: Number(i.qty) || 1,
      price: Number(i.price) || 0,
      subtotal: (Number(i.price) || 0) * (Number(i.qty) || 0),
      updatedAt: now,
      synced: 0
    }))
    await db.transaction_items.bulkPut(itemsToInsert)

    // 4. Rekonsiliasi Mutasi Keuangan & Arus Kas
    const oldAccId = existingTrxHeader.paymentMethod === 'QRIS' ? 'acc_bank' : existingTrxHeader.paymentMethod === 'CASH' ? 'acc_cash' : null
    const newAccId = newPaymentMethod === 'QRIS' ? 'acc_bank' : newPaymentMethod === 'CASH' ? 'acc_cash' : null

    // Batalkan dampak kas transaksi lama
    if (oldAccId && (existingTrxHeader.total || 0) > 0) {
      await financeRepo.addMutation({
        accountId: oldAccId,
        type: 'OUT',
        amount: existingTrxHeader.total,
        category: 'Koreksi Transaksi',
        note: `Koreksi Edit Nota #${originalTrx.id.slice(0, 8)}`,
        refId: originalTrx.id
      })
    }

    // Masukkan arus kas transaksi baru
    if (newAccId && newTotal > 0) {
      await financeRepo.addMutation({
        accountId: newAccId,
        type: 'IN',
        amount: newTotal,
        category: 'Penjualan Kasir',
        note: `Update Nota #${originalTrx.id.slice(0, 8)}`,
        refId: originalTrx.id
      })
    }

    // 5. Rekonsiliasi Piutang & Total Spending Member
    if (existingTrxHeader.memberId) {
      const oldMember = await memberRepo.getById(existingTrxHeader.memberId)
      if (oldMember) {
        const debtRollback = existingTrxHeader.paymentMethod === 'TEMPO' ? (existingTrxHeader.total || 0) : 0
        await memberRepo.update(existingTrxHeader.memberId, {
          debt: Math.max(0, (oldMember.debt || 0) - debtRollback),
          total_spending: Math.max(0, (oldMember.total_spending || 0) - (existingTrxHeader.total || 0))
        })
      }
    }

    if (newMemberId) {
      const newMember = await memberRepo.getById(newMemberId)
      if (newMember) {
        const addedDebt = newPaymentMethod === 'TEMPO' ? newTotal : 0
        await memberRepo.update(newMemberId, {
          debt: (newMember.debt || 0) + addedDebt,
          total_spending: (newMember.total_spending || 0) + newTotal
        })
      }
    }

    // 6. Update header transaksi
    const newAmountPaid = newPaymentMethod === 'TEMPO' ? 0 : (newPaymentData.amountPaid !== undefined ? newPaymentData.amountPaid : newTotal)
    const newRemaining = newPaymentMethod === 'TEMPO' ? newTotal : 0

    await db.transactions.update(originalTrx.id, {
      total: newTotal,
      paymentMethod: newPaymentMethod,
      memberId: newMemberId,
      amountPaid: newAmountPaid,
      paidAmount: newAmountPaid,
      remaining: newRemaining,
      updatedAt: now,
      synced: 0
    })

    return { id: originalTrx.id, total: newTotal }
  },

  async voidTransaction(transaction) {
    const now = dayjs().toISOString()

    if (transaction.trxType === 'BARANG') {
      if (transaction.status === 'void') throw new Error('Transaksi ini sudah dibatalkan.')

      const items = await transactionRepo.getItemsByTransactionId(transaction.id)
      for (const item of items) {
        if (item.productId) {
          const product = await productRepo.getById(item.productId)
          if (product) {
            await productRepo.update(item.productId, {
              qty: (product.qty || 0) + (item.qty || 0)
            })
          }
        }
      }

      if (transaction.paymentMethod === 'CASH' && transaction.total > 0) {
        await financeRepo.addMutation({
          accountId: 'acc_cash',
          type: 'OUT',
          amount: transaction.total,
          category: 'Pembatalan Transaksi',
          note: `Void Transaksi Kasir #${transaction.id.slice(0, 8)}`,
          refId: transaction.id
        })
      } else if (transaction.paymentMethod === 'QRIS' && transaction.total > 0) {
        await financeRepo.addMutation({
          accountId: 'acc_bank',
          type: 'OUT',
          amount: transaction.total,
          category: 'Pembatalan Transaksi',
          note: `Void Transaksi QRIS #${transaction.id.slice(0, 8)}`,
          refId: transaction.id
        })
      }

      if (transaction.memberId) {
        const member = await memberRepo.getById(transaction.memberId)
        if (member) {
          const newSpending = Math.max(0, (member.total_spending || 0) - transaction.total)
          const newDebt = transaction.paymentMethod === 'TEMPO' 
            ? Math.max(0, (member.debt || 0) - (transaction.remaining || transaction.total))
            : (member.debt || 0)

          await memberRepo.update(transaction.memberId, {
            total_spending: newSpending,
            debt: newDebt
          })
        }
      }

      await db.transactions.update(transaction.id, {
        status: 'void',
        updatedAt: now,
        synced: 0
      })

    } else if (transaction.trxType === 'DIGITAL') {
      if (transaction.status === 'CANCELLED') throw new Error('Transaksi digital sudah dibatalkan.')

      const isTopup = transaction.digitalType === 'topup'
      const isCashAdmin = transaction.adminPaymentMethod === 'cash'

      if (isTopup) {
        const cashOut = transaction.nominal + (isCashAdmin ? transaction.adminFee : 0)
        if (cashOut > 0) {
          await financeRepo.addMutation({
            accountId: 'acc_cash',
            type: 'OUT',
            amount: cashOut,
            category: 'Pembatalan Transaksi Digital',
            note: `Void Top Up ${transaction.provider}`,
            refId: transaction.id
          })
        }
        const bankIn = isCashAdmin ? transaction.nominal : Math.max(0, transaction.nominal - transaction.adminFee)
        await financeRepo.addMutation({
          accountId: 'acc_bank',
          type: 'IN',
          amount: bankIn,
          category: 'Pembatalan Transaksi Digital',
          note: `Pengembalian Modal Top Up ${transaction.provider}`,
          refId: transaction.id
        })
      } else {
        const cashIn = transaction.nominal - (isCashAdmin ? transaction.adminFee : 0)
        if (cashIn > 0) {
          await financeRepo.addMutation({
            accountId: 'acc_cash',
            type: 'IN',
            amount: cashIn,
            category: 'Pembatalan Transaksi Digital',
            note: `Void Tarik Tunai ${transaction.provider}`,
            refId: transaction.id
          })
        }
        const bankOut = isCashAdmin ? transaction.nominal : transaction.nominal + transaction.adminFee
        await financeRepo.addMutation({
          accountId: 'acc_bank',
          type: 'OUT',
          amount: bankOut,
          category: 'Pembatalan Transaksi Digital',
          note: `Penarikan Saldo Tarik Tunai ${transaction.provider}`,
          refId: transaction.id
        })
      }

      await db.digital_transactions.update(transaction.id, {
        status: 'CANCELLED',
        profit: 0,
        updatedAt: now,
        synced: 0
      })
    }
  }
}

===== src/schemas/product.schema.js =====
import * as z from 'zod'

export const productSchema = z.object({
  name: z.string().min(3, 'Nama minimal 3 huruf'),
  code: z.string().optional(),
  category: z.string().default('Umum'),
  unit: z.string().min(1, 'Satuan wajib dipilih'), // Validasi wajib diisi
  purchasePackName: z.string().optional(),
  packPrice: z.coerce.number().optional().default(0),
  packQty: z.coerce.number().optional().default(0),
  price_modal: z.coerce.number().min(1, 'Modal/pcs wajib'),
  price_sell: z.coerce.number().min(1, 'Harga jual wajib'),
  qty: z.coerce.number().min(0).default(0),
  minStock: z.coerce.number().min(1).default(5)
})

===== src/utils/formatters/currency.js =====
export const toNumber = (v) => Number(String(v?? '').replace(/[^0-9]/g, '')) || 0

export const formatRp = (v) => {
  const n = toNumber(v)
  if (!n) return ''
  return `Rp ${n.toString().replace(/\B(?=(\d{3})+(?!\d))/g, '.')}`
}

export const formatRibuan = (v) => {
  const n = toNumber(v)
  if (!n) return ''
  return n.toString().replace(/\B(?=(\d{3})+(?!\d))/g, '.')
}
===== src/utils/calculator/product.js =====
import Big from 'big.js'

// Helper buat ubah "12.000" atau "Rp 1.250.000" jadi 12000
const toNumber = (v) => {
  if (typeof v === 'number') return v
  if (!v) return 0
  const cleaned = String(v).replace(/[^0-9]/g, '') // hapus titik, koma, Rp
  return Number(cleaned) || 0
}

export const calcModalDariPack = (packPrice, packQty) => {
  const price = toNumber(packPrice)
  const qty = toNumber(packQty)
  if (!price || !qty) return 0
  try {
    return Number(Big(price).div(qty).round(0))
  } catch { return 0 }
}

export const calcMargin = (jual, modal) => {
  const j = toNumber(jual)
  const m = toNumber(modal)
  if (!m || !j) return 0
  try {
    return Number(Big(j).minus(m).div(m).times(100).round(0))
  } catch { return 0 }
}

export const getBarangTipis = (products) =>
  products.filter(p => (p.qty || 0) <= (p.minStock || 5))

// ROUTE GUARD UNTUK SESI OPERATOR (Modul Vue Router 4 Tanpa Callback next)
router.beforeEach((to) => {
  const operatorStore = useOperatorStore()

  // Update judul halaman secara otomatis
  if (to.meta?.title) {
    document.title = `${to.meta.title} - Sinar Pagi POS`
  }

  // Redirect jika belum login dan mencoba mengakses halaman privat
  if (!to.meta.guestOnly && !operatorStore.isLoggedIn) {
    return '/login'
  }

  // Redirect jika sudah login tetapi membuka halaman login/guest
  if (to.meta.guestOnly && operatorStore.isLoggedIn) {
    return '/kasir'
  }
})

===== src/db/repositories/operatorRepository.js =====
// src/db/repositories/operatorRepository.js
import { db } from '../index.js'
import dayjs from 'dayjs'

export const operatorRepo = {
  getAll: async () => {
    const table = db.operators || db.table('operators')
    const list = await table.toArray()
    // Hanya tampilkan data yang belum di-soft-delete
    return list.filter(item => !item.deleted)
  },

  getById: async (id) => {
    const table = db.operators || db.table('operators')
    const item = await table.get(id)
    return item && !item.deleted ? item : null
  },

  create: async (data) => {
    const id = crypto.randomUUID()
    const now = dayjs().toISOString()
    const item = { 
      id, 
      name: data.name,
      role: data.role || 'Kasir',
      pin: data.pin || '',
      deleted: false,
      updatedAt: now, 
      synced: 0 
    }
    const table = db.operators || db.table('operators')
    await table.add(item)
    return id
  },

  update: async (id, data) => {
    const now = dayjs().toISOString()
    const table = db.operators || db.table('operators')
    await table.update(id, { ...data, updatedAt: now, synced: 0 })
  },

  delete: async (id) => {
    const now = dayjs().toISOString()
    const table = db.operators || db.table('operators')
    // Gunakan Soft Delete agar sinkronisasi offline-first berjalan konsisten
    await table.update(id, { deleted: true, updatedAt: now, synced: 0 })
  }
}

===== src/db/repositories/shoppingRepository.js =====
import { db } from '../index.js'
import dayjs from 'dayjs'

export const shoppingRepository = {
  async getAll() {
    const table = db.shopping_list || db.table('shopping_list')
    return await table.toArray()
  },

  async getById(id) {
    const table = db.shopping_list || db.table('shopping_list')
    return await table.get(id)
  },

  async add(item) {
    const id = item.id || crypto.randomUUID()
    const now = dayjs().toISOString()
    const newItem = {
      ...item,
      id,
      isBought: item.isBought ? 1 : 0,
      createdAt: item.createdAt || now,
      updatedAt: now,
      synced: 0
    }
    
    const table = db.shopping_list || db.table('shopping_list')
    await table.put(newItem)
    return newItem
  },

  async addBulk(items) {
    const now = dayjs().toISOString()
    const preparedItems = items.map(item => ({
      ...item,
      id: item.id || crypto.randomUUID(),
      isBought: item.isBought ? 1 : 0,
      createdAt: item.createdAt || now,
      updatedAt: now,
      synced: 0
    }))

    const table = db.shopping_list || db.table('shopping_list')
    await table.bulkPut(preparedItems)
    return preparedItems
  },

  async update(id, changes) {
    const now = dayjs().toISOString()
    const table = db.shopping_list || db.table('shopping_list')
    const updateData = { ...changes, updatedAt: now, synced: 0 }
    if (typeof changes.isBought === 'boolean') {
      updateData.isBought = changes.isBought ? 1 : 0
    }
    await table.update(id, updateData)
  },

  async delete(id) {
    const table = db.shopping_list || db.table('shopping_list')
    await table.delete(id)
  },

  async clearBought() {
    const table = db.shopping_list || db.table('shopping_list')
    const boughtItems = await table.where('isBought').equals(1).toArray()
    const ids = boughtItems.map(i => i.id)
    await table.bulkDelete(ids)
    return ids.length
  }
}
