===== src/db/repositories/transactionRepository.js =====
import { db } from '../index.js'
import dayjs from 'dayjs'

export const transactionRepo = {
  getAll: () => db.transactions.toArray(),
  getById: (id) => db.transactions.get(id),
  getToday: () => db.transactions.where('date').aboveOrEqual(dayjs().startOf('day').toISOString()).toArray(),
  create: (data) => db.transactions.add(data),
  getItemsByTransactionId: (transactionId) => db.transaction_items.where('transactionId').equals(transactionId).toArray(),
  getAllItems: () => db.transaction_items.toArray()
}

===== src/db/repositories/memberRepository.js =====
// src/db/repositories/memberRepository.js
import { db } from '../index.js'
import dayjs from 'dayjs'

export const memberRepo = {
  // Hanya ambil member yang belum di-soft-delete
  getAll: async () => {
    const list = await db.members.toArray()
    return list.filter(m => !m.deleted)
  },

  getById: async (id) => {
    const item = await db.members.get(id)
    return item && !item.deleted ? item : null
  },
  
  create: async (data) => {
    const id = crypto.randomUUID()
    const now = dayjs().toISOString()
    await db.members.add({
      id,
      name: (data.name || '').toUpperCase().trim(),
      phone: (data.phone || '').trim(),
      address: (data.address || '').toUpperCase().trim(),
      total_spending: 0,
      points: 0,
      debt: 0,
      deleted: false,
      updatedAt: now,
      synced: 0
    })
    return id
  },

  update: async (id, data) => {
    const now = dayjs().toISOString()
    const payload = {
      ...data,
      updatedAt: now,
      synced: 0
    }
    if (data.name) payload.name = data.name.toUpperCase().trim()
    if (data.address) payload.address = data.address.toUpperCase().trim()

    await db.members.update(id, payload)
  },

  delete: async (id) => {
    const stringId = String(id)
    const now = dayjs().toISOString()
    
    // Soft Delete di IndexedDB lokal agar status hapus tersinkron dengan aman saat offline/online
    await db.members.update(stringId, {
      deleted: true,
      updatedAt: now,
      synced: 0
    })
  },

  addDebtPayment: async (paymentData) => {
    const now = dayjs().toISOString()
    const id = paymentData.id || crypto.randomUUID()
    await db.debt_payments.add({
      ...paymentData,
      id,
      date: paymentData.date || now,
      updatedAt: now,
      synced: 0
    })
    return id
  }
}

===== src/db/repositories/productRepository.js =====

// src/db/repositories/productRepository.js - FINAL v3 SOFT DELETE + CLOUDINARY OFFLINE-FIRST
import { db } from '../index.js'
import dayjs from 'dayjs'

export const productRepo = {
  getAll: async () => {
    const list = await db.products.toArray()
    return list.filter(p => !p.deleted)
  },
  getById: async (id) => {
    const item = await db.products.get(id)
    return item && !item.deleted ? item : null
  },
  getByCode: async (code) => {
    const item = await db.products.where('code').equals(code).first()
    return item && !item.deleted ? item : null
  },
  create: async (data, imageFile = null) => {
    const id = crypto.randomUUID()
    const now = dayjs().toISOString()
    const doc = {
      ...data,
      id,
      updatedAt: now,
      synced: 0,
      deleted: false,
      imageUrl: null,
      imagePublicId: null,
      imageSynced: imageFile ? 0 : 1,
      imageLocal: imageFile || undefined
    }
    if (imageFile) {
      doc.imagePublicId = `sinar-pagi/products/${id}`
    }
    await db.products.add(doc)
    return id
  },
  update: async (id, data, imageFile = null) => {
    const now = dayjs().toISOString()
    const changes = {
      ...data,
      updatedAt: now,
      synced: 0
    }
    if (imageFile) {
      changes.imageLocal = imageFile
      changes.imageSynced = 0
      const existing = await db.products.get(id)
      changes.imagePublicId = existing?.imagePublicId || `sinar-pagi/products/${id}`
    }
    await db.products.update(id, changes)
  },
  delete: async (id) => {
    // SOFT DELETE - biar 498 di HP jadi 498 juga di cloud
    const now = dayjs().toISOString()
    await db.products.update(id, {
      deleted: true,
      updatedAt: now,
      synced: 0
    })
  },
  hardDelete: (id) => db.products.delete(id)
}

===== src/db/repositories/expenseRepository.js =====
import { db } from '../index.js'
import dayjs from 'dayjs'

export const expenseRepository = {
  async getAll() {
    return await db.expenses.orderBy('date').reverse().toArray()
  },

  async getById(id) {
    return await db.expenses.get(id)
  },

  async create(data) {
    const id = crypto.randomUUID()
    const now = dayjs().toISOString()
    const item = {
      id,
      date: data.date || now,
      category: data.category || 'Lain-lain',
      amount: Number(data.amount) || 0,
      note: data.note || '',
      paymentMethod: data.paymentMethod || 'CASH',
      cashPart: data.paymentMethod === 'CASH' ? Number(data.amount) : 0,
      qrisPart: data.paymentMethod === 'QRIS' ? Number(data.amount) : 0,
      updatedAt: now,
      synced: 0
    }
    await db.expenses.add(item)
    return item
  },

  async update(id, data) {
    const now = dayjs().toISOString()
    await db.expenses.update(id, {
      ...data,
      cashPart: data.paymentMethod === 'CASH' ? Number(data.amount) : 0,
      qrisPart: data.paymentMethod === 'QRIS' ? Number(data.amount) : 0,
      updatedAt: now,
      synced: 0
    })
  },

  async delete(id) {
    await db.expenses.delete(id)
  }
}

===== src/db/repositories/settingsRepository.js =====
// src/db/repositories/settingsRepository.js
import { db } from '../index.js'
import dayjs from 'dayjs'

export const settingsRepo = {
  async getSettings() {
    const table = db.settings || db.table('settings')
    const item = await table.get('app_config')
    if (!item) {
      const now = dayjs().toISOString()
      const defaultConfig = {
        id: 'app_config',
        storeName: 'SINAR PAGI',
        storeAddress: 'CIHELEUT JL. TALAS UJUNG NO. 38',
        storePhone: '089516477760',
        receiptFooter: 'HATUR NUHUN!',
        printerPaperSize: '58mm',
        autoPrintReceipt: false,
        theme: 'dark',
        updatedAt: now,
        synced: 0
      }
      await table.put(defaultConfig)
      return defaultConfig
    }
    return item
  },

  async saveSettings(data) {
    const table = db.settings || db.table('settings')
    const now = dayjs().toISOString()
    await table.put({
      ...data,
      id: 'app_config',
      updatedAt: now,
      synced: 0
    })
  }
}

===== src/db/repositories/categoryRepository.js =====
// src/db/repositories/categoryRepository.js
import { db } from '../index.js'
import dayjs from 'dayjs'

export const categoryRepo = {
  getAll: () => db.categories.toArray(),
  getById: (id) => db.categories.get(id),
  create: async (name) => {
    const id = crypto.randomUUID()
    const now = dayjs().toISOString()
    await db.categories.add({
      id,
      name,
      updatedAt: now,
      synced: 0
    })
    return id
  },
  update: async (id, name) => {
    const now = dayjs().toISOString()
    const oldCategory = await db.categories.get(id)

    if (oldCategory && oldCategory.name !== name) {
      const oldName = oldCategory.name

      // 1. Update nama kategori di tabel categories
      await db.categories.update(id, {
        name,
        updatedAt: now,
        synced: 0
      })

      // 2. Cari semua produk yang menggunakan kategori lama, lalu perbarui ke nama baru
      const products = await db.products.where('category').equals(oldName).toArray()
      for (const p of products) {
        await db.products.update(p.id, {
          category: name,
          updatedAt: now,
          synced: 0
        })
      }
    } else {
      await db.categories.update(id, {
        name,
        updatedAt: now,
        synced: 0
      })
    }
  },
  delete: (id) => db.categories.delete(id)
}

===== src/db/repositories/unitRepository.js =====
// src/db/repositories/unitRepository.js
import { db } from '../index.js'
import dayjs from 'dayjs'

export const unitRepo = {
  getAll: () => (db.units ? db.units.toArray() : db.table('units').toArray()),
  getById: (id) => (db.units ? db.units.get(id) : db.table('units').get(id)),
  create: async (name) => {
    const id = crypto.randomUUID()
    const now = dayjs().toISOString()
    const item = { id, name, updatedAt: now, synced: 0 }
    const table = db.units || db.table('units')
    await table.add(item)
    return id
  },
  update: async (id, name) => {
    const now = dayjs().toISOString()
    const table = db.units || db.table('units')
    await table.update(id, { name, updatedAt: now, synced: 0 })
  },
  delete: (id) => (db.units || db.table('units')).delete(id)
}

===== src/db/repositories/financeRepository.js =====
// src/db/repositories/financeRepository.js
import { db } from '../index.js'
import dayjs from 'dayjs'

export const financeRepo = {
  // Ambil daftar akun kas & bank (saldo digital disatukan ke bank)
  async getAccounts() {
    const table = db.cash_accounts || db.table('cash_accounts')
    let accounts = await table.toArray()
    
    // Inisialisasi akun bawaan jika belum ada
    if (accounts.length === 0) {
      const defaultAccounts = [
        { id: 'acc_cash', name: 'Kas Laci (Tunai)', type: 'CASH', balance: 0, updatedAt: dayjs().toISOString(), synced: 0 },
        { id: 'acc_bank', name: 'Rekening / QRIS / Wallet', type: 'BANK', balance: 0, updatedAt: dayjs().toISOString(), synced: 0 }
      ]
      await table.bulkPut(defaultAccounts)
      accounts = defaultAccounts
    } else {
      // Hapus akun digital lama jika ada
      const digitalAcc = accounts.find(a => a.id === 'acc_digital')
      if (digitalAcc) {
        await table.delete('acc_digital')
        accounts = accounts.filter(a => a.id !== 'acc_digital')
      }
    }
    return accounts
  },

  // Ambil total piutang member saat ini
  async getTotalPiutang() {
    const members = await db.members.toArray()
    return members.reduce((sum, m) => sum + (Number(m.debt) || 0), 0)
  },

  // Perbarui saldo akun
  async updateBalance(accountId, deltaAmount) {
    const table = db.cash_accounts || db.table('cash_accounts')
    // Jika ada mutasi ke acc_digital, otomatis dialihkan ke acc_bank
    const targetId = accountId === 'acc_digital' ? 'acc_bank' : accountId
    const acc = await table.get(targetId)
    if (acc) {
      const newBalance = (acc.balance || 0) + deltaAmount
      await table.update(targetId, { balance: newBalance, updatedAt: dayjs().toISOString(), synced: 0 })
    }
  },

  // Set saldo awal manual
  async setInitialBalance(accountId, targetBalance, note = 'Set Saldo Awal') {
    const targetId = accountId === 'acc_digital' ? 'acc_bank' : accountId
    const table = db.cash_accounts || db.table('cash_accounts')
    const acc = await table.get(targetId)
    if (!acc) return

    const currentBalance = acc.balance || 0
    const diff = targetBalance - currentBalance

    if (diff !== 0) {
      await this.addMutation({
        accountId: targetId,
        type: diff > 0 ? 'IN' : 'OUT',
        amount: Math.abs(diff),
        category: 'Penyesuaian Saldo',
        note
      })
    }
  },

  // Ambil daftar mutasi saldo
  async getMutations() {
    const table = db.cash_mutations || db.table('cash_mutations')
    return await table.orderBy('date').reverse().toArray()
  },

  // Tambah mutasi baru
  async addMutation(data) {
    const id = crypto.randomUUID()
    const now = dayjs().toISOString()
    const targetAccId = data.accountId === 'acc_digital' ? 'acc_bank' : data.accountId

    const item = {
      id,
      date: now,
      accountId: targetAccId,
      type: data.type, // 'IN' atau 'OUT'
      amount: Number(data.amount) || 0,
      category: data.category || 'Lainnya',
      note: data.note || '',
      refId: data.refId || null,
      updatedAt: now,
      synced: 0
    }
    
    const table = db.cash_mutations || db.table('cash_mutations')
    await table.add(item)

    const delta = data.type === 'IN' ? item.amount : -item.amount
    await this.updateBalance(targetAccId, delta)

    return id
  },

  // HITUNG ULANG SALDO DARI RIWAYAT TRANSAKSI
  async recalculateFromHistory() {
    const transactions = await db.transactions.toArray()
    const digitalTrx = await db.digital_transactions.toArray()
    const mutationsTable = db.cash_mutations || db.table('cash_mutations')
    const accountsTable = db.cash_accounts || db.table('cash_accounts')
    
    await mutationsTable.clear()
    await this.getAccounts()
    await accountsTable.update('acc_cash', { balance: 0 })
    await accountsTable.update('acc_bank', { balance: 0 })

    // 1. Olah transaksi Kasir (Barang)
    for (const t of transactions) {
      if (t.paymentMethod === 'CASH' && (t.amountPaid > 0 || t.paidAmount > 0 || t.total > 0)) {
        await this.addMutation({
          accountId: 'acc_cash',
          type: 'IN',
          amount: t.paidAmount || t.amountPaid || t.total,
          category: 'Penjualan Toko',
          note: `Transaksi Kasir #${t.id.slice(0, 8)}`,
          refId: t.id
        })
      } else if (t.paymentMethod === 'QRIS') {
        await this.addMutation({
          accountId: 'acc_bank',
          type: 'IN',
          amount: t.total,
          category: 'Penjualan Toko',
          note: `Transaksi QRIS #${t.id.slice(0, 8)}`,
          refId: t.id
        })
      }
    }

        // 2. Olah transaksi Produk Digital
    for (const d of digitalTrx) {
      const isTopup = d.type === 'topup'
      const isCashAdmin = d.adminPaymentMethod === 'cash'

      if (isTopup) {
        const cashIn = d.nominal + (isCashAdmin ? d.adminFee : 0)
        if (cashIn > 0) {
          await this.addMutation({
            accountId: 'acc_cash',
            type: 'IN',
            amount: cashIn,
            category: 'Transaksi Digital',
            note: `Top Up ${d.provider}`,
            refId: d.id
          })
        }

        // Potong bank sesuai skema potong saldo (nominal - admin)
        const bankOut = isCashAdmin ? d.nominal : Math.max(0, d.nominal - d.adminFee)
        await this.addMutation({
          accountId: 'acc_bank',
          type: 'OUT',
          amount: bankOut,
          category: 'Transaksi Digital',
          note: `Modal Top Up ${d.provider}`,
          refId: d.id
        })
      } else {
        const cashOut = d.nominal - (isCashAdmin ? d.adminFee : 0)
        if (cashOut > 0) {
          await this.addMutation({
            accountId: 'acc_cash',
            type: 'OUT',
            amount: cashOut,
            category: 'Transaksi Digital',
            note: `Tarik Tunai ${d.provider}`,
            refId: d.id
          })
        }
        
        const bankIn = isCashAdmin ? d.nominal : d.nominal + d.adminFee
        await this.addMutation({
          accountId: 'acc_bank',
          type: 'IN',
          amount: bankIn,
          category: 'Transaksi Digital',
          note: `Masuk Saldo Tarik Tunai ${d.provider}`,
          refId: d.id
        })
      }
    }
  }
}

===== src/db/repositories/packageRepository.js =====
// src/db/repositories/packageRepository.js
import { db } from '../index.js'

export const packageRepository = {
  async getAll() {
    const list = await db.product_packages.toArray()
    // Hanya tampilkan paket yang belum di-soft-delete
    return list.filter(item => !item.deleted)
  },

  async savePackage(packageData) {
    const id = packageData.id || `pkg_${Date.now()}`
    const newItem = { 
      ...packageData, 
      id, 
      deleted: false,
      updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      synced: 0 
    }
    await db.product_packages.put(newItem)
    return newItem
  },

  async deletePackage(id) {
    const stringId = String(id)
    const now = new Date().toISOString()

    // Soft Delete di lokal agar syncService aman mendorong status hapus ke Firestore
    await db.product_packages.update(stringId, { 
      deleted: true, 
      updatedAt: now, 
      synced: 0 
    })
  }
}

===== src/db/repositories/serviceRepository.js =====
// src/db/repositories/serviceRepository.js
import { db } from '../index.js'
import dayjs from 'dayjs'

export const serviceRepository = {
  // Hanya ambil jasa yang belum di-soft-delete
  getAll: async () => {
    const list = await db.services.toArray()
    return list.filter(s => !s.deleted)
  },

  getById: async (id) => {
    const item = await db.services.get(id)
    return item && !item.deleted ? item : null
  },

  saveService: async (data) => {
    const now = dayjs().toISOString()
    const id = data.id || crypto.randomUUID()
    await db.services.put({
      ...data,
      id,
      deleted: false,
      updatedAt: now,
      synced: 0
    })
    return id
  },

  deleteService: async (id) => {
    const stringId = String(id)
    const now = dayjs().toISOString()

    // Soft Delete di IndexedDB lokal agar sinkron ke cloud via syncService
    await db.services.update(stringId, {
      deleted: true,
      updatedAt: now,
      synced: 0
    })
  }
}

===== src/db/index.js =====

// src/db/index.js - v3 CLOUDINARY SUPPORT
import Dexie from 'dexie'

export const db = new Dexie('SinarPagiDB')

db.version(3).stores({
  products: 'id, code, name, category, price_modal, price_sell, qty, unit, minStock, idealStock, purchasePackName, packQty, packPrice, updatedAt, synced, imageSynced, deleted',
  product_packages: 'id, productId, name, qty_pcs, price_sell, updatedAt, synced, deleted',
  categories: 'id, name, updatedAt, synced, deleted',
  transactions: 'id, date, total, memberId, paymentMethod, amountPaid, paidAmount, remaining, change, status, dueDate, updatedAt, synced',
  transaction_items: 'id, transactionId, productId, serviceId, updatedAt, synced',
  members: 'id, name, phone, address, total_spending, points, debt, updatedAt, synced, deleted',
  debt_payments: 'id, transactionId, memberId, date, amount, paymentMethod, updatedAt, synced',
  shopping_list: 'id, productId, isBought, category, updatedAt, synced',
  cash_accounts: 'id, name, type, balance, updatedAt, synced, deleted',
  cash_mutations: 'id, date, accountId, type, amount, category, note, refId, updatedAt, synced',
  expenses: 'id, date, category, amount, note, paymentMethod, cashPart, qrisPart, updatedAt, synced',
  digital_transactions: 'id, date, type, provider, nominal, adminFee, adminPaymentMethod, totalReceived, profit, updatedAt, synced',
  services: 'id, name, price, updatedAt, synced, deleted',
  units: 'id, name, updatedAt, synced, deleted',
  operators: 'id, name, role, pin, updatedAt, synced, deleted',
  settings: 'id, updatedAt, synced'
}).upgrade(tx => {
  // migrasi dari v2 ke v3: set default imageSynced=1 buat produk lama yang ga ada gambar
  return tx.table('products').toCollection().modify(p => {
    if (p.imageSynced === undefined) p.imageSynced = 1
    if (p.deleted === undefined) p.deleted = false
  })
})

export default db

        <button 
          v-for="f in filterOptions" 
          :key="f.value"
          @click="activeFilter = f.value"
          :class="[
            'flex-1 py-2 rounded-xl text-[11px] font-bold transition',
            activeFilter === f.value ? 'bg-white text-zinc-900 shadow-sm' : 'text-zinc-500'
          ]"
        >
          {{ f.label }}
        </button>
      </div>

      <!-- Clear Completed Button -->
      <button 
        v-if="boughtItemsCount > 0"
        @click="clearCompletedItems"
        title="Hapus yang sudah dibeli"
        class="p-2.5 text-zinc-400 hover:text-red-500 hover:bg-red-50 border border-zinc-200 rounded-2xl transition shrink-0"
      >
        <Trash2 class="w-4 h-4" />
      </button>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div v-if="filteredItems.length === 0" class="py-16 text-center text-zinc-400 text-xs bg-white rounded-3xl border border-zinc-100 p-6">
    <ShoppingBag class="w-10 h-10 text-zinc-300 mx-auto mb-2" />
    <div class="font-bold text-zinc-500 text-sm">Tidak ada barang belanjaan</div>
    <p class="text-[11px] text-zinc-400 mt-1">Klik "+ Barang Tipis" untuk otomatis mengambil produk dari master</p>
  </div>

  <div v-else class="space-y-2">
    <div 
      v-for="item in filteredItems" 
      :key="item.id"
      :class="[
        'p-4 rounded-3xl border transition flex items-center justify-between gap-3 shadow-sm',
        item.isBought 
          ? 'bg-zinc-50/80 border-zinc-200/60 opacity-60' 
          : 'bg-white border border-zinc-100 hover:border-zinc-300'
      ]"
    >
      <!-- Checkbox & Detail Item -->
      <div class="flex items-center gap-3 flex-1 min-w-0">
        <button 
          @click="handleToggleClick(item)"
          :class="[
            'w-7 h-7 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 transition',
            item.isBought ? 'bg-emerald-500 text-white' : 'bg-zinc-100 text-transparent border border-zinc-300'
          ]"
        >
          <Check class="w-4 h-4 stroke-[3]" />
        </button>

        <div class="min-w-0 flex-1">
          <div :class="['font-bold text-sm text-zinc-900 truncate', item.isBought && 'line-through text-zinc-400']">
            {{ item.name }}
          </div>
          <div class="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px] text-zinc-400 mt-0.5">
            <span class="px-1.5 py-0.5 bg-zinc-100 rounded text-zinc-600 font-semibold text-[10px]">{{ item.category || 'Umum' }}</span>
            <span>Qty: <strong class="text-zinc-800">{{ item.qty }} {{ item.unit }}</strong></span>
            <span v-if="item.estimatedPrice > 0">• Est: <strong class="text-amber-600">Rp {{ formatRibuan(item.estimatedPrice) }}</strong></span>
          </div>
          <div v-if="item.notes" class="text-[10px] text-amber-600/90 font-medium italic mt-0.5">
            {{ item.notes }}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <!-- Delete Action -->
      <button 
        @click="deleteItem(item.id)" 
        class="p-2 text-zinc-300 hover:text-red-500 hover:bg-red-50 rounded-xl transition shrink-0"
      >
        <X class="w-4 h-4" />
      </button>
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- Modal Konfirmasi Bayar Kulakan -->
  <div v-if="showPayModal" class="fixed inset-0 z-[90] bg-black/40 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center p-0 sm:p-4">
    <div class="w-full max-w-md bg-white rounded-t-[2rem] sm:rounded-[2rem] p-6 shadow-xl animate-in slide-in-from-bottom duration-200">
      <div class="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
        <div>
          <h2 class="font-black text-base text-zinc-900">Konfirmasi Pembayaran Kulakan</h2>
          <p class="text-xs text-zinc-400">{{ targetItemForPay?.name }} ({{ targetItemForPay?.qty }} {{ targetItemForPay?.unit }})</p>
        </div>
        <button @click="showPayModal = false" class="p-2 bg-zinc-100 rounded-xl"><X class="w-4 h-4" /></button>
      </div>

      <form @submit.prevent="submitToggleBought(targetItemForPay, payForm)" class="space-y-4">
        <div>
          <label class="text-[10px] font-bold text-zinc-500 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1 block">Sumber Dana Pembayaran *</label>
          <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
            <button 
              type="button"
              @click="payForm.accountId = 'acc_cash'" 
              :class="['py-3 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold border transition', payForm.accountId === 'acc_cash' ? 'bg-zinc-900 text-white border-zinc-900' : 'bg-zinc-50 border-zinc-200 text-zinc-600']"
            >
              Kas Laci (Tunai)
            </button>
            <button 
              type="button"
              @click="payForm.accountId = 'acc_bank'" 
              :class="['py-3 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold border transition', payForm.accountId === 'acc_bank' ? 'bg-zinc-900 text-white border-zinc-900' : 'bg-zinc-50 border-zinc-200 text-zinc-600']"
            >
              Bank / QRIS
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div>
          <label class="text-[10px] font-bold text-zinc-500 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1 block">Total Nominal Riil *</label>
          <input 
            :value="formatRp(payForm.actualPrice)" 
            @input="e => payForm.actualPrice = toNumber(e.target.value)" 
            type="text" 
            inputmode="numeric" 
            required
            class="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-zinc-50 border border-zinc-200 rounded-xl text-lg font-black text-zinc-900" 
          />
        </div>

        <div class="flex gap-2 pt-2">
          <button type="button" @click="showPayModal = false" class="flex-1 py-3 bg-zinc-100 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl">Batal</button>
          <button type="submit" class="flex-1 py-3 bg-zinc-900 text-white font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-md">Simpan &amp; Potong Kas</button>
        </div>
      </form>
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- Modal Form Tambah Belanjaan -->
  <div v-if="showAddModal" class="fixed inset-0 z-[90] bg-black/40 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center p-0 sm:p-4">
    <div class="w-full max-w-md bg-white rounded-t-[2rem] sm:rounded-[2rem] p-6 shadow-xl max-h-[85vh] flex flex-col animate-in slide-in-from-bottom duration-200">
      <div class="flex justify-between items-center mb-4 shrink-0">
        <div>
          <h2 class="font-black text-base text-zinc-900">Tambah Belanjaan</h2>
          <p class="text-xs text-zinc-400">Pilih dari master produk atau input manual</p>
        </div>
        <button @click="showAddModal = false" class="p-2 bg-zinc-100 rounded-xl">
          <X class="w-4 h-4" />
        </button>
      </div>

      <!-- Modal Toggle Mode -->
      <div class="flex bg-zinc-100 p-1 rounded-xl mb-4 shrink-0">
        <button 
          @click="isCustomItem = false" 
          :class="['flex-1 py-2 text-xs font-bold rounded-lg transition', !isCustomItem ? 'bg-white text-zinc-900 shadow-sm' : 'text-zinc-500']"
        >
          Dari Master Produk
        </button>
        <button 
          @click="isCustomItem = true" 
          :class="['flex-1 py-2 text-xs font-bold rounded-lg transition', isCustomItem ? 'bg-white text-zinc-900 shadow-sm' : 'text-zinc-500']"
        >
          Input Manual
        </button>
      </div>

      <div class="overflow-auto flex-1 space-y-3 pr-1">
        <!-- MODE 1: PILIH DARI MASTER PRODUK -->
        <div v-if="!isCustomItem" class="space-y-3">
          <div class="relative">
            <Search class="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-4 h-4 text-zinc-400" />
            <input 
              v-model="productSearch" 
              placeholder="Cari master produk..." 
              class="w-full pl-9 pr-3 py-2.5 bg-zinc-50 border rounded-xl text-xs font-bold"

          <div v-if="filteredMasterProducts.length > 0" class="max-h-[160px] overflow-auto border border-zinc-100 rounded-xl divide-y">
            <div 
              v-for="p in filteredMasterProducts" 
              :key="p.id"
              @click="selectProductForAdd(p)"
              :class="['p-2.5 text-xs flex justify-between items-center cursor-pointer hover:bg-zinc-50 transition', form.productId === p.id && 'bg-amber-50 font-bold border-l-4 border-amber-500']"

              <span class="text-[10px] bg-zinc-100 px-2 py-0.5 rounded font-black text-zinc-600">Pilih</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <!-- FORM INPUT UTAMA -->
        <form @submit.prevent="saveItem" class="space-y-3">
          <div>

            <input 
              v-model="form.name" 
              required 
              placeholder="Nama barang / produk" 
              class="w-full px-4 py-2.5 bg-zinc-50 border rounded-xl text-sm font-bold"

          <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
            <div>
              <label class="text-[10px] font-bold text-zinc-500 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1 block">Jumlah Belanja *</label>
              <input 
                v-model.number="form.qty" 
                type="number" 
                min="0" 
                required 
                class="w-full px-4 py-2.5 bg-zinc-50 border rounded-xl text-sm font-bold" 
              />
            </div>
            <div>
              <label class="text-[10px] font-bold text-zinc-500 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1 block">Satuan *</label>

              >

                  {{ u.label }} - Rp {{ formatRibuan(u.priceModal) }}
                </option>
              </select>
              <!-- Input manual jika Custom Item -->
              <input 
                v-else 
                v-model="form.unit" 
                placeholder="pcs / dus / slop" 
                class="w-full px-4 py-2.5 bg-zinc-50 border rounded-xl text-sm font-bold" 
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
            <div>
              <label class="text-[10px] font-bold text-zinc-500 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1 block">Est. Total Modal (Rp)</label>
              <input 
                :value="formatRp(form.estimatedPrice)" 
                @input="e => form.estimatedPrice = toNumber(e.target.value)" 
                type="text" 
                inputmode="numeric" 
                placeholder="Rp 0" 
                class="w-full px-3 py-2.5 bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 rounded-xl text-sm font-black text-zinc-900" 
              />
            </div>
            <div>
              <label class="text-[10px] font-bold text-zinc-500 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1 block">Kategori</label>
              <select v-model="form.category" class="w-full px-3 py-2.5 bg-zinc-50 border rounded-xl text-xs font-bold">
                <option v-for="c in categories" :key="c.id" :value="c.name">{{ c.name }}</option>
                <option value="Umum">Umum</option>
              </select>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div>
            <label class="text-[10px] font-bold text-zinc-500 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1 block">Catatan Tambahan</label>
            <input 
              v-model="form.notes" 
              placeholder="Misal: Beli rasa cokelat &amp; keju" 
              class="w-full px-4 py-2.5 bg-zinc-50 border rounded-xl text-xs font-bold" 
            />
          </div>

          <div class="flex gap-2 pt-2">
            <button type="button" @click="showAddModal = false" class="flex-1 py-3 bg-zinc-100 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl">Batal</button>
            <button type="submit" class="flex-1 py-3 bg-zinc-900 text-white font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-md">Simpan Belanjaan</button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>
</template>

<script setup>
import { onMounted } from 'vue'
import { Plus, Search, Trash2, X, Check, ShoppingBag, Sparkles, Share2 } from 'lucide-vue-next'
import { useShopping } from '../composables/useShopping.js'
import { formatRibuan, formatRp, toNumber } from '../utils/formatters/currency.js'
import { toast } from 'vue-sonner'

const {
  categories,
  searchQuery,
  activeFilter,
  loading,
  showAddModal,
  showPayModal,
  targetItemForPay,
  payForm,
  isCustomItem,
  form,
  productSearch,
  filteredMasterProducts,
  availableUnits,
  totalItems,
  boughtItemsCount,
  estimatedTotalCost,
  filteredItems,
  loadData,
  handleAutoRestock,
  handleToggleClick,
  submitToggleBought,
  openModal,
  selectProductForAdd,
  saveItem,
  deleteItem,
  clearCompletedItems,
  exportShoppingListText
} = useShopping()

const filterOptions = [
  { label: 'Semua', value: 'ALL' },
  { label: 'Belum', value: 'PENDING' },
  { label: 'Selesai', value: 'BOUGHT' }
]

const triggerAutoRestock = async () => {
  const count = await handleAutoRestock()
  if (count > 0) {
    toast.success(`Berhasil menambahkan ${count} produk tipis ke daftar belanja!`)
  } else {
    toast.info('Tidak ada produk baru bersisa stok tipis untuk ditambahkan.')
  }
}

const handleExportCopy = () => {
  const text = exportShoppingListText()
  if (!text) {
    toast.info('Belum ada item yang perlu dibeli.')
    return
  }

  if (navigator.clipboard && window.isSecureContext) {
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(text).then(() => {
      toast.success('Daftar belanja berhasil disalin ke clipboard!')
    }).catch(() => {
      copyFallback(text)
    })
  } else {
    copyFallback(text)
  }
}

const copyFallback = (text) => {
  const textarea = document.createElement('textarea')
  textarea.value = text
  document.body.appendChild(textarea)
  textarea.select()
  document.execCommand('copy')
  document.body.removeChild(textarea)
  toast.success('Daftar belanja berhasil disalin!')
}

onMounted(() => {
  loadData()
})
</script>

<!-- src/views/Finance.vue -->
<template>
<div class="flex-1 overflow-auto p-4 pb-28 space-y-4 font-sans">
  <!-- Header & Actions -->
  <div class="flex justify-between items-center">
    <div>
      <h1 class="font-black text-xl text-zinc-900">Kas & Keuangan</h1>
      <p class="text-xs text-zinc-400">Ringkasan kas, bank/QRIS, dan saldo piutang</p>
    </div>
    <button 
      @click="syncFromHistory" 
      :disabled="loading"
      class="px-3.5 py-2 bg-zinc-100 hover:bg-zinc-200 active:scale-95 text-zinc-800 rounded-xl font-bold text-xs transition flex items-center gap-1.5 disabled:opacity-50"
    >
      <RefreshCw :class="['w-3.5 h-3.5', loading && 'animate-spin']" />
      Hitung Ulang
    </button>
  </div>

  <!-- Total Saldo Kas + Bank -->
  <div class="bg-zinc-900 text-white p-5 rounded-[2rem] shadow-md flex justify-between items-center">
    <div>
      <div class="text-[10px] font-black text-zinc-400 uppercase tracking-widest">Total Saldo Aset (Kas + Bank)</div>
      <div class="text-2xl font-black mt-1">Rp {{ formatRibuan(totalSaldo) }}</div>
    </div>
    <button 
      @click="openModal" 
      class="px-3 py-2 bg-white/10 hover:bg-white/20 text-white rounded-xl font-bold text-xs active:scale-95 transition"
    >
      Set Saldo Awal
    </button>
  </div>

  <!-- Kartu Akun Saldo & Piutang -->
  <div class="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3">
    <div 
      v-for="acc in accounts" 
      :key="acc.id"
      class="bg-white p-4 rounded-2xl border border-zinc-100 shadow-sm flex justify-between items-center"
    >
      <div class="flex items-center gap-3">
        <div :class="[
          'w-10 h-10 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center font-black text-xs uppercase',
          acc.type === 'CASH' ? 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800' : 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-800'
        ]">
          {{ acc.type === 'CASH' ? 'TUNAI' : 'BANK' }}
        </div>
        <div>
          <div class="font-bold text-sm text-zinc-900">{{ acc.name }}</div>
          <div class="text-xs font-black text-zinc-600 mt-0.5">Rp {{ formatRibuan(acc.balance || 0) }}</div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="bg-amber-50/80 p-4 rounded-2xl border border-amber-200/80 shadow-sm flex justify-between items-center">
      <div class="flex items-center gap-3">
        <div class="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-amber-500 text-white flex items-center justify-center font-black text-xs uppercase">
          TEMPO
        </div>
        <div>
          <div class="font-bold text-sm text-amber-950">Total Piutang Member</div>
          <div class="text-xs font-black text-amber-700 mt-0.5">Rp {{ formatRibuan(totalPiutang) }}</div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <router-link to="/members" class="px-3 py-1.5 bg-amber-200/60 hover:bg-amber-200 text-amber-900 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl transition">
        Lihat Member
      </router-link>
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- Riwayat Mutasi -->
  <div class="bg-white p-4 rounded-2xl border border-zinc-100 shadow-sm space-y-3">
    <div class="font-black text-xs text-zinc-400 uppercase tracking-wider">Mutasi Terakhir</div>
    
    <div v-if="mutations.length === 0" class="text-center py-8 text-xs text-zinc-400">
      Belum ada mutasi saldo. Klik "Hitung Ulang" untuk memuat dari riwayat transaksi.
    </div>

    <div v-else class="space-y-2">
      <div 
        v-for="m in mutations.slice(0, 15)" 
        :key="m.id"
        class="flex justify-between items-center py-2.5 border-b border-zinc-50 last:border-0 text-xs"
      >
        <div>
          <div class="font-bold text-zinc-800">{{ m.note || m.category }}</div>
          <div class="text-[10px] text-zinc-400">{{ formatDate(m.date) }}</div>
        </div>
        <div :class="['font-black', m.type === 'IN' ? 'text-emerald-600' : 'text-red-500']">
          {{ m.type === 'IN' ? '+' : '-' }}Rp {{ formatRibuan(m.amount) }}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- Modal Set Saldo Awal -->
  <div v-if="showModal" class="fixed inset-0 z-[90] bg-black/40 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center p-0 sm:p-4">
    <div class="w-full max-w-md bg-white rounded-t-[2rem] sm:rounded-[2rem] p-6 shadow-xl space-y-4 animate-in slide-in-from-bottom duration-200">
      <div class="flex justify-between items-center">
        <h2 class="font-black text-base text-zinc-900">Atur Saldo Awal</h2>
        <button @click="showModal = false" class="p-2 bg-zinc-100 rounded-xl"><X class="w-4 h-4"/></button>
      </div>

      <form @submit.prevent="submitBalance" class="space-y-3">
        <div>
          <label class="text-[10px] font-bold text-zinc-500 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1 block">Pilih Akun</label>
          <select v-model="form.accountId" class="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-zinc-50 border border-zinc-200 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold">
            <option v-for="a in accounts" :key="a.id" :value="a.id">{{ a.name }}</option>
          </select>
        </div>

        <div>
          <label class="text-[10px] font-bold text-zinc-500 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1 block">Nominal Saldo (Rp)</label>
          <input 
            :value="formatRp(form.amount)" 
            @input="e => form.amount = toNumber(e.target.value)" 
            type="text" 
            inputmode="numeric" 
            placeholder="Rp 0" 
            class="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-zinc-50 border border-zinc-200 rounded-xl text-lg font-black text-zinc-900 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-zinc-900"
          />
        </div>

        <div>
          <label class="text-[10px] font-bold text-zinc-500 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1 block">Catatan</label>
          <input v-model="form.note" placeholder="Misal: Saldo Laci Pagi Ini" class="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-zinc-50 border border-zinc-200 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold" />
        </div>

        <div class="flex gap-2 pt-2">
          <button type="button" @click="showModal = false" class="flex-1 py-3 bg-zinc-100 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl">Batal</button>
          <button type="submit" :disabled="loading" class="flex-1 py-3 bg-zinc-900 disabled:bg-zinc-400 text-white font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-md">Simpan Saldo</button>
        </div>
      </form>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>
</template>

<script setup>
import { ref, onMounted, onActivated } from 'vue'
import { RefreshCw, X } from 'lucide-vue-next'
import { useFinance } from '../composables/useFinance.js'
import { formatRibuan, formatRp, toNumber } from '../utils/formatters/currency.js'
import dayjs from 'dayjs'

const { 
  accounts, 
  mutations, 
  totalSaldo, 
  totalPiutang, 
  loading, 
  loadData, 
  syncFromHistory, 
  setBalance 
} = useFinance()

const showModal = ref(false)
const form = ref({
  accountId: '',
  amount: 0,
  note: 'Saldo Awal'
})

const openModal = () => {
  form.value = {
    accountId: accounts.value[0]?.id || '',
    amount: 0,
    note: 'Saldo Awal'
  }
  showModal.value = true
}

const submitBalance = async () => {
  const success = await setBalance(form.value.accountId, form.value.amount, form.value.note)
  if (success) {
    showModal.value = false
  }
}

const formatDate = (d) => dayjs(d).format('DD MMM YYYY • HH:mm')

onMounted(loadData)
onActivated(loadData)
</script>

<template>

    <button @click="resetFilter" class="text-[10px] bg-zinc-100 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full font-bold">Reset</button>
  </div>

  <!-- LIST BARANG -->
  <div v-for="p in filtered" :key="p.id" class="p-4 bg-white rounded-2xl border border-zinc-100 mb-2 flex justify-between items-center shadow-sm">

      <div class="font-bold text-sm text-zinc-900 truncate">{{ p.name }}</div>
      <div class="text-[11px] text-zinc-400 flex items-center gap-1.5 mt-0.5">
        <span>{{ p.code || 'Tanpa Barcode' }}</span>
        <span>•</span>
        <span class="font-bold text-zinc-700">{{ p.category || 'Umum' }}</span>
        <span>•</span>
        <span>Stok: <strong class="text-zinc-900">{{ p.qty }} {{ p.unit || 'pcs' }}</strong></span>
      </div>
      <div class="text-[11px] text-zinc-500 mt-1">
        Modal Rp {{ formatRibuan(p.price_modal) }} → <strong class="text-emerald-700">Jual Rp {{ formatRibuan(p.price_sell) }}</strong>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="flex flex-col items-end gap-2 shrink-0">
      <div class="flex items-center gap-1">
        <span v-if="p.qty===0" class="text-[10px] bg-zinc-900 text-white px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full font-black">HABIS</span>
        <span v-else-if="p.qty <= (p.minStock || 5)" class="text-[10px] bg-red-100 text-red-600 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full font-black">TIPIS</span>
        <span v-else class="text-[10px] bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full font-black">AMAN</span>
        <span v-if="p.isPublished" class="text-[10px] bg-blue-100 text-blue-700 px-2 py-1 rounded-full font-black flex items-center gap-0.5"><Globe class="w-3 h-3"/> WEB</span>
      </div>

      <!-- TOGGLE PUBLISH + EDIT & HAPUS -->
      <div class="flex items-center gap-1">
        <button @click="togglePublish(p)" :title="p.isPublished ? 'Unpublish dari Web' : 'Publish ke sinarpagi.my.id'" :class="['p-2 rounded-xl transition', p.isPublished ? 'text-emerald-600 bg-emerald-50 hover:bg-emerald-100' : 'text-zinc-400 bg-zinc-50 hover:bg-zinc-100']">
          <Globe v-if="p.isPublished" class="w-4 h-4"/>
          <EyeOff v-else class="w-4 h-4"/>
        </button>
        <button @click="openEditModal(p)" title="Edit Barang" class="p-2 text-zinc-600 hover:bg-zinc-100 rounded-xl transition">
          <Pencil class="w-4 h-4"/>
        </button>
        <button @click="handleDelete(p)" title="Hapus Barang" class="p-2 text-red-500 hover:bg-red-50 rounded-xl transition">
          <Trash2 class="w-4 h-4"/>
        </button>

  <!-- MODAL EDIT BARANG -->
  <div v-if="showEditModal" class="fixed inset-0 z-[90] bg-black/40 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center p-0 sm:p-4">
    <div class="w-full max-w-lg bg-white rounded-t-[2rem] sm:rounded-[2rem] p-5 shadow-xl max-h-[90vh] flex flex-col">
      <div class="flex justify-between items-center mb-3 shrink-0">
        <h2 class="font-black text-base text-zinc-900">Edit Data Barang</h2>
        <button @click="showEditModal=false" class="p-2 bg-zinc-100 rounded-xl"><X class="w-4 h-4"/></button>
      </div>

      <div class="overflow-auto flex-1 pr-1">
        <ProductForm :initialData="selectedProduct" @success="handleEditSuccess" />
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- MODAL FILTER -->
  <div v-if="showFilter" class="fixed inset-0 z-[90] bg-black/40 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-end">
    <div class="w-full max-w-xl mx-auto bg-white rounded-t-[2rem] p-5 pb-8">
      <div class="w-10 h-1 bg-zinc-200 rounded-full mx-auto mb-4"></div>
      <div class="flex justify-between items-center mb-5">
        <div class="font-black text-base">Filter Barang</div>
        <button @click="showFilter=false" class="p-2 bg-zinc-100 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold">Tutup</button>
      </div>

      <div class="space-y-5">
        <div>
          <div class="text-[10px] font-black tracking-widest text-zinc-400 mb-2">KATEGORI</div>
          <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
            <button v-for="c in categories" :key="c" @click="filterKategori=c" :class="['px-3 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-black border', filterKategori===c?'bg-zinc-900 text-white border-zinc-900':'bg-white border-zinc-200 text-zinc-600']">{{ c==='all'?'Semua':c }}</button>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div>
          <div class="text-[10px] font-black tracking-widest text-zinc-400 mb-2">STOK</div>
          <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
            <button v-for="s in stokOptions" :key="s.value" @click="filterStok=s.value" :class="['py-3 rounded-xl text-xs font-black border text-left px-4', filterStok===s.value?'bg-zinc-900 text-white border-zinc-900':'bg-white border-zinc-200']">
              {{ s.label }}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div>
          <div class="text-[10px] font-black tracking-widest text-zinc-400 mb-2">URUTKAN</div>

            <option value="name-asc">Nama A-Z</option>
            <option value="name-desc">Nama Z-A</option>
            <option value="stok-asc">Stok Terendah</option>
            <option value="stok-desc">Stok Terbanyak</option>
            <option value="harga-desc">Harga Termahal</option>
            <option value="harga-asc">Harga Termurah</option>
          </select>
        </div>

        <button @click="showFilter=false" class="w-full bg-zinc-900 text-white py-4 rounded-2xl font-black text-sm">Terapkan Filter</button>
      </div>
