<script setup>
import { ref, computed, onMounted, onUnmounted, onActivated, onDeactivated, nextTick } from 'vue'
import { Plus, Search, Filter, Pencil, Trash2, X, ScanLine, Globe, EyeOff } from 'lucide-vue-next'
import { useProductsStore } from '../stores/productStore.js'
import { useProductScanner } from '../composables/useProductScanner.js'
import { formatRibuan } from '../utils/formatters/currency.js'
import ProductForm from '../components/product/ProductForm.vue'
import { toast } from 'vue-sonner'

const store = useProductsStore()
const { isScanning, start, stop } = useProductScanner('reader-products')

const searchInputRef = ref(null)
const q = ref('')
const filterKategori = ref('all')
const filterStok = ref('all')
const sortBy = ref('name-asc')
const showFilter = ref(false)

const showEditModal = ref(false)
const selectedProduct = ref(null)

const focusSearchInput = () => {
  nextTick(() => {
    searchInputRef.value?.focus()
  })
}

const clearSearch = () => {
  q.value = ''
  focusSearchInput()
}

const toggleCamera = async () => {
  try {
    if (isScanning.value) {
      await stop()
    } else {
      await start((decoded) => {
        q.value = decoded.trim()
        toast.success(`Barcode terbaca: ${decoded}`)
      })
    }
  } catch (err) {
    toast.error(err.message || 'Gagal mengakses kamera HP')
  }
}

const categories = computed(() => {
  const cats = new Set(store.list.map(p => p.category).filter(Boolean))
  return ['all', ...Array.from(cats)]
})

const stokOptions = [
  { value: 'all', label: 'Semua' },
  { value: 'tipis', label: 'Tipis (≤ minStok)' },
  { value: 'habis', label: 'Habis (0)' },
  { value: 'aman', label: 'Aman' },
]

const labelStok = computed(() => stokOptions.find(s => s.value === filterStok.value)?.label || 'All')
const isFiltered = computed(() => filterKategori.value !== 'all' || filterStok.value !== 'all' || q.value)

const filtered = computed(() => {
  let list = store.list.filter(p => {
    const matchQ = !q.value || p.name.toLowerCase().includes(q.value.toLowerCase()) || (p.code || '').toLowerCase().includes(q.value.toLowerCase())
    const matchCat = filterKategori.value === 'all' || p.category === filterKategori.value
    let matchStok = true
    if (filterStok.value === 'tipis') matchStok = p.qty <= (p.minStock || 5) && p.qty > 0
    if (filterStok.value === 'habis') matchStok = p.qty === 0
    if (filterStok.value === 'aman') matchStok = p.qty > (p.minStock || 5)
    return matchQ && matchCat && matchStok
  })

  if (sortBy.value === 'name-asc') list = list.sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name))
  if (sortBy.value === 'name-desc') list = list.sort((a, b) => b.name.localeCompare(a.name))
  if (sortBy.value === 'stok-asc') list = list.sort((a, b) => a.qty - b.qty)
  if (sortBy.value === 'stok-desc') list = list.sort((a, b) => b.qty - a.qty)
  if (sortBy.value === 'harga-desc') list = list.sort((a, b) => (b.price_sell || 0) - (a.price_sell || 0))
  if (sortBy.value === 'harga-asc') list = list.sort((a, b) => (a.price_sell || 0) - (b.price_sell || 0))

  return list
})

const resetFilter = () => {
  filterKategori.value = 'all'
  filterStok.value = 'all'
  q.value = ''
  sortBy.value = 'name-asc'
}

const openEditModal = (p) => {
  selectedProduct.value = p
  showEditModal.value = true
}

const handleEditSuccess = () => {
  showEditModal.value = false
  selectedProduct.value = null
}

const handleDelete = async (p) => {
  if (confirm(`Hapus barang "${p.name}"?`)) {
    await store.remove(p.id)
  }
}

const togglePublish = async (p) => {
  const newStatus = !p.isPublished
  await store.update(p.id, { isPublished: newStatus })
  toast.success(newStatus ? `${p.name} publish ke web` : `${p.name} unpublish`)
}

const publishAll = async () => {
  const count = filtered.value.length
  if (!count) return toast.error('Ga ada barang di filter')
  if (!confirm(`Publish ${count} barang ke sinarpagi.my.id? (sesuai filter sekarang)`)) return
  let ok = 0
  for (const prod of filtered.value) {
    if (!prod.isPublished) {
      await store.update(prod.id, { isPublished: true })
      ok++
    }
  }
  toast.success(`${ok} barang publish ke web!`)
  if (ok === 0) toast.info('Semua sudah publish')
}

// --- LOGIKA LISTENER BARCODE SCANNER HID ---
let barcodeBuffer = ''
let lastKeyTime = Date.now()

const handleGlobalKeydown = (e) => {
  if (['Shift', 'Control', 'Alt', 'Meta', 'CapsLock', 'Tab'].includes(e.key)) return

  const activeEl = document.activeElement
  const isTypingInInput = activeEl && ['INPUT', 'TEXTAREA', 'SELECT'].includes(activeEl.tagName)
  const isProductSearchInput = activeEl && activeEl.id === 'product-search-input'
  if (isTypingInInput && !isProductSearchInput) return

  const currentTime = Date.now()
  if (currentTime - lastKeyTime > 50) barcodeBuffer = ''

  if (e.key === 'Enter') {
    if (isProductSearchInput) {
      e.preventDefault()
      barcodeBuffer = ''
      nextTick(() => {
        searchInputRef.value?.select()
      })
    } else if (barcodeBuffer.trim().length > 0) {
      e.preventDefault()
      q.value = barcodeBuffer.trim()
      barcodeBuffer = ''
      nextTick(() => {
        searchInputRef.value?.focus()
        searchInputRef.value?.select()
      })
    }
  } else if (e.key.length === 1) {
    barcodeBuffer += e.key
  }

  lastKeyTime = currentTime
}

const attachListener = () => {
  window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleGlobalKeydown)
  window.addEventListener('keydown', handleGlobalKeydown)
}

const detachListener = () => {
  window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleGlobalKeydown)
}

onMounted(() => {
  store.fetch()
  focusSearchInput()
  attachListener()
})

onUnmounted(() => {
  detachListener()
  stop()
})

onActivated(() => {
  store.fetch()
  focusSearchInput()
  attachListener()
})

onDeactivated(() => {
  detachListener()
  stop()
})
</script>

===== src/views/Kasir.vue =====
<!-- src/views/Kasir.vue -->
<template>
<div class="flex flex-col md:flex-row flex-1 h-full overflow-hidden p-0 md:p-4 gap-4 font-sans">
  <!-- KOLOM KIRI (TABLET): Search & Keranjang Belanja -->
  <div class="flex-1 flex flex-col overflow-hidden min-w-0">
    <!-- Search & Bar Atas -->
    <div class="p-4 md:p-0 md:pb-4 shrink-0">
      <div class="relative flex gap-2">
        <div class="relative flex-1">
          <Search class="absolute left-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-4 h-4 text-zinc-400"/>
          <input 
            id="kasir-search-input"
            ref="searchInputRef"
            v-model="q" 
            @focus="showSearch=true" 
            placeholder="Ketik nama / scan barcode..." 
            class="w-full pl-11 pr-12 py-4 bg-white border border-zinc-200 rounded-2xl text-sm font-bold focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-zinc-900 shadow-sm"
          />
          
          <!-- TOMBOL SCANNER KAMERA KHUSUS HP -->
          <button 
            type="button"
            @click="toggleCamera"
            class="absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 p-2 bg-zinc-900 text-white rounded-xl block md:hidden active:scale-95 transition flex items-center justify-center"
            title="Scan Barcode Kamera"
          >
            <ScanLine class="w-4 h-4" />
          </button>
        </div>

        <!-- Tombol Lihat Transaksi Tertunda (Pending) -->
        <button 
          @click="showPendingModal = true" 
          class="relative px-4 bg-amber-500 text-white rounded-2xl font-bold text-xs flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 shrink-0 active:scale-95 transition"
        >
          <Clock class="w-4 h-4"/>
          <span class="hidden sm:inline">Pending</span>
          <span v-if="store.pendingCarts.length > 0" class="absolute -top-1 -right-1 bg-red-600 text-white text-[10px] w-5 h-5 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center font-black border-2 border-white">
            {{ store.pendingCarts.length }}
          </span>
        </button>
      </div>

      <!-- CONTAINER KAMERA SCANNER -->
      <div v-show="isScanning" class="mt-3 bg-black rounded-2xl overflow-hidden relative border border-zinc-700 shadow-lg block md:hidden">
        <div id="reader-kasir" class="w-full"></div>
        <button 
          @click="stop"
          class="absolute top-3 right-3 z-10 px-3 py-1.5 bg-red-600 text-white rounded-xl text-xs font-black shadow-md active:scale-95 transition"
        >
          Tutup Kamera
        </button>
      </div>

      <!-- Dropdown Rekomendasi Barang -->
      <div v-if="showSearch && q.length > 0" class="relative">
        <div class="absolute z-30 w-full mt-2 bg-white border border-zinc-100 rounded-2xl shadow-xl max-h-[320px] overflow-auto">
          <div v-if="filteredProducts.length === 0" class="p-4 text-center text-xs text-zinc-400">
            Barang tidak ditemukan
          </div>
          <div 
            v-for="p in filteredProducts" 
            :key="p.id" 
            @click="handleSelectProduct(p)" 
            class="p-4 border-b last:border-0 border-zinc-50 flex justify-between active:bg-zinc-50 cursor-pointer"
          >
            <div>
              <div class="font-bold text-sm">{{ p.name }}</div>
              <div class="text-[11px] text-zinc-500">
                <span class="px-1.5 py-0.5 bg-zinc-100 rounded font-semibold text-zinc-700 mr-1">{{ p.category || 'Umum' }}</span>
                Stok {{ p.qty }} • Rp {{ formatRibuan(p.price_sell) }}
              </div>
            </div>
            <div class="text-[10px] bg-zinc-900 text-white px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full h-fit font-black">+</div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <!-- Pilihan Member Aktif -->
      <div v-if="store.selectedMember" class="mt-3 text-[10px] bg-zinc-900 text-white px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-full font-black w-fit flex items-center gap-2">
        Member: {{ store.selectedMember.name }} 
        <button @click="store.selectedMember=null" class="bg-white/20 rounded-full w-4 h-4 flex items-center justify-center">✕</button>
      </div>
    </div>

    <!-- Area Keranjang Belanja -->
    <div class="flex-1 overflow-auto px-4 pb-4 md:p-0">
      <div class="bg-white border border-zinc-100 rounded-[1.5rem] p-4 h-full flex flex-col">
        
        <!-- BANNER PERINGATAN MODE EDIT NOTA -->
        <div v-if="store.editingTransactionId" class="mb-3 bg-amber-500 text-zinc-950 p-3 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-between font-bold text-xs shadow-md shrink-0">
          <div class="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-0">
            <Pencil class="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" />
            <span class="truncate">Mode Edit Nota #{{ store.editingTransactionId.slice(0, 8) }}</span>
          </div>
          <button @click="store.clearCart()" class="px-2.5 py-1 bg-zinc-950 text-white rounded-xl text-[10px] uppercase font-black shrink-0 active:scale-95 transition">
            Batal Edit
          </button>
        </div>

        <div class="flex justify-between items-center mb-3 shrink-0">
          <div class="font-black text-[10px] text-zinc-400 tracking-widest uppercase">
            Keranjang • {{ store.cart.length }} item
          </div>
          <button 
            v-if="store.cart.length > 0 && !store.editingTransactionId" 
            @click="store.holdCurrentCart()" 
            class="text-[10px] bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 font-bold px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl border border-amber-200 flex items-center gap-1 active:scale-95 transition"
          >
            <PauseCircle class="w-3.5 h-3.5"/> Tunda Transaksi
          </button>
        </div>

        <div v-if="store.cart.length === 0" class="flex-1 flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-12">
          <ShoppingBag class="w-8 h-8 text-zinc-300 mb-2"/>
          <div class="text-sm font-bold text-zinc-400">Keranjang Kosong</div>
          <div class="text-[11px] text-zinc-400">Ketik nama barang / scan barcode</div>
        </div>

        <div v-else class="space-y-1 overflow-auto flex-1 pr-1">
          <div v-for="(c, i) in store.cart" :key="c.cartItemId || c.id" class="flex gap-3 py-3 border-b border-zinc-50 last:border-0">
            <div class="flex-1 min-w-0">
              <div class="font-bold text-[13px] leading-tight truncate">{{ c.name }}</div>
              <div class="text-[11px] text-zinc-500">
                Rp {{ formatRibuan(c.price_sell) }} x {{ c.cartQty }}
              </div>
            </div>
            <div class="flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0">
              <button @click="store.dec(i)" class="w-8 h-8 bg-zinc-100 rounded-full font-black active:scale-95">-</button>
              <span class="w-6 text-center text-sm font-black">{{ c.cartQty }}</span>
              <button @click="store.inc(i)" class="w-8 h-8 bg-zinc-900 text-white rounded-full font-black active:scale-95">+</button>
            </div>
            <div class="w-[80px] text-right font-black text-[13px] shrink-0">
              Rp {{ formatRibuan(c.price_sell * c.cartQty) }}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- KOLOM KANAN (TABLET): Area Pembayaran / Checkout -->
  <div 
    v-if="store.cart.length > 0" 
    class="w-full md:w-[380px] shrink-0 bg-zinc-900 rounded-t-[2rem] md:rounded-[2rem] p-5 pb-7 md:pb-5 shadow-[0_-20px_60px_rgba(0,0,0,0.35)] md:shadow-xl flex flex-col justify-between"
  >
    <div>
      <div class="w-10 h-1 bg-white/20 rounded-full mx-auto mb-4 md:hidden"></div>
      
      <div class="hidden md:block text-xs font-black text-zinc-400 uppercase tracking-wider mb-3">
        Metode Pembayaran
      </div>

      <div class="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 mb-4">
        <button 
          v-for="m in ['CASH','QRIS','TEMPO']" 
          :key="m" 
          @click="handleMethod(m)" 
          :class="['py-2.5 rounded-xl text-[11px] font-black border transition', store.paymentMethod===m ? 'bg-white text-zinc-900 border-white' : 'bg-zinc-800 border-zinc-700 text-zinc-400']"
        >
          {{ m }}
        </button>
      </div>

      <div v-if="store.paymentMethod === 'CASH'" class="space-y-3">
        <div class="flex justify-between items-center">
          <span class="text-zinc-400 text-xs">Total</span>
          <span class="font-black text-xl text-white">Rp {{ formatRibuan(total) }}</span>
        </div>
        <div class="relative">
          <span class="absolute left-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 font-black text-zinc-500 text-sm">Rp</span>
          <input 
            :value="bayarDisplay" 
            @input="e => bayarDisplay = e.target.value" 
            type="text" 
            inputmode="numeric" 
            placeholder="0" 
            class="w-full pl-12 pr-4 py-3.5 bg-white text-zinc-900 rounded-2xl font-black text-lg focus:outline-none"
          />
        </div>
        <button @click="store.bayarNominal=total" class="w-full py-2.5 bg-white/10 border border-white/10 text-white rounded-xl text-[11px] font-black active:scale-95 transition">
          UANG PAS Rp {{ formatRibuan(total) }}
        </button>
        <div v-if="store.bayarNominal > 0" class="flex justify-between text-xs pt-2 border-t border-zinc-800">
          <span class="text-zinc-400">Kembalian</span>
          <span :class="['font-black', kurangBayar > 0 ? 'text-red-400' : 'text-green-400']">
            {{ kurangBayar > 0 ? `Kurang Rp ${formatRibuan(kurangBayar)}` : `Rp ${formatRibuan(kembalian)}` }}
          </span>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div v-if="store.paymentMethod === 'QRIS'" class="mb-4">
        <div class="flex justify-between items-center">
          <span class="text-zinc-400 text-xs">Total Bayar</span>
          <span class="font-black text-xl text-white">Rp {{ formatRibuan(total) }}</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div v-if="store.paymentMethod === 'TEMPO'" class="bg-amber-500/10 border border-amber-500/20 rounded-xl p-3 text-[11px] font-black text-amber-400 mb-4">
        TEMPO → {{ store.selectedMember?.name || 'Pilih member' }}
      </div>
    </div>

    <!-- Tombol Simpan/Bayar -->
    <button 
      v-if="store.paymentMethod==='QRIS' || store.paymentMethod==='TEMPO' || (store.paymentMethod==='CASH' && store.bayarNominal>0)" 
      :disabled="(store.paymentMethod==='CASH' && store.bayarNominal < total) || (store.paymentMethod==='TEMPO' && !store.selectedMember)" 
      @click="onCheckout" 
      :class="[
        'w-full py-4 rounded-2xl font-black text-sm mt-4 active:scale-95 transition',
        store.editingTransactionId ? 'bg-amber-500 text-zinc-950 hover:bg-amber-400' : 'bg-white text-zinc-900 disabled:bg-zinc-700 disabled:text-zinc-500'
      ]"
    >
      <template v-if="store.editingTransactionId">
        Simpan Perubahan Nota
      </template>
      <template v-else>
        {{ store.paymentMethod==='QRIS'? 'Bayar dengan QRIS' : `Bayar Rp ${formatRibuan(total)}` }}
      </template>
    </button>
  </div>
</div>

<!-- Modal Pilihan Varian & Penyajian -->
<div v-if="selectedProductForOption" class="fixed inset-0 z-[90] bg-black/40 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center p-0 sm:p-4">
  <div class="w-full max-w-md bg-white rounded-t-[2rem] sm:rounded-[2rem] p-6 shadow-xl animate-in slide-in-from-bottom duration-200 max-h-[80vh] flex flex-col">
    <div class="flex justify-between items-center mb-4 shrink-0">
      <div>
        <h2 class="font-black text-base">{{ selectedProductForOption.name }}</h2>
        <p class="text-xs text-zinc-400">Pilih varian / opsi penyajian</p>
      </div>
      <button @click="selectedProductForOption = null" class="p-2 bg-zinc-100 rounded-xl"><X class="w-4 h-4"/></button>
    </div>

    <div v-if="['rokok', 'permen'].includes((selectedProductForOption.category || '').toLowerCase())" class="space-y-2 overflow-auto flex-1 pr-1">
      <button 
        v-for="pkg in getProductPackages(selectedProductForOption.id)" 
        :key="pkg.id"
        @click="confirmAddToCart(selectedProductForOption, { packageInfo: pkg, price_sell: pkg.price_sell, customName: `${pkg.name} ${selectedProductForOption.name}` })"
        class="w-full p-3.5 bg-zinc-50 border border-zinc-200 rounded-xl font-bold text-sm text-left flex justify-between items-center hover:bg-zinc-100 transition"
      >
        <span>{{ pkg.name }}</span>
        <span class="font-black">Rp {{ formatRibuan(pkg.price_sell) }}</span>
      </button>
    </div>

    <div v-else class="space-y-2 overflow-auto flex-1 pr-1">
      <button 
        @click="confirmAddToCart(selectedProductForOption, { isServed: false, customName: selectedProductForOption.name })"
        class="w-full p-3.5 bg-zinc-50 border border-zinc-200 rounded-xl font-bold text-sm text-left flex justify-between items-center hover:bg-zinc-100 transition"
      >
        <div>
          <div class="font-bold text-zinc-900">Mentah / Kemasan</div>
          <div class="text-[10px] text-zinc-400">Bawa pulang tanpa jasa tambahan</div>
        </div>
        <span class="font-black text-zinc-900">Rp {{ formatRibuan(selectedProductForOption.price_sell) }}</span>
      </button>

      <button 
        v-for="s in filteredServicesByCategory" 
        :key="s.id"
        @click="confirmAddToCart(selectedProductForOption, { isServed: true, serviceFee: s.price, customName: `${selectedProductForOption.name} ${s.name}` })"
        class="w-full p-3.5 bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 rounded-xl font-bold text-sm text-left flex justify-between items-center hover:bg-amber-100 transition"
      >
        <div>
          <div class="font-bold text-amber-900">
            {{ selectedProductForOption.name }} {{ s.name }}
          </div>
          <div class="text-[10px] text-amber-700">+ Biaya {{ s.name }} Rp {{ formatRibuan(s.price) }}</div>
        </div>
        <span class="font-black text-amber-900">Rp {{ formatRibuan(selectedProductForOption.price_sell + s.price) }}</span>
      </button>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>

<!-- Modal Member Picker -->
<div v-if="showMemberPicker" class="fixed inset-0 z-[90] bg-black/40 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center p-0 sm:p-4">
  <div class="w-full max-w-xl mx-auto bg-white rounded-t-[2rem] sm:rounded-[2rem] p-5 max-h-[80vh] flex flex-col shadow-xl">
    <div class="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
      <div class="font-black text-sm">{{ store.paymentMethod==='TEMPO'? 'Wajib Pilih Member' : 'Pilih Member' }}</div>
      <button @click="showMemberPicker=false" class="p-2 bg-zinc-100 rounded-xl"><X class="w-4 h-4"/></button>
    </div>
    <input 
      ref="memberSearchInputRef"
      v-model="memberQ" 
      placeholder="Cari nama / hp..." 
      class="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-zinc-50 border rounded-xl text-sm mb-3 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-zinc-900"
    />
    <div class="flex-1 overflow-auto">
      <div v-for="m in filteredMembers" :key="m.id" @click="selectMember(m)" class="p-3 border-b border-zinc-50 flex justify-between cursor-pointer">
        <div>
          <div class="font-bold text-sm">{{ m.name }}</div>
          <div class="text-[11px] text-zinc-500">Utang Rp {{ formatRibuan(m.debt||0) }}</div>
        </div>
        <span class="text-xs font-black">Pilih</span>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>

<!-- Modal Transaksi Pending -->
<div v-if="showPendingModal" class="fixed inset-0 z-[90] bg-black/40 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center p-0 sm:p-4">
  <div class="w-full max-w-md bg-white rounded-t-[2rem] sm:rounded-[2rem] p-6 shadow-xl max-h-[80vh] flex flex-col">
    <div class="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
      <div>
        <h2 class="font-black text-base">Transaksi Ditunda</h2>
        <p class="text-xs text-zinc-400">Total {{ store.pendingCarts.length }} transaksi tertunda</p>
      </div>
      <button @click="showPendingModal = false" class="p-2 bg-zinc-100 rounded-xl"><X class="w-4 h-4"/></button>
    </div>

    <div v-if="store.pendingCarts.length === 0" class="py-12 text-center text-zinc-400 text-xs">
      Tidak ada transaksi yang ditunda
    </div>

    <div v-else class="flex-1 overflow-auto space-y-3">
      <div v-for="p in store.pendingCarts" :key="p.id" class="p-4 border border-zinc-100 rounded-2xl bg-zinc-50 flex justify-between items-center">
        <div>
          <div class="font-black text-sm text-zinc-900">{{ p.label }}</div>
          <div class="text-[11px] text-zinc-500 mt-0.5">{{ p.createdAt }} • {{ p.cart.length }} item</div>
          <div class="text-xs font-bold text-zinc-900 mt-1">Total: Rp {{ formatRibuan(p.cart.reduce((s,c)=>s+(c.price_sell*c.cartQty),0)) }}</div>
        </div>
        <div class="flex gap-1">
          <button @click="restorePending(p.id)" class="px-3 py-2 bg-zinc-900 text-white rounded-xl font-bold text-xs">Lanjutkan</button>
          <button @click="store.removePending(p.id)" class="p-2 text-red-500 hover:bg-red-50 rounded-xl"><Trash2 class="w-4 h-4"/></button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>

<!-- Modal Struk & Cetak Nota -->
<ReceiptModal 
  v-if="completedTransaction" 
  :transaction="completedTransaction" 
  @close="completedTransaction = null" 
/>

<div v-if="showSearch" @click="showSearch=false" class="fixed inset-0 z-20"></div>
</template>

<script setup>
import { ref, onMounted, onUnmounted, onActivated, nextTick, watch } from 'vue'
import { Search, X, ShoppingBag, Clock, PauseCircle, Trash2, ScanLine, Pencil } from 'lucide-vue-next'
import { useKasir } from '../composables/useKasir.js'
import { useProductScanner } from '../composables/useProductScanner.js'
import { formatRibuan } from '../utils/formatters/currency.js'
import { toast } from 'vue-sonner'
import ReceiptModal from '../components/ReceiptModal.vue'

const { 
  store, operatorStore, q, memberQ, filteredProducts, filteredMembers, 
  total, kembalian, kurangBayar, bayarDisplay, showSearch, 
  selectedProductForOption, filteredServicesByCategory,
  load, handleSelectProduct, confirmAddToCart, getProductPackages, checkout, processBarcodeScan
} = useKasir()

// Menggunakan composable scanner dengan container ID "reader-kasir"
const { isScanning, start, stop } = useProductScanner('reader-kasir')

const searchInputRef = ref(null)
const memberSearchInputRef = ref(null)
const showMemberPicker = ref(false)
const showPendingModal = ref(false)
const completedTransaction = ref(null)

const focusSearchInput = () => {
  nextTick(() => {
    searchInputRef.value?.focus()
  })
}

const toggleCamera = async () => {
  try {
    if (isScanning.value) {
      await stop()
    } else {
      await start((decoded) => {
        processBarcodeScan(decoded)
      })
    }
  } catch (err) {
    toast.error(err.message || 'Gagal mengakses kamera HP')
  }
}

const handleMethod = (m) => { 
  store.paymentMethod = m
  if (m === 'QRIS') store.bayarNominal = total.value
  if (m === 'TEMPO' && !store.selectedMember) showMemberPicker.value = true 
}

const selectMember = (m) => { 
  store.selectedMember = m
  showMemberPicker.value = false 
}

const restorePending = (pendingId) => {
  store.resumeCart(pendingId)
  showPendingModal.value = false
  toast.info('Transaksi ditunda berhasil dimuat kembali')
}

const onCheckout = async () => { 
  try { 
    const isEditing = !!store.editingTransactionId
    
    const currentCartSnapshot = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(store.cart))
    const currentMemberSnapshot = store.selectedMember
    const currentMethodSnapshot = store.paymentMethod
    const currentPaidSnapshot = store.bayarNominal

    const currentOperatorName = operatorStore.operatorName || (
      typeof operatorStore.activeOperator === 'object' && operatorStore.activeOperator !== null 
        ? (operatorStore.activeOperator.name || 'Admin')
        : (operatorStore.activeOperator || 'Admin')
    )

    const res = await checkout()
    
    completedTransaction.value = {
      id: res.id,
      date: new Date().toISOString(),
      total: res.total,
      paymentMethod: currentMethodSnapshot,
      amountPaid: currentMethodSnapshot === 'TEMPO' ? 0 : (currentMethodSnapshot === 'QRIS' ? res.total : currentPaidSnapshot),
      memberName: currentMemberSnapshot?.name || 'Umum',
      operatorName: currentOperatorName,
      items: currentCartSnapshot.map(item => ({
        id: item.cartItemId || item.id,
        productName: item.name,
        price: item.price_sell,
        qty: item.cartQty,
        subtotal: item.price_sell * item.cartQty
      }))
    }

    if (isEditing) {
      toast.success('Nota berhasil diperbarui!')
    } else {
      toast.success('Transaksi berhasil diproses!')
    }

    if (res.stockWarnings && res.stockWarnings.length > 0) {
      setTimeout(() => {
        toast.warning(`Perhatian! Stok produk berikut menjadi minus:\n${res.stockWarnings.join(', ')}`)
      }, 400)
    }

  } catch(e) { 
    toast.error(e.message) 
  } 
}

watch(total, t => { if (store.paymentMethod === 'QRIS') store.bayarNominal = t })

watch(showMemberPicker, (isOpen) => {
  if (isOpen) {
    nextTick(() => {
      memberSearchInputRef.value?.focus()
    })
  }
})

const openMemberHandler = () => showMemberPicker.value = true

onMounted(() => { 
  load()
  focusSearchInput()
  window.addEventListener('open-member-picker', openMemberHandler) 
})

onActivated(() => {
  load()
  focusSearchInput()
})

onUnmounted(() => {
  window.removeEventListener('open-member-picker', openMemberHandler)
})
</script>

===== src/views/Members.vue =====
<template>
  <div class="flex-1 overflow-auto p-4 pb-24">
    <!-- Header -->
    <div class="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
      <div>
        <h1 class="font-black text-xl">Daftar Member</h1>
        <p class="text-xs text-zinc-400">Total {{ members.length }} pelanggan terdaftar</p>
      </div>
      <button 
        @click="openModal()" 
        class="bg-zinc-900 text-white px-4 py-2.5 rounded-2xl font-bold text-xs flex items-center gap-2 active:scale-95 transition"
      >
        <UserPlus class="w-4 h-4" /> Tambah
      </button>
    </div>

    <!-- Search Input -->
    <div class="relative mb-4">
      <Search class="absolute left-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-4 h-4 text-zinc-400" />
      <input 
        v-model="searchQuery" 
        placeholder="Cari nama / nomor HP..." 
        class="w-full pl-11 pr-4 py-3 bg-white border border-zinc-200 rounded-2xl text-sm font-bold focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-zinc-900 shadow-sm"
      />
    </div>

    <!-- Empty State -->
    <div v-if="filteredMembers.length === 0" class="py-12 text-center text-zinc-400 text-xs">
      Belum ada member ditemukan
    </div>

    <!-- Member Cards -->
    <div v-else class="space-y-3">
      <div 
        v-for="m in filteredMembers" 
        :key="m.id" 
        class="p-4 bg-white rounded-2xl border border-zinc-100 shadow-sm flex flex-col gap-3"
      >
        <div class="flex items-start justify-between">
          <div class="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div class="w-10 h-10 bg-zinc-900 text-white rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center font-black text-sm shrink-0">
              {{ m.name.charAt(0).toUpperCase() }}
            </div>
            <div>
              <div class="font-bold text-sm text-zinc-900">{{ m.name }}</div>
              <div class="text-xs text-zinc-400">{{ m.phone || 'Tanpa No HP' }}</div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div class="flex items-center gap-1">
            <button @click="openModal(m)" class="p-2 text-zinc-600 hover:bg-zinc-100 rounded-xl transition">
              <Pencil class="w-4 h-4" />
            </button>
            <button @click="deleteMember(m)" class="p-2 text-red-500 hover:bg-red-50 rounded-xl transition">
              <Trash2 class="w-4 h-4" />
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        <!-- Detail Poin, Total Belanja & Utang -->
        <div class="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 pt-2 border-t border-zinc-100 text-center">
          <div class="bg-amber-50 p-2 rounded-xl border border-amber-100">
            <div class="text-[9px] font-black text-amber-600 uppercase">Poin Member</div>
            <div class="text-xs font-black text-amber-700 mt-0.5">{{ formatRibuan(m.points || 0) }} pts</div>
          </div>

          <div class="bg-zinc-50 p-2 rounded-xl border border-zinc-100">
            <div class="text-[9px] font-black text-zinc-400 uppercase">Total Belanja</div>
            <div class="text-xs font-black text-zinc-900 mt-0.5">Rp {{ formatRibuan(m.total_spending || 0) }}</div>
          </div>

          <div :class="['p-2 rounded-xl border', (m.debt || 0) > 0 ? 'bg-red-50 border-red-100' : 'bg-emerald-50 border-emerald-100']">
            <div :class="['text-[9px] font-black uppercase', (m.debt || 0) > 0 ? 'text-red-500' : 'text-emerald-600']">Utang / Piutang</div>
            <div :class="['text-xs font-black mt-0.5', (m.debt || 0) > 0 ? 'text-red-600' : 'text-emerald-700']">
              Rp {{ formatRibuan(m.debt || 0) }}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <!-- Tombol Bayar Utang -->
        <button 
          v-if="(m.debt || 0) > 0" 
          @click="openPayModal(m)"
          class="w-full py-2.5 bg-red-600 text-white rounded-xl font-bold text-xs flex items-center justify-center gap-2 active:scale-95 transition mt-1"
        >
          <CreditCard class="w-4 h-4" /> Bayar / Cicil Utang
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>

    <!-- Modal Form (Tambah / Edit Member) -->
    <div v-if="showModal" class="fixed inset-0 z-[90] bg-black/40 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center p-0 sm:p-4">
      <div class="w-full max-w-md bg-white rounded-t-[2rem] sm:rounded-[2rem] p-6 shadow-xl animate-in slide-in-from-bottom duration-200">
        <div class="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
          <h2 class="font-black text-base">{{ editingId ? 'Edit Member' : 'Tambah Member Baru' }}</h2>
          <button @click="showModal = false" class="p-2 bg-zinc-100 rounded-xl"><X class="w-4 h-4" /></button>
        </div>

        <form @submit.prevent="saveMember" class="space-y-3">
          <div>
            <label class="text-[10px] font-bold text-zinc-500 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1 block">Nama Lengkap *</label>
            <input 
              v-model="form.name" 
              required 
              placeholder="CONTOH: BUDI SANTOSO" 
              class="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-zinc-50 border rounded-xl text-sm font-bold uppercase" 
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label class="text-[10px] font-bold text-zinc-500 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1 block">No. Telepon / WA</label>
            <input v-model="form.phone" type="tel" placeholder="081234567890" class="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-zinc-50 border rounded-xl text-sm font-bold" />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label class="text-[10px] font-bold text-zinc-500 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1 block">Alamat</label>
            <textarea 
              v-model="form.address" 
              rows="2" 
              placeholder="Alamat rumah / toko" 
              class="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-zinc-50 border rounded-xl text-sm font-bold uppercase"
            ></textarea>
          </div>

          <div class="flex gap-2 pt-2">
            <button type="button" @click="showModal = false" class="flex-1 py-3 bg-zinc-100 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl">Batal</button>
            <button type="submit" class="flex-1 py-3 bg-zinc-900 text-white font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-md">{{ editingId ? 'Simpan' : 'Tambah' }}</button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>

    <!-- Modal Bayar Utang -->
    <div v-if="showPayModal" class="fixed inset-0 z-[90] bg-black/40 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center p-0 sm:p-4">
      <div class="w-full max-w-md bg-white rounded-t-[2rem] sm:rounded-[2rem] p-6 shadow-xl animate-in slide-in-from-bottom duration-200">
        <div class="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
          <div>
            <h2 class="font-black text-base">Pembayaran Utang</h2>
            <p class="text-xs text-zinc-400">{{ selectedMemberForPay?.name }}</p>
          </div>
          <button @click="showPayModal = false" class="p-2 bg-zinc-100 rounded-xl"><X class="w-4 h-4" /></button>
        </div>

        <div class="bg-red-50 border border-red-100 p-3 rounded-xl mb-4">
          <div class="text-[10px] text-red-500 font-black uppercase">Sisa Utang Saat Ini</div>
          <div class="text-lg font-black text-red-600 mt-0.5">Rp {{ formatRibuan(selectedMemberForPay?.debt || 0) }}</div>
        </div>

        <form @submit.prevent="submitPayDebt" class="space-y-3">
          <!-- Pilihan Metode Pembayaran (CASH / QRIS) -->
          <div>
            <label class="text-[10px] font-bold text-zinc-500 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1 block">Metode Pembayaran</label>
            <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
              <button 
                type="button"
                @click="paymentMethod = 'CASH'" 
                :class="['py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold border transition', paymentMethod === 'CASH' ? 'bg-zinc-900 text-white border-zinc-900' : 'bg-zinc-50 border-zinc-200 text-zinc-600']"
              >
                Tunai (CASH)
              </button>
              <button 
                type="button"
                @click="paymentMethod = 'QRIS'" 
                :class="['py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold border transition', paymentMethod === 'QRIS' ? 'bg-zinc-900 text-white border-zinc-900' : 'bg-zinc-50 border-zinc-200 text-zinc-600']"
              >
                QRIS / Bank
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div>
            <label class="text-[10px] font-bold text-zinc-500 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1 block">Nominal Bayar *</label>
            <input 
              :value="formatRp(payAmount)" 
              @input="e => payAmount = toNumber(e.target.value)"
              type="text" 
              inputmode="numeric" 
              placeholder="Rp 0" 
              required 
              class="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-zinc-50 border rounded-xl text-lg font-black text-zinc-900"
            />
          </div>

          <button 
            type="button" 
            @click="payAmount = selectedMemberForPay?.debt || 0" 
            class="w-full py-2 bg-zinc-100 border text-zinc-700 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold"
          >
            Pelunasan Total (Rp {{ formatRibuan(selectedMemberForPay?.debt || 0) }})
          </button>

          <div class="flex gap-2 pt-2">
            <button type="button" @click="showPayModal = false" class="flex-1 py-3 bg-zinc-100 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl">Batal</button>
            <button type="submit" :disabled="payAmount <= 0" class="flex-1 py-3 bg-zinc-900 disabled:bg-zinc-300 text-white font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-md">
              Proses Bayar ({{ paymentMethod }})
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
</template>

<script setup>
import { onMounted } from 'vue'
import { UserPlus, Search, Pencil, Trash2, X, CreditCard } from 'lucide-vue-next'
import { formatRibuan, formatRp, toNumber } from '../utils/formatters/currency.js'
import { useMembers } from '../composables/useMembers.js'

const {
  members,
  searchQuery,
  filteredMembers,
  showModal,
  editingId,
  form,
  showPayModal,
  selectedMemberForPay,
  payAmount,
  paymentMethod,
  loadMembers,
  openModal,
  saveMember,
  deleteMember,
  openPayModal,
  submitPayDebt
} = useMembers()

onMounted(() => {
  loadMembers()
})
</script>

<template>
  <div class="flex-1 overflow-auto p-4 pb-28 space-y-5 font-sans">
    
    <!-- HEADER & AKSI -->

      <div>

        <p class="text-xs text-zinc-400 font-medium mt-0.5">
          Menampilkan <span class="font-bold text-zinc-700">{{ filteredTransactions.length }}</span> transaksi
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>

    <!-- METRICS RINGKASAN BUKU KAS (3 KOLOM MODERN) -->
    <div class="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 gap-2.5">
      <!-- Total Arus Kas -->
      <div class="bg-gradient-to-br from-zinc-900 to-zinc-800 text-white p-3.5 rounded-2xl shadow-md flex flex-col justify-between">
        <div class="flex justify-between items-center text-zinc-400">
          <span class="text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-wider">Total Omzet</span>
          <Wallet class="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400" />
        </div>
        <div class="text-base md:text-lg font-black mt-2 tracking-tight">
          Rp {{ formatRibuan(totalOmzet) }}
        </div>
      </div>

      <!-- Sisa Piutang TEMPO -->
      <div class="bg-amber-500/10 border border-amber-500/20 p-3.5 rounded-2xl shadow-xs flex flex-col justify-between">
        <div class="flex justify-between items-center text-amber-700">
          <span class="text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-wider">Piutang TEMPO</span>
          <Clock class="w-4 h-4 text-amber-600" />
        </div>
        <div class="text-base md:text-lg font-black text-amber-700 mt-2 tracking-tight">
          Rp {{ formatRibuan(totalPiutang) }}
        </div>
      </div>

      <!-- Transaksi Sukses / Transaksi Void -->
      <div class="col-span-2 md:col-span-1 bg-white border border-zinc-200/80 p-3.5 rounded-2xl shadow-xs flex flex-col justify-between">
        <div class="flex justify-between items-center text-zinc-400">

          <CheckCircle2 class="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-500" />
        </div>
        <div class="flex items-center justify-between mt-2">

            <span class="text-emerald-600 font-black text-sm">{{ countSuccessTrx }}</span> Berhasil
          </div>
          <div class="text-xs font-bold text-zinc-400">
            <span class="text-red-500 font-black text-sm">{{ countVoidTrx }}</span> Batal
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <!-- FILTER & PENCARIAN -->
    <div class="space-y-2.5">
      <!-- Search Bar -->
      <div class="relative">
        <Search class="absolute left-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-4 h-4 text-zinc-400" />
        <input 
          v-model="searchQuery" 
          placeholder="Cari ID nota, nama member, atau produk..." 
          class="w-full pl-11 pr-4 py-3 bg-white border border-zinc-200 rounded-2xl text-xs font-bold focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-zinc-900 shadow-xs transition"
        />
        <button 
          v-if="searchQuery" 
          @click="searchQuery = ''"
          class="absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 p-1 text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-600 rounded-lg"
        >
          <X class="w-4 h-4" />
        </button>
      </div>

      <!-- Filter Chips -->

          'bg-white p-4 rounded-2xl border transition-all duration-150 cursor-pointer flex flex-col gap-3 group hover:border-zinc-300 active:scale-[0.99]',
          isVoid(t) ? 'border-red-200 bg-red-50/20 opacity-75' : 'border-zinc-200/70 shadow-xs'
        ]"

        <!-- Header Kartu: Member & Badge Metode -->

              <!-- Tag Jenis Transaksi -->
              <span 
                :class="t.trxType === 'DIGITAL' ? 'bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-700 border-indigo-200' : 'bg-zinc-100 text-zinc-600 border-zinc-200'"
                class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md text-[9px] font-black uppercase tracking-wider border shrink-0"
              >
                {{ t.trxType }}
              </span>

              <!-- Tag Status VOID -->
              <span v-if="isVoid(t)" class="bg-red-600 text-white px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md text-[9px] font-black uppercase shrink-0">
                VOID
              </span>
            </div>

            <!-- Tanggal & ID Nota -->
            <div class="text-[10px] text-zinc-400 font-medium mt-1 flex items-center gap-2">
              <span class="flex items-center gap-1"><Calendar class="w-3 h-3 text-zinc-400" /> {{ formatDate(t.date) }}</span>
              <span>•</span>
              <span class="font-mono text-zinc-400">#{{ t.id.slice(0, 8) }}</span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <!-- Badge Metode Pembayaran -->
          <div :class="[
            'px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-wider border shrink-0 flex items-center gap-1.5',
            isVoid(t) ? 'bg-red-100 border-red-200 text-red-600' :
            t.paymentMethod === 'TEMPO' ? 'bg-amber-100 border-amber-200 text-amber-800' :
            t.paymentMethod === 'QRIS' ? 'bg-blue-100 border-blue-200 text-blue-700' : 'bg-emerald-100 border-emerald-200 text-emerald-800'
          ]">
            <component :is="getPaymentIcon(t.paymentMethod)" class="w-3 h-3" />
            <span>{{ isVoid(t) ? 'BATAL' : t.paymentMethod }}</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <!-- Preview Isi Barang Nota -->
        <div class="bg-zinc-50/80 p-2.5 rounded-xl border border-zinc-100 text-xs text-zinc-600 font-medium flex justify-between items-center">
          <div class="truncate pr-2">
            <template v-if="t.trxType === 'DIGITAL'">
              <span class="font-bold text-zinc-800">{{ t.digitalType === 'topup' ? 'Top Up Digital' : 'Tarik Tunai' }}</span> 
              • Admin: Rp {{ formatRibuan(t.adminFee) }}
            </template>
            <template v-else>
              <span class="text-zinc-700 font-semibold truncate">{{ getItemPreview(t) }}</span>
            </template>
          </div>
          <span class="text-[10px] font-black text-zinc-400 shrink-0 uppercase">{{ t.items?.length || 0 }} ITEM</span>
        </div>

        <!-- Footer Kartu: Total Harga & Aksi Cepat Cetak -->
        <div class="flex justify-between items-center pt-1 border-t border-zinc-100/80">
          <div class="text-[10px] text-zinc-400 font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">
            {{ t.trxType === 'DIGITAL' ? 'Arus Kas Fisik' : 'Total Bayar' }}
          </div>

          <div class="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div :class="['text-sm font-black tracking-tight', isVoid(t) ? 'line-through text-zinc-400' : t.total < 0 ? 'text-red-500' : 'text-zinc-900']">
              {{ t.total < 0 ? '-' : '' }}Rp {{ formatRibuan(Math.abs(t.total)) }}
            </div>

            <!-- Shortcut Cetak Nota Cepat -->
            <button 
              @click.stop="quickPrint(t)"
              title="Cetak Nota" 
              class="p-2 bg-zinc-100 hover:bg-zinc-900 hover:text-white text-zinc-600 rounded-xl transition active:scale-90"
            >
              <Printer class="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <!-- MODAL DETAIL TRANSAKSI MACOS/MOBILE SHEET STYLE -->
    <div v-if="selectedTransaction" class="fixed inset-0 z-[90] bg-black/50 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center p-0 sm:p-4">
      <div class="w-full max-w-md bg-white rounded-t-[2.5rem] sm:rounded-[2rem] p-6 shadow-2xl max-h-[85vh] flex flex-col animate-in slide-in-from-bottom duration-200">
        
        <!-- Modal Header -->
        <div class="flex justify-between items-start mb-4 border-b border-zinc-100 pb-3">
          <div>
            <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
              <h2 class="font-black text-base text-zinc-900">
                {{ selectedTransaction.trxType === 'DIGITAL' ? 'Detail Transaksi Digital' : 'Detail Rincian Nota' }}
              </h2>
              <span v-if="isVoid(selectedTransaction)" class="text-[9px] bg-red-600 text-white px-2 py-0.5 rounded font-black uppercase tracking-wider">BATAL</span>
            </div>
            <p class="text-[11px] font-mono text-zinc-400 mt-0.5">ID: {{ selectedTransaction.id }}</p>
          </div>
          <button @click="selectedTransaction = null" class="p-2 bg-zinc-100 hover:bg-zinc-200 text-zinc-600 rounded-xl transition">
            <X class="w-4 h-4" />
          </button>
        </div>

        <!-- Info Card Ringkas -->
        <div class="bg-zinc-50 p-3.5 rounded-2xl border border-zinc-100 space-y-2 mb-4 text-xs">
          <div class="flex justify-between items-center">
            <span class="text-zinc-500 font-medium">Waktu Transaksi:</span>
            <span class="font-bold text-zinc-800">{{ formatDate(selectedTransaction.date) }}</span>
          </div>
          <div class="flex justify-between items-center">
            <span class="text-zinc-500 font-medium">Nama Pelanggan:</span>
            <span class="font-bold text-zinc-800">{{ selectedTransaction.memberName || 'Pelanggan Umum' }}</span>
          </div>
          <div class="flex justify-between items-center">
            <span class="text-zinc-500 font-medium">Metode Pembayaran:</span>
            <span class="font-black text-zinc-900 uppercase px-2 py-0.5 bg-white border border-zinc-200 rounded-md text-[10px]">
              {{ selectedTransaction.paymentMethod }}
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <!-- Rincian Item / Produk -->
        <div class="flex-1 overflow-auto space-y-2 mb-4 pr-1">
          <div class="text-[10px] font-black text-zinc-400 uppercase tracking-wider mb-2">Item Produk</div>
          
          <template v-if="selectedTransaction.trxType === 'DIGITAL'">
            <div class="flex justify-between items-center py-2 border-b border-zinc-100">
              <span class="text-xs font-bold text-zinc-800">Nominal {{ selectedTransaction.digitalType === 'topup' ? 'Top Up' : 'Tarik Tunai' }}</span>
              <span class="font-black text-xs text-zinc-900">Rp {{ formatRibuan(selectedTransaction.nominal) }}</span>
            </div>
            <div class="flex justify-between items-center py-2 border-b border-zinc-100">
              <span class="text-xs font-bold text-zinc-800">Biaya Admin (Profit)</span>
              <span class="font-black text-xs text-emerald-600">Rp {{ formatRibuan(selectedTransaction.adminFee) }}</span>
            </div>
          </template>

          <template v-else>
            <div 
              v-for="item in selectedTransaction.items" 
              :key="item.id"
              class="flex justify-between items-center py-2 border-b border-zinc-100 last:border-0"
            >
              <div>
                <div class="font-bold text-xs text-zinc-900">{{ item.productName }}</div>
                <div class="text-[10px] text-zinc-400 font-medium">Rp {{ formatRibuan(item.price) }} x {{ item.qty }}</div>
              </div>
              <div class="font-black text-xs text-zinc-900">
                Rp {{ formatRibuan(item.subtotal || (item.price * item.qty)) }}
              </div>
            </div>
          </template>
        </div>

        <!-- Action Buttons Footer -->
        <div class="border-t border-zinc-100 pt-3 space-y-2.5">
          <div class="flex justify-between items-center mb-1">
            <span class="font-bold text-xs text-zinc-500 uppercase tracking-wider">Total Pembayaran</span>
            <span :class="['font-black text-lg tracking-tight', isVoid(selectedTransaction) ? 'line-through text-zinc-400' : 'text-zinc-900']">
              Rp {{ formatRibuan(Math.abs(selectedTransaction.total)) }}
            </span>
          </div>

          <div class="flex gap-2">
            <!-- Edit di Kasir (Barang Only) -->
            <button 
              v-if="selectedTransaction.trxType === 'BARANG' && !isVoid(selectedTransaction)"
              @click="handleEditInKasir(selectedTransaction)"
              class="flex-1 py-3 bg-amber-500 hover:bg-amber-600 text-zinc-950 rounded-xl font-bold text-xs transition active:scale-95 flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 shadow-sm"
            >
              <Pencil class="w-4 h-4" /> Edit di Kasir
            </button>

            <!-- Cetak Struk -->
            <button 
              @click="showReceiptModal = true"
              class="flex-1 py-3 bg-zinc-900 hover:bg-zinc-800 text-white rounded-xl font-bold text-xs flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 shadow-md transition active:scale-95"
            >
              <Printer class="w-4 h-4" /> Cetak Nota
            </button>
          </div>

          <div class="flex gap-2">
            <button 
              v-if="!isVoid(selectedTransaction)"
              @click="handleVoid(selectedTransaction)" 
              :disabled="loading"
              class="flex-1 py-3 bg-red-50 hover:bg-red-100 border border-red-200 text-red-600 rounded-xl font-bold text-xs transition active:scale-95"
            >
              Batalkan (Void)
            </button>
            <button 
              @click="selectedTransaction = null" 
              class="flex-1 py-3 bg-zinc-100 text-zinc-700 rounded-xl font-bold text-xs hover:bg-zinc-200 transition"
            >
              Tutup
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <!-- Modal Struk / Reprint Nota -->
    <ReceiptModal 
      v-if="showReceiptModal && selectedTransaction" 
      :transaction="selectedTransaction" 
      @close="handleCloseReceipt" 
    />
  </div>
</template>

<script setup>
import { ref, computed, onMounted, onActivated } from 'vue'
import { useRouter } from 'vue-router'
import { 
  Search, Receipt, Calendar, X, Printer, Pencil, Wallet, Clock, CheckCircle2,
  Banknote, QrCode, BookOpen
} from 'lucide-vue-next'
import { formatRibuan } from '../utils/formatters/currency.js'
import { useSalesHistory } from '../composables/useSalesHistory.js'
import { useKasirStore } from '../stores/kasirStore.js'
import { toast } from 'vue-sonner'
import ReceiptModal from '../components/ReceiptModal.vue'

const router = useRouter()
const kasirStore = useKasirStore()

const {
  searchQuery,
  selectedFilter,
  selectedTransaction,
  loading,
  filterOptions,
  filteredTransactions,
  totalOmzet,
  totalPiutang,
  loadHistory,
  handleVoid,
  formatDate
} = useSalesHistory()

const showReceiptModal = ref(false)

// Pengecekan Transaksi Dibatalkan / Void
const isVoid = (t) => t?.status === 'void' || t?.status === 'CANCELLED'

// Hitung Statistik Transaksi Berhasil & Batal
const countSuccessTrx = computed(() => filteredTransactions.value.filter(t => !isVoid(t)).length)
const countVoidTrx = computed(() => filteredTransactions.value.filter(t => isVoid(t)).length)

// Format Ringkasan Item Produk di Kartu
const getItemPreview = (t) => {
  if (!t.items || t.items.length === 0) return 'Tidak ada detail barang'
  const firstItems = t.items.slice(0, 2).map(i => `${i.productName} (${i.qty}x)`).join(', ')
  const remaining = t.items.length - 2
  return remaining > 0 ? `${firstItems} +${remaining} lainnya` : firstItems
}

// Ikon Metode Pembayaran
const getPaymentIcon = (method) => {
  if (method === 'QRIS') return QrCode
  if (method === 'TEMPO') return BookOpen
  return Banknote
}

// Quick Print langsung dari Kartu
const quickPrint = (transaction) => {
  selectedTransaction.value = transaction
  showReceiptModal.value = true
}

// Lempar Transaksi ke Kasir
const handleEditInKasir = (transaction) => {
  kasirStore.loadTransactionForEdit(transaction)
  toast.info(`Memuat nota #${transaction.id.slice(0, 8)} ke kasir...`)
  router.push('/kasir')
}

const handleCloseReceipt = () => {
  showReceiptModal.value = false
  selectedTransaction.value = null
}

onMounted(() => loadHistory())
onActivated(() => loadHistory())
</script>

            {{ e.category }}

          <div class="font-black text-sm text-red-600">-Rp {{ formatRibuan(e.amount) }}</div>

===== src/views/Login.vue =====
<template>
<div class="min-h-screen bg-zinc-900 text-white flex items-center justify-center p-4 font-sans">
  <div class="w-full max-w-sm bg-zinc-800 border border-zinc-700/60 p-6 rounded-[2.5rem] shadow-2xl flex flex-col gap-5">

    <!-- Header -->
    <div class="text-center space-y-1">
      <div class="w-12 h-12 bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-400 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-2 font-black text-xl">
        SP
      </div>
      <h1 class="font-black text-xl tracking-tight">Sinar Pagi POS</h1>
      <p class="text-xs text-zinc-400">Pilih akun operator untuk memulai shift</p>
    </div>

    <!-- Pilihan Operator -->
    <div class="space-y-3">
      <div>
        <label class="text-[10px] font-black text-zinc-400 uppercase tracking-widest mb-1.5 block">Pilih Operator</label>
        <select
          v-model="selectedOperatorId"
          @change="pin = ''; showNumpad = false"
          class="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-700 rounded-2xl text-sm font-bold text-white focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-500"
        >
          <option value="" disabled>-- Pilih Operator --</option>
          <option v-for="op in operatorStore.operators" :key="op.id" :value="op.id">
            {{ op.name }} ({{ op.role }})
          </option>
        </select>
      </div>

      <!-- Input PIN dengan Numpad Custom -->
      <div v-if="activeSelectedOperator?.pin">
        <label class="text-[10px] font-black text-zinc-400 uppercase tracking-widest mb-1.5 block">PIN Otorisasi</label>
        <input
          :value="'•'.repeat(pin.length)"
          readonly
          placeholder="••••"
          @click="showNumpad = true"
          @focus="showNumpad = true"
          class="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-700 rounded-2xl text-center text-lg font-black tracking-widest text-white focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-500 cursor-pointer"
        />
        <Numpad
          v-if="showNumpad"
          v-model="pin"
          :max-length="6"
          @submit="handleLogin"
          @close="showNumpad = false"
        />
      </div>
    </div>

    <!-- Button Submit -->
    <button
      v-if="!showNumpad"
      @click="handleLogin"
      :disabled="!selectedOperatorId || loading"
      class="w-full py-4 bg-white hover:bg-zinc-100 disabled:bg-zinc-700 disabled:text-zinc-500 text-zinc-900 font-black text-xs rounded-2xl transition active:scale-95 shadow-lg mt-2"
    >
      {{ loading ? 'VERIFIKASI...' : 'MASUK SHIFT' }}
    </button>
  </div>
</div>
</template>

<script setup>
import { ref, computed, onMounted } from 'vue'
import { useRouter } from 'vue-router'
import { useOperatorStore } from '../stores/operatorStore.js'
import { toast } from 'vue-sonner'
import Numpad from '../components/Numpad.vue'

const router = useRouter()
const operatorStore = useOperatorStore()

const selectedOperatorId = ref('')
const pin = ref('')
const loading = ref(false)
const showNumpad = ref(false)

const activeSelectedOperator = computed(() => {
  return operatorStore.operators.find(o => o.id === selectedOperatorId.value)
})

const loadOperators = async () => {
  await operatorStore.loadOperators()
  if (operatorStore.operators.length > 0) {
    selectedOperatorId.value = operatorStore.operators[0].id
  }
}

const handleLogin = async () => {
  if (!selectedOperatorId.value) return
  loading.value = true
  try {
    await operatorStore.login(selectedOperatorId.value, pin.value)
    toast.success(`Selamat bertugas, ${operatorStore.operatorName}!`)
    router.push('/kasir')
  } catch (err) {
    toast.error(err.message)
    pin.value = ''
  } finally {
    loading.value = false
  }
}

onMounted(loadOperators)
</script>

===== src/views/Reports.vue =====
<!-- src/views/Reports.vue -->
<template>
<div class="flex-1 overflow-auto p-4 pb-28 space-y-4 font-sans">
  <!-- Header & Actions -->
  <div class="flex justify-between items-center">
    <div>
      <h1 class="font-black text-xl text-zinc-900">Laporan & Rekap</h1>

    </div>

      @click="handleExportToExcel" 
      :disabled="loading || filteredTransactions.length === 0"
      class="px-3.5 py-2.5 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 disabled:bg-zinc-300 text-white rounded-2xl font-bold text-xs transition flex items-center gap-1.5 shadow-sm active:scale-95"
    >
      <FileSpreadsheet class="w-4 h-4" />
      <span>Ekspor Excel</span>
    </button>
  </div>

  <!-- Filter Periode Waktu -->
  <div class="flex bg-zinc-100 p-1 rounded-2xl gap-1">
    <button 
      v-for="p in periodOptions" 
      :key="p.value"
      @click="selectedPeriod = p.value"
      :class="[
        'flex-1 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition',
        selectedPeriod === p.value ? 'bg-white text-zinc-900 shadow-sm' : 'text-zinc-500'
      ]"
    >
      {{ p.label }}
    </button>
  </div>

  <!-- Ringkasan Statistik Utama -->
  <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
    <!-- Omzet -->
    <div class="bg-white border border-zinc-100 p-4 rounded-3xl shadow-sm">
      <div class="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1">
        <div class="w-7 h-7 bg-blue-50 text-blue-600 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center">
          <TrendingUp class="w-4 h-4" />
        </div>
        <span class="text-[10px] font-black text-zinc-400 uppercase">Total Omzet</span>
      </div>

    </div>

    <!-- Keuntungan Bersih -->
    <div class="bg-emerald-500 text-white p-4 rounded-3xl shadow-md">
      <div class="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1">
        <div class="w-7 h-7 bg-white/20 text-white rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center">
          <BadgePercent class="w-4 h-4" />
        </div>
        <span class="text-[10px] font-black text-white/80 uppercase">Profit (Untung)</span>
      </div>
      <div class="text-lg font-black text-white mt-1">Rp {{ formatRibuan(totalProfit) }}</div>
      <div class="text-[10px] text-white/70 mt-0.5">Margin ~{{ totalOmzet > 0 ? Math.round((totalProfit / totalOmzet) * 100) : 0 }}%</div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- Breakdown Sumber Keuntungan -->
  <div class="bg-white p-4 rounded-3xl border border-zinc-100 shadow-sm space-y-3">
    <div class="font-black text-xs text-zinc-400 uppercase tracking-wider">Rincian Sumber Laba</div>
    
    <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 text-xs">
      <div class="p-3 bg-zinc-50 rounded-2xl border border-zinc-100">
        <div class="text-[10px] font-bold text-zinc-400">Toko & Barang</div>
        <div class="font-black text-zinc-900 mt-1">Rp {{ formatRibuan(profitBarang) }}</div>
        <div class="text-[10px] text-zinc-400 mt-0.5">{{ countBarang }} Transaksi</div>
      </div>

      <div class="p-3 bg-blue-50/60 rounded-2xl border border-blue-100">
        <div class="text-[10px] font-bold text-blue-600">Produk Digital</div>
        <div class="font-black text-blue-900 mt-1">Rp {{ formatRibuan(profitDigital) }}</div>
        <div class="text-[10px] text-blue-500 mt-0.5">{{ countDigital }} Transaksi</div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="bg-white p-4 rounded-3xl border border-zinc-100 shadow-sm space-y-3">
    <div class="font-black text-xs text-zinc-400 uppercase tracking-wider">Metode Pembayaran Kasir</div>

    <div class="space-y-2 text-xs">
      <div class="flex justify-between items-center py-1.5 border-b border-zinc-50">
        <span class="font-bold text-zinc-700">Tunai (CASH)</span>
        <span class="font-black text-zinc-900">Rp {{ formatRibuan(omzetCash) }}</span>
      </div>

        <span class="font-black text-zinc-900">Rp {{ formatRibuan(omzetQRIS) }}</span>
      </div>
      <div class="flex justify-between items-center py-1.5">
        <span class="font-bold text-amber-600">Tempo / Piutang</span>
        <span class="font-black text-amber-600">Rp {{ formatRibuan(omzetTempo) }}</span>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>
</template>

<script setup>
import { computed, onMounted, onActivated } from 'vue'
import { Line } from 'vue-chartjs'
import { Chart as ChartJS, CategoryScale, LinearScale, PointElement, LineElement, Tooltip, Filler } from 'chart.js'
import { 
  Store, ShoppingCart, PackagePlus, BarChart3, Wallet, 
  TrendingUp, Receipt, Clock, AlertTriangle, ArrowRight, Flame 
} from 'lucide-vue-next'
import { useDashboard } from '../composables/useDashboard.js'
import { formatRibuan } from '../utils/formatters/currency.js'

ChartJS.register(CategoryScale, LinearScale, PointElement, LineElement, Tooltip, Filler)

const { stats, lowProducts, topProducts, chartRaw, loading, load } = useDashboard()

const today = new Date().toLocaleDateString('id-ID', { weekday: 'long', day: 'numeric', month: 'long' })

// Otomatis ter-update tanpa butuh state ref 'chartReady' manual
const chartData = computed(() => {
  if (!chartRaw.value || !Array.isArray(chartRaw.value) || chartRaw.value.length === 0) {
    return { labels: [], datasets: [] }
  }

  return {
    labels: chartRaw.value.map(c => c.label || ''),
    datasets: [{
      data: chartRaw.value.map(c => Number(c.value) || 0),
      borderColor: '#18181b',
      backgroundColor: 'rgba(24,24,27,0.06)',
      borderWidth: 3,
      pointBackgroundColor: '#18181b',
      pointBorderColor: '#ffffff',
      pointBorderWidth: 2,
      pointRadius: 4,
      fill: true,
      tension: 0.3
    }]
  }
})

const chartOptions = { 
  responsive: true, 
  maintainAspectRatio: false, 
  plugins: { legend: { display: false } }, 
  scales: { 
    y: { display: false }, 
    x: { 
      grid: { display: false },
      ticks: { font: { size: 10, weight: 'bold' }, color: '#a1a1aa' }
    } 
  } 
}

const initDashboard = async () => {
  try {
    await load()
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('Gagal memuat dashboard:', e)
  }
}

onMounted(initDashboard)
onActivated(initDashboard)
</script>

      </button>
      <button

    <!-- TAB 2: HARGA PAKET (ROKOK & PERMEN) -->
    <div v-if="activeTab === 'rokok'" class="space-y-4">
      <!-- Sub-Filter Kategori Paket -->

            selectedPackageCat === 'ALL' ? 'bg-zinc-900 text-white shadow-sm' : 'bg-white text-zinc-600 border border-zinc-200'

            >
              + Opsi Paket
            </button>
          </div>

          <div class="space-y-1.5">
            <div 
              v-for="pkg in getPackagesForProduct(p.id)" 
              :key="pkg.id" 
              class="flex justify-between items-center p-2.5 bg-zinc-50 rounded-xl text-xs"
            >
              <div>
                <span class="font-bold text-zinc-800">{{ pkg.name }}</span>
                <span class="text-zinc-400 ml-2">({{ pkg.qty_pcs }} pcs/batang)</span>
              </div>
              <div class="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <span class="font-black text-zinc-900 mr-1">Rp {{ formatRibuan(pkg.price_sell) }}</span>
                <button @click="openPackageModal(p, pkg)" title="Edit Paket" class="p-1 text-zinc-600 hover:bg-zinc-200 rounded-lg transition">
                  <Pencil class="w-3.5 h-3.5"/>
                </button>
                <button @click="handleDeletePackage(pkg)" title="Hapus Paket" class="p-1 text-red-500 hover:bg-red-100 rounded-lg transition">
                  <Trash2 class="w-3.5 h-3.5"/>
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <!-- Modal Form Jasa -->
    <div v-if="showServiceModal" class="fixed inset-0 z-[90] bg-black/40 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center p-0 sm:p-4">
      <div class="w-full max-w-md bg-white rounded-t-[2rem] sm:rounded-[2rem] p-6 shadow-xl space-y-4">
        <div class="flex justify-between items-center">
          <h2 class="font-black text-base">{{ editingServiceId ? 'Edit Tarif Jasa' : 'Tambah Tarif Jasa' }}</h2>
          <button @click="showServiceModal = false" class="p-2 bg-zinc-100 rounded-xl"><X class="w-4 h-4"/></button>
        </div>

        <form @submit.prevent="handleSaveService" class="space-y-3">
          <div>
            <label class="text-[10px] font-bold text-zinc-500 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1 block">Untuk Kategori Produk *</label>
            <!-- Tombol Pemilih Modal -->
            <button 
              type="button" 
              @click="showCategoryPickerModal = true"
              class="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-zinc-50 border border-zinc-200 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold uppercase text-left flex justify-between items-center focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-zinc-900"
            >
              <span class="truncate">{{ serviceForm.category || 'PILIH KATEGORI' }}</span>
              <ChevronDown class="w-4 h-4 text-zinc-400 shrink-0" />
            </button>
          </div>
          <div>
            <label class="text-[10px] font-bold text-zinc-500 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1 block">Nama Layanan *</label>
            <!-- INPUT DIBUAT AUTOMATIC UPPERCASE -->
            <input 
              v-model="serviceForm.name" 
              required 
              placeholder="CONTOH: SEDUH, ES BATU, DLL" 
              class="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-zinc-50 border rounded-xl text-sm font-bold uppercase"
            />
          </div>
          <div>
            <label class="text-[10px] font-bold text-zinc-500 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1 block">Biaya Jasa (Rp) *</label>
            <input 
              :value="formatRp(serviceForm.price)" 
              @input="e => serviceForm.price = toNumber(e.target.value)" 
              type="text" 
              inputmode="numeric" 
              required 
              placeholder="Rp 0" 
              class="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-zinc-50 border rounded-xl text-lg font-black text-zinc-900"
            />
          </div>
          <div class="flex gap-2 pt-2">
            <button type="button" @click="showServiceModal = false" class="flex-1 py-3 bg-zinc-100 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl">Batal</button>
            <button type="submit" class="flex-1 py-3 bg-zinc-900 text-white font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-md">Simpan</button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>

    <!-- Modal Form Paket Eceran (Rokok / Permen) -->
    <div v-if="showPackageModal" class="fixed inset-0 z-[90] bg-black/40 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center p-0 sm:p-4">
      <div class="w-full max-w-md bg-white rounded-t-[2rem] sm:rounded-[2rem] p-6 shadow-xl space-y-4">
        <div class="flex justify-between items-center">
          <div>
            <h2 class="font-black text-base">{{ editingPackageId ? 'Edit Paket Eceran' : 'Tambah Paket Eceran' }}</h2>
            <p class="text-xs text-zinc-400">{{ selectedRokokProduct?.name }}</p>
          </div>
          <button @click="showPackageModal = false" class="p-2 bg-zinc-100 rounded-xl"><X class="w-4 h-4"/></button>
        </div>

        <form @submit.prevent="handleSavePackage" class="space-y-3">
          <div>
            <label class="text-[10px] font-bold text-zinc-500 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1 block">Nama Varian *</label>
            <input 
              v-model="packageForm.name" 
              required 
              placeholder="CONTOH: 1 BATANG, 3 BIJI, 1 RENTENG" 
              class="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-zinc-50 border rounded-xl text-sm font-bold uppercase"
            />
          </div>
          <div>
            <label class="text-[10px] font-bold text-zinc-500 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1 block">Jumlah Pcs/Batang Dipotong *</label>
            <input v-model.number="packageForm.qty_pcs" type="number" required placeholder="1" class="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-zinc-50 border rounded-xl text-sm font-bold"/>
          </div>
          <div>
            <label class="text-[10px] font-bold text-zinc-500 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1 block">Harga Jual Varian (Rp) *</label>
            <input 
              :value="formatRp(packageForm.price_sell)" 
              @input="e => packageForm.price_sell = toNumber(e.target.value)" 
              type="text" 
              inputmode="numeric" 
              required 
              placeholder="Rp 0" 
              class="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-zinc-50 border rounded-xl text-lg font-black text-zinc-900"
            />
          </div>
          <div class="flex gap-2 pt-2">
            <button type="button" @click="showPackageModal = false" class="flex-1 py-3 bg-zinc-100 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl">Batal</button>
            <button type="submit" class="flex-1 py-3 bg-zinc-900 text-white font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-md">
              {{ editingPackageId ? 'Simpan Perubahan' : 'Simpan Paket' }}
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>

    <!-- Modal Picker Pilih Kategori (Urut A-Z) -->
    <div v-if="showCategoryPickerModal" class="fixed inset-0 z-[100] bg-black/40 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center p-4">
      <div class="w-full max-w-sm bg-white rounded-[2rem] p-5 shadow-xl animate-in zoom-in-95 duration-200 max-h-[80vh] flex flex-col">
        <div class="flex justify-between items-center mb-3">
          <h3 class="font-black text-base text-zinc-900">Pilih Kategori Produk</h3>
          <button type="button" @click="showCategoryPickerModal = false" class="p-2 bg-zinc-100 rounded-xl"><X class="w-4 h-4"/></button>
        </div>

        <div class="space-y-1.5 overflow-auto flex-1 pr-1">
          <div v-if="categoryOptions.length === 0" class="py-6 text-center text-xs text-zinc-400">
            Belum ada kategori
          </div>
          <button 
            v-for="cat in categoryOptions" 
            :key="cat.id" 
            type="button"
            @click="serviceForm.category = cat.name; showCategoryPickerModal = false"
            :class="['w-full p-3 rounded-xl font-bold text-xs text-left uppercase transition', serviceForm.category === cat.name ? 'bg-zinc-900 text-white shadow-md' : 'bg-zinc-50 hover:bg-zinc-100 text-zinc-800']"
          >
            {{ cat.name }}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
</template>

<script setup>
import { ref, computed, onMounted, onActivated } from 'vue'
import { Plus, Pencil, Trash2, X, ChevronDown } from 'lucide-vue-next'
import { useSettingsMenu } from '../composables/useSettingsMenu.js'
import { formatRibuan, formatRp, toNumber } from '../utils/formatters/currency.js'

const activeTab = ref('seduh')
const selectedPackageCat = ref('ALL')

const {
  rokokProducts,
  services,
  categoryOptions,
  loadData,
  getPackagesForProduct,
  savePackage,
  deletePackage,
  saveService,
  deleteService
} = useSettingsMenu()

// URUTKAN TARIF JASA SECARA ALFABETIS (A-Z)
const sortedServices = computed(() => {
  return [...services.value].sort((a, b) => 
    (a.name || '').localeCompare(b.name || '', 'id', { sensitivity: 'base' })
  )
})

// Filter Kategori Paket
const availablePackageCategories = computed(() => {
  const cats = new Set(rokokProducts.value.map(p => (p.category || 'UMUM').toUpperCase()))
  return Array.from(cats)
})

const filteredRokokProducts = computed(() => {
  if (selectedPackageCat.value === 'ALL') return rokokProducts.value
  return rokokProducts.value.filter(
    p => (p.category || 'UMUM').toUpperCase() === selectedPackageCat.value
  )
})

// Modal State Jasa & Picker Kategori
const showServiceModal = ref(false)
const showCategoryPickerModal = ref(false)
const editingServiceId = ref(null)
const serviceForm = ref({ name: '', price: 0, category: 'Mie' })

// Modal State Paket Eceran
const showPackageModal = ref(false)
const editingPackageId = ref(null)
const selectedRokokProduct = ref(null)
const packageForm = ref({ name: '', qty_pcs: 1, price_sell: 0 })

const openServiceModal = async (s = null) => {
  await loadData() // Ambil data kategori terbaru
  if (s) {
    editingServiceId.value = s.id
    serviceForm.value = { 
      name: (s.name || '').toUpperCase(), 
      price: s.price, 
      category: s.category || 'Mie' 
    }
  } else {
    editingServiceId.value = null
    serviceForm.value = { 
      name: 'SEDUH', 
      price: 2000, 
      category: categoryOptions.value[0]?.name || 'Mie' 
    }
  }
  showServiceModal.value = true
}

const handleSaveService = async () => {
  await saveService({
    id: editingServiceId.value,
    name: (serviceForm.value.name || '').toUpperCase().trim(),
    price: serviceForm.value.price,
    category: serviceForm.value.category
  })
  showServiceModal.value = false
}

const handleDeleteService = async (s) => {
  if (confirm(`Hapus tarif "${s.name}"?`)) {
    await deleteService(s.id)
  }
}

const openPackageModal = (p, pkg = null) => {
  selectedRokokProduct.value = p
  if (pkg) {
    editingPackageId.value = pkg.id
    packageForm.value = {
      name: pkg.name,
      qty_pcs: pkg.qty_pcs,
      price_sell: pkg.price_sell
    }
  } else {
    editingPackageId.value = null
    packageForm.value = { name: '', qty_pcs: 1, price_sell: 0 }
  }
  showPackageModal.value = true
}

const handleSavePackage = async () => {
  if (!selectedRokokProduct.value) return
  await savePackage({
    id: editingPackageId.value || undefined,
    productId: selectedRokokProduct.value.id,
    name: (packageForm.value.name || '').toUpperCase().trim(),
    qty_pcs: packageForm.value.qty_pcs,
    price_sell: packageForm.value.price_sell
  })
  showPackageModal.value = false
}

const handleDeletePackage = async (pkg) => {
  if (confirm(`Hapus opsi paket "${pkg.name}"?`)) {
    await deletePackage(pkg.id)
  }
}

onMounted(() => {
  loadData()
})

// Sinkronkan data saat berpindah tab kembali ke halaman ini (KeepAlive)
onActivated(() => {
  loadData()
})
</script>

    </div>

    <!-- Modal Form (Tambah / Edit Operator) -->

      <div class="w-full max-w-md bg-white rounded-t-[2rem] sm:rounded-[2rem] p-6 shadow-xl animate-in slide-in-from-bottom duration-200">
        <div class="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
          <h2 class="font-black text-base">{{ editingId ? 'Edit Operator' : 'Tambah Operator Baru' }}</h2>
          <button @click="showModal = false" class="p-2 bg-zinc-100 rounded-xl"><X class="w-4 h-4" /></button>
        </div>

        <form @submit.prevent="saveOperator" class="space-y-3">
          <div>
            <label class="text-[10px] font-bold text-zinc-500 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1 block">Nama Operator / Kasir *</label>
            <input v-model="form.name" required placeholder="Contoh: Kasir A, Budi" class="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-zinc-50 border rounded-xl text-sm font-bold" />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label class="text-[10px] font-bold text-zinc-500 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1 block">Peran / Jabatan</label>
            <select v-model="form.role" class="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-zinc-50 border rounded-xl text-sm font-bold">
              <option value="Kasir">Kasir</option>
              <option value="Admin">Admin / Owner</option>
              <option value="Shift Pagi">Shift Pagi</option>
              <option value="Shift Malam">Shift Malam</option>
            </select>
          </div>

          <div>
            <label class="text-[10px] font-bold text-zinc-500 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1 block">PIN Otorisasi (Opsional)</label>
            <input v-model="form.pin" type="password" maxlength="6" placeholder="Misal: 1234" class="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-zinc-50 border rounded-xl text-sm font-bold" />
          </div>

          <div class="flex gap-2 pt-2">
            <button type="button" @click="showModal = false" class="flex-1 py-3 bg-zinc-100 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl">Batal</button>
            <button type="submit" class="flex-1 py-3 bg-zinc-900 text-white font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-md">{{ editingId ? 'Simpan' : 'Tambah' }}</button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
</template>

<script setup>

      </div>
